All ten scored campaigns completed: **228 chapter playthroughs, 228 valid independent grades, 76 syntheses, 1126 actions and 1660 fresh provider threads**. Transport retries: 0; rejected protocol replies: 0; look refreshes: 1. One A5 fast initialization failed on a same-second report-directory name collision before any player started; it was restarted in a new directory. One A6 fast grader response failed the required count/ratio arithmetic check; a fresh grader replaced it, and its invalid grade is excluded from the retained assessment. No scored cohort was abandoned or omitted.

All roles used actual **gpt-6.1-terra / medium**, fresh isolated participants and unchanged ordinary-tenth-grade guidance (`2f369fe0c06b828b5ff33cf67527e5ef7f570902727c01771b976ccea2400656`), with runtime `85fa5b68e5cc263b54813f60738100f2d5a4fcc3d19489c4604bff8363b51375`. The first pair shared 12 provider slots; the focused pair shared eight. The final variants used six slots each, with no more than 12 combined; the initialization retry waited for capacity and used five. The merged-goal follow-up used six slots. Recorded elapsed times include queueing, retries and engine work, not human play time. Each campaign passed native frozen-source replay, original provider response/array identity, model/effort/fresh-thread provenance, source/runtime hashes and report arithmetic checks. Reviewers did not receive prior judgments or scores; players/graders received only their appropriate runtime records and fixed guidance.

Every playable chapter received fresh fast and detailed tests; the four subsequent changes and both finale layouts and the subsequent merged-goal correction were retested. Earlier checkpoint history is supplied, not earlier decisions made by that participant. Model role-playing and prose picture proxies do not establish student comprehension, human enjoyment or artwork readability. In particular, chapter 12's graphical unity remains untested. Only requested response arrays, reports and these concise outcomes are retained; owned draft, packet, source-snapshot and replay scratch is removed after verification. No engine, graphics, screenshot, hosting, publication or commit/push work was performed.


<a id="local-assembly-arc"></a>
### Current arc

1. **1–6:** enclosure, cellular products, differentiation, bones/opposing muscles, nerve-cell shape and membranes, central/peripheral anatomy.
2. **7–12:** blood, vessel structure, heart, lungs and breathing, then the local lymph return route.
3. **13–18:** food transformations, absorption, liver storage, colon and microbes, urinary filtering/recovery, water conservation/replacement.
4. **19–24:** skin, fuel-regulating messages, airway defense, immune memory, a local breach and tissue repair.
5. **25–28:** eye, ear, balance and continuing cell replacement.
6. **29:** prepare head, regional anatomy and skin independently on one board, then assemble the body model; **30** is the text ending.

The earlier arcs and source hashes below remain historical. The new ending builds the anatomical whole after the local subjects, while recalling that living cells keep working and replacing material.


<a id="things-revision"></a>
## Physical things, materials and products — 13 November 2026

The user's next playthrough rejected glucose as a permanent cell fitting, ATP + cell → Working cell, the DNA/membrane division exercise, too many weakly distinguished bone operands and the imposed tendon order. Chapter 4's differentiation comparison worked. The feedback also required a whole-story response: the user cannot serve as the only detector of recurring design faults.

The [active brief](../../stories/anatomy/design-brief.md#lessons-that-govern-every-revision) now owns the resulting requirements: handle physical things; distinguish persistent structures from consumed supplies; preserve producers; demand a material contribution; introduce parts through use; choose a useful level of anatomical detail; allow natural assembly orders; retain understandable independent projects; audit actual school-level demands. Earlier broad “physical model” statements had not prevented fuel fittings and activation badges. These new checks are concrete enough to reject those designs before scoring.

### Hypotheses and changes before testing

| Hypothesis | Observable revision and intended evidence |
|---|---|
| T1 — Structures and products make clearer actions than fuel fittings and activity badges. | Feed glucose to the preserved, mitochondria-containing cell to produce an ATP icon. Use ATP with an RNA-bearing ribosome and visibly supplied amino acids to produce protein. No Working cell result; ATP and glucose are not slots. Check whether players explain fuel versus building material. |
| T2 — An unused fitting should be bundled or introduced where it does work. | Remove the opening water channel and pump. The sugar carrier admits the glucose used immediately; the nucleus supplies RNA, the ribosome makes protein, mitochondria support ATP production. Return to the membrane in nerve fibres with an explicitly taught gated-channel example. |
| T3 — Remove the unsuccessful division exercise rather than explain its tokens more. | Supply a real unspecialized cell population for the retained differentiation chapter. Division remains visible when immune descendants and replacement cells actually grow. It is no longer credited as an earlier played molecular lesson. |
| T4 — Merge weak small constructions around stronger independent projects. | Replace old bone/muscle chapters' twelve required operations with four: mineralized bone, jointed skeleton, muscle bundles, complete arm. Bone and muscle can be pursued independently; tendons are continuous included anatomy. Add joint-before-mineral and fibres-before-channel alternatives where the physical pieces suggest them. |
| T5 — Apply the same test to every later chapter, not only reported examples. | Independent current-source audit found time/weather tokens, glands with no gameplay use, producer-to-process transformations and organs reinstalled as demonstration windows. Bundle digestive glands; preserve microbes, pancreatic cells, marrow and skin producers; output actual molecules or cells. Cut standalone temperature and clock interactions, immune/body-memory badges and a seeing-body badge. Rename urine by its physical location. Add vein-first, iris-first and alternative tissue-layer orders. |
| T6 — A depicted before/after difference must justify a fitting. | Fresh review found the repaired skin already spanning its gap before installation. The final pictures keep produced cells/fibres at the edge and the gap open; fitting then shows incorporation. Debris clearance now leaves a visibly debris-cleared wound, rather than a “healing” label. |
| T7 — A more specific nerve lesson spends part of the curriculum allowance. | Count local ion crossing/channel propagation as one of four advanced detours. Remove skin-vessel widening/narrowing from picture and reading, retaining ordinary evaporation/shivering. Lymph return, opposed blood-sugar signals and mechanical balance occupy the other three slots. |

The early two-hotspot arm proposal was considered, but the selected draft bundles regional placement into the bone/muscle convergence rather than multiplying representations of the same skeleton or adding a completion gesture. This is an editorial simplification, not a claim that the engine cannot support any such scene. It removes the original tendon-order restriction. The same biceps/triceps anatomy and opposite pulls remain pictured.

The former clock subject is not declared uninteresting. Its current recipes manipulated conditions and observations; those interactions failed the physical-item rule. A future physical premise would need to earn its own place. Likewise, cutting actions is not a finding that the body lacks material for 200 combinations.

### Independent review and mechanical corrections

A fresh current-source audit examined all 32 preceding chapters without old reviews or scores. A separate fresh author drafted later physical-product replacements under the current constraints. Two subsequent fresh source reviewers examined the opening, selected later changes and their scientific/prerequisite boundaries; neither received playtest outcomes. They found the missing visible cytosol/supporting machinery, tempting blocked enzyme/assembly routes, the wound's duplicated closure, an unintroduced chromosome reference and the nerve mechanism's extra scope. The revisions address those findings. The authoring review did not establish student understanding or enjoyment.

One scratch opening tried two fixed contexts for the cell and RNA–ribosome complex, including a three-input protein operation. It exceeded the existing fixed-item analysis bound. It was not selected or playtested. The replacement uses direct two-item operations with explicit background amino-acid supply, requiring no engine change. Final source review accepted the material roles of ATP, supplied amino acids and the same-cell enlarged ribosome, plus immune descendants and renewal products. The only late repair-picture defect was corrected and checked separately.

### Matched baseline and the limits of scores

The unchanged opening selection was frozen as `7c7e25dc804f52e87adc18741776ab8f9c2b9bb195cc7db66abf126450bcdd61`. A new [detailed baseline](playtests/2026.11.13_21.31.12/report.md) used the unchanged ordinary-tenth-grade guidance and actual Terra Medium players, independent graders and synthesis for old chapters 1, 2, 5, 6 and 7: **15 players, 15 grades, five syntheses and 87 actions**. Full native/provider/report audit passed: 124 fresh provider threads, no transport retries or rejected replies.

```text
Old chapter                  Fun     Worthwhile  Hooks
1 Enclosure                  2.3/5   4/5         2H/1M
2 Cell energy                4/7     6.7/7       2H/1M
5 Bone and joint             4/8     7/8         1H/2M
6 Muscles and tendons        2.3/5   5/5         3M
7 Nerve cells                2/4     4/4         3H
```

This baseline itself exposes an evaluation limit: models valued the working-cell result and credited the awkward bone chapter highly even after the human author rejected their premises. Its bone players did independently reveal a concrete problem: all three tried putting bone cells directly into the mineral framework, which the prescribed blood-vessel prerequisite blocked. These observations inform hypotheses; the high scores do not authorize restoring rejected items. New merged chapter 4 must be compared with both old bone and muscle passages, not presented as a like-for-like rescore of only one. Chapter-level samples cannot establish cumulative human enjoyment.

<a id="things-selected"></a>
### Selection and evidence at that revision

The selected manuscript is **C9**, SHA-256 `2ddbb40cfe92bd5b7a12a60a1d9bec1b0957fd6bc4e938639ca3a83caa78ddfe`: **131 combinations on the native reference completion route, 28 playable chapters plus a text ending, 184 recipes and 279 items**. Alternatives and player attempts can change route/action counts. Native lint reports **zero errors and six reviewed mixed-fate warnings**, with no fixed-analysis-limit warning. The warnings reflect intentional use versus incorporation, implicit fittings that retain their parent, and later physical transformations: ribosomes, breathing apparatus, kidney pair, body with urinary system, layered skin and the debris-cleared wound. Native completion reaches the ending.

This is a correction of the physical interaction model, **not a claim that every chapter now meets the full Fun/hook aspiration**. The roughly 200-combination ambition remains unmet; keep substantive expansion open without restoring rejected fuel fittings, completion badges or routine anatomical padding.

C5 (`e370eaaaf7bfc874d85ec01b5942fbdd3b39450aec5fcc9f05bf7f70dcb41981`) received paired fast/detailed tests of all 20 materially changed chapters. ### What the iterations support—and do not

| Follow-up hypothesis | Observation and selection |
|---|---|
| T8 — Let the receiving-cell blood supply and channel-bearing fibre be independent projects. | C5 forced the blood supply to the end: nerve Fun fell to 1/4 from the matched old chapter's 2/4. C8 restores 2/4, with Worthwhile 4/4 and 2H/1M detailed hooks. This is recovery to the earlier Fun count, not improvement beyond it. Natural fibres-before-channels and blood-before/after-fibres orders remain. |
| T9 — Remove repeated portal-vein fitting from the liver transition. | All three C5 players found that extra fitting weak. C8 keeps actual liver storage and a larger-scale digestive-tract integration: 1/2 Fun, 2/2 Worthwhile and 3H detailed hooks, compared with 1/3, 2/3 and 2H/1M. The extra action disappears; its credited value was not recovered elsewhere. The two-action integration is a bounded payoff after meal breakdown and absorption, not an extended setup chapter. |
| T10 — Explain ATP's contribution in the goal before asking for its use. | All three C5 detailed players inspected to discover the final pairing. C8 removes that hint dependence and preserves 5/6 Worthwhile with 2H/1M hooks, but Fun falls **3.3/6 → 1.7/6**. Clearer guidance does not create independent decisions; do not reinstate obscurity to recover the score. Keep the physically correct ATP/protein model, and retain this interaction weakness for future redesign. |
| T11 — Make the kidney fittings visibly change the route. | C8 improves fast Worthwhile 3/6 → 5/6, but detailed Worthwhile falls 4/7 → 3/6. All three detailed players then reveal another defect: they use the already connected body's kidneys, which the authored collection recipe did not directly accept. C9 adds that physically equivalent route without another status item; final result below. Pictures alone did not fix the whole problem. |
| T12 — Use hormones on existing tissue directly, with the actual substrate visibly supplied. | Replacing the two preparatory hotspot placements lowers fast actions 6 → 4 while preserving 3.3 credited Worthwhile actions. Detailed results move 1.3/4 Fun, 3/4 Worthwhile → 1.7/4, 3.3/4; all hooks remain Medium. This is a modest model result, not strong evidence that the advanced blood-sugar detour earns its scope. |
| T13 — Show supplied immune helper contact and the activated cell's real change. | This addresses a prerequisite/physical-state ambiguity, but fast Worthwhile falls **2/5 → 1.7/5**. Detailed Worthwhile stays 4/5 while Fun falls 1.3/5 → 1/5. Keep the compact population/protein/engulfment consequence as a provisional payoff of earlier differentiation/protein work and the preceding two airway-defense projects. Its cross-mode disagreement and low Fun remain unresolved; it is not a successful Fun improvement or a licence for more signalling machinery. |
| T14 — Name wound cleanup before asking players to close the gap. | C8's corrected pictures establish a real change on incorporation, but all three detailed players first attempt skin closure on the uncleared wound. C9 makes clearance an initial goal and states the relation in its opening while leaving independent growth projects available. Final result below. Never credit a failed, unexplained prerequisite as desirable challenge. |

The merged bone/muscle chapter removes old chapters 5–6's many small operands and tendon-order restriction. C5's 3/4 Fun becomes 2/4 in C8 after a clearer grouping goal, while detailed Worthwhile stays 4/4 with 2H/1M hooks; fast Worthwhile improves 3.3/4 → 4/4 but hooks remain 3M. This is mixed evidence, not a universal improvement. Two substantial constructions remain, and the four required actions are preferable to reinstating the rejected twelve-operation anatomical checklist merely to raise credited counts.

Chapter 1 is deliberately short: one independent choice between a membrane enclosure and a nucleus, followed by incorporation; **1/3 Fun and 3/3 Worthwhile** with 3H detailed hooks. This is not classified as an emotional-payoff exception or called high Fun. The next chapter offers ATP and protein projects. Differentiation remains the accepted parallel comparison, with some second-branch repetition. Colon, skin fittings and clotting retain weak repeated assembly moments; the completed tests do not erase them. The two-action liver transition and compact immune consequence are the explicitly recorded payoff candidates, with the immune reservation above. No long run of low-agency chapters is justified merely by labelling it a payoff.

C9's final two-chapter retest resolves the observed route/prerequisite failures: all six detailed players complete in the normal six kidney or eight repair actions. Kidney detailed Worthwhile improves **3/6 → 5/6**, Fun falls **3/6 → 2.3/6**, and hooks move from 3M to 1H/2M. Fast Worthwhile falls **5/6 → 4.3/6**, with 3M hooks. Wound detailed Worthwhile changes **8.7/9 → 8/8** and Fun **5.3/9 → 4/8**; the former extra action was the rejected premature closure attempt. Fast Worthwhile changes **7.3/8 → 7/8**; hooks stay 3M in both modes. These are mixed scored changes alongside a concrete reduction in confusion, not across-the-board score gains. Routine drainage fittings and make-then-incorporate wound pacing remain the reported weak moments. Keep C9's valid kidney route and explicit clearance goal; there is no reason to restore those avoidable failures.

<a id="things-arc"></a>
### Arc at that revision

1. Enclosure and nucleus; ATP and protein production; differentiation.
2. Bone and muscle in parallel; membrane signalling; brain, cord and peripheral nerves.
3. Blood; vessel routes; heart; lung exchange; ventilation; regional supply and lymph return.
4. Digestion; absorption; liver storage; colon tissues and microbes; kidney filtering/recovery; conserving and replacing water.
5. Skin; pancreatic messages and physical fuel stores; airway defenses; antibody production and memory cells.
6. Clot; replacement tissue and wound closure; eyes; ears; balance; continuing red-cell and skin-cell renewal, then a text ending.

The older narrative-arc section below is historical. Current chapter numbers and exact vocabulary are mapped in [curriculum.md](curriculum.md). No process token or extra fitting should be introduced simply to recover the removed action count.


<a id="enclosed-cell-revision"></a>

## One enclosed cell, concrete messages — 13 November 2026

The user rejected the opening's free-standing “oxygen entering the cell” event item, membrane patches, and separate pump/carrier membrane identities. They requested an enclosed bag with channels and material inside it, suggested a compound, and asked what differentiation signals physically are and where they come from. “Young” versus “developing” muscle cells did not establish a worthwhile difference. This is a focused revision of chapters 1, 2 and 4, with chapter 3's necessary handoff from the newly built cell. Other chapters remain outside this change apart from the chapter-6 display name inherited from chapter 4. No graphics, screenshots or engine changes.

### Prospective hypotheses and alternatives

- **E1 — keep the physical whole.** One compound enclosure retains its water channel, sugar carrier, nucleus, mitochondria, free glucose, ribosome and pump. Oxygen is an actual molecule supplied to that enclosed cell, not an event-shaped output. Predict fewer location/identity contradictions and clearer relationships among transport and metabolism.
- **E2 — preserve useful parallel construction.** Build the DNA-containing nucleus independently of the membrane and its routes. In chapter 2, prepare fuel/mitochondria, protein-building machinery and the pump before their common ATP-powered result. Predict meaningful project choices without relying on permutations of fittings alone.
- **E3 — distinguish molecular contents, not vague ages.** Extracellular protein messengers from neighbouring developing tissues change gene use. Show RNA copies before the amino-acid operation and actual protein structures after it. Name the physical distinction and preserve the same-DNA/different-protein comparison. Predict less ambiguity about what the second operation adds; cut or rethink the stage if it remains padding.
- **E4 — let completed construction matter.** ATP activates the assembled cell, producing a single working-cell item that retains its pictured components. Chapter 3 grows and copies this same cell rather than receiving another preassembled primitive. Allow membrane production before or after copying DNA. Predict a more coherent handoff without adding a ceremonial “completion” combination.

An isolated Terra Medium author received only the differentiation scope, audience, physical-item requirement and downstream interfaces, with no current draft or earlier scores. Its three sketches used neighbourhoods, messengers/materials, and existing tissue settings. None was adopted wholesale: they repeatedly added DNA to cells already containing it, used abstract accessible-DNA ingredients, or exceeded the requested compact recipe count. The useful chemical-messenger direction was developed without those steps. OpenStax Biology 9.1 distinguishes extracellular local messages from physical scaffolds and explains how local signalling can contribute to differentiation; the story supplies the prior cellular history and supporting machinery rather than representing one universal muscle chemical.

A fresh physical reader and a separate fresh audience/gameplay reader received the first four candidate chapters without author notes, old drafts, criticism or scores. They supported the persistent enclosure and identified useful nucleus/boundary projects. Their adopted requests clarify the RNA-to-protein change, membrane sacs joining the growing boundary, preservation of all cell parts during division, chromosome terminology and the distinction between intracellular contractile proteins and later whole muscle fibres. The physical reader's claim that glucose could arrive before the carrier does not follow from this story's goals: chapter 1 requires that fitted carrier before chapter 2 supplies glucose. No new artificial ATP/carrier dependency was added in response.

Candidate 1 prepared an ATP-bound pump outside the cell before fitting it. Although its physical reviewer accepted the distinction between binding and transport, that design would reject a sensible unpowered-pump installation. Candidate 2 instead fits the unpowered pump in the same persistent cell, then uses ATP on the assembled whole. Its final working-cell picture shows both protein production and active transport. This is a substantive untested hypothesis, not evidence that the combined payoff is better. Native analysis gives **172 required combinations, 32 chapters, 210 recipes**, with zero lint errors before playtesting. Candidate 1 was reviewed but not playtested.

A new matched detailed baseline uses the unchanged canonical source `d4c5660422057561a13cb8455a73cbc0578f57add668606c27927d198afb69c3`, the same ordinary-tenth-grader guidance and current runtime. [Baseline report](playtests/2026.11.13_20.13.45/report.md): nine players, nine independent grades and three syntheses; all completed, all provenance/replay/arithmetic checks passed. These are small model cohorts, not students. No guidance or criteria change between this baseline and the new candidates.

```text
Chapter                            Fun     Worthwhile  Hooks
1 Inside and outside               2.3/4   4/4         3M
2 The powerhouse of the cell       2.7/7   5.3/7       2H/1M
4 Same DNA, different jobs          2.3/4   3.3/4       2H/1M
```

Candidate 2 receives fresh paired fast/detailed cohorts in all four chapters, with Terra Medium for every player, independent grader and synthesis. The chapter-3 handoff is included because its physical identity and available production routes change. No old Fun score is transferred to the revised opening.

### First enclosure trials and corrective hypothesis

[Candidate 2 fast](playtests/2026.11.13_20.25.22/report.md) and [detailed](playtests/2026.11.13_20.25.23/report.md) each completed twelve players, twelve independent grades and four syntheses. The enclosed-cell payoff was valued, but chapter 1 fell to **1.7/5 Fun and 3.3/5 Worthwhile**. Every detailed player found the empty bag describing future fitted components. This was an authoring error: all the bracketed paragraphs sat inside one unqualified reading, so native play correctly delivered them together. Lint completion does not certify the intended timing of valid prose. The remedy uses repeated declarations, each with its own leading condition; the existing format specification now clarifies that syntax. No engine change is involved.

Chapter 2 scored **4/7 Fun, 4.7/7 Worthwhile**, with one High/two Medium hooks: more credited choices than the matched baseline, but less Worthwhile. The prepared ribosome's fitting remains routine; all three valued ATP supporting both jobs inside the completed cell. Chapter 4 scored **1.7/4 Fun, 3/4 Worthwhile**, with three High hooks. Clearer anatomy alone did not improve its Fun or Worthwhile. The second messenger branch repeated the first, while the contrasting inside/outside protein results were valued.

**E5, before the next trial:** correct the premature descriptions and the new-membrane picture that showed incorporation before use. Make Chapter 4's intermediate titles actual contents—“Cells with muscle-protein RNA” and “Cells with support-protein RNA”—and show the copying and protein-building stages in each of its two independent goal chains. This removes the unexplained precursor label and gives the first operation a distinct stated purpose; it may still leave repetition, so test rather than declare success. Candidate 3 changes no recipe or required-action count. A native text check confirms nine separate compound readings, with no nucleus/carrier/mitochondria/pump in the bare bag picture and only the water channel newly visible after its fitting. Fresh paired trials cover chapters 1–4; earlier candidate scores do not become current scores.

### Second enclosure trials and final focused test

[Candidate 3 fast](playtests/2026.11.13_20.31.59/report.md) and [detailed](playtests/2026.11.13_20.32.00/report.md) again completed all twelve players per mode. The premature-compartment descriptions disappeared. A fresh physical reviewer, supplied only this current draft, audience and actual compound/goal semantics, found no material physiological or continuity defect; its optional chromosome-drawing refinement is not a claim of new learning evidence.

Chapter 2 now scores **3.7/7 Fun and 5.7/7 Worthwhile**, with all three detailed hooks High, versus the matched baseline's 2.7/7 and 5.3/7. Its different preparation projects were pursued in different orders, and all three valued the common ATP-supported cell. Chapter 4 recovers slightly from the previous candidate to **2/4 Fun and 3/4 Worthwhile**, with three High hooks; it remains below the baseline's 2.3/4 and 3.3/4. The RNA-versus-protein distinction is now legible, but the second branch repeats the first. Keep that regression visible rather than calling clarified titles a measured Fun improvement.

Chapter 1's **1/5 Fun and 3.7/5 Worthwhile**, all Medium detailed hooks, remains weak despite repaired physical continuity. All three detailed players chose nucleus construction first. Its goal names an immediately available construction, while the boundary goal skips ahead to fitted transport routes. **E6, before the final focused trial:** give the boundary an equally immediate “Build the cell’s outer enclosure” goal before its routes, leaving the independent nucleus goal alongside it. No new item, mechanism or recipe is added. Also label the already pictured RNA strand as a copy of a DNA instruction, resolving one fast player's vocabulary confusion. Retest chapter 1 in both modes; the small cohort cannot establish a causal goal-order effect, but actual choices and attributed reasons can test the invitation. Chapters 2–4 retain their unchanged local source and applicable candidate-3 evidence, with the preceding chapter's clearer goal/label history disclosed.

### Selected enclosure revision and comparisons

The canonical [anatomy.md](anatomy.md) is now SHA-256 `7c7e25dc804f52e87adc18741776ab8f9c2b9bb195cc7db66abf126450bcdd61`: **172 required combinations across 32 chapters**, 210 recipes and 330 items (162 primitive). It adopts candidate 4's exact bytes. Chapters **5–32 are byte-for-byte unchanged** from this pass's baseline; chapter 6 displays the new muscle-cell title through its existing shared identity. Chapter 3 now uses the actual cell built in chapter 2, with membrane preparation permitted before or after copying DNA. No graphics, screenshots, engine or publication work was performed.

Selected evidence: chapter 1 comes from [final fast](playtests/2026.11.13_20.39.43/report.md) and [final detailed](playtests/2026.11.13_20.39.45/report.md); chapters 2–4 retain [candidate-3 fast](playtests/2026.11.13_20.31.59/report.md) and [candidate-3 detailed](playtests/2026.11.13_20.32.00/report.md). Their local source is unchanged, while preceding chapter 1's goal invitation and RNA label changed as disclosed above. This is not a single continuous playthrough or a uniform final-source cohort. Counts below are mean credited actions / mean recorded gameplay actions. Fast does not measure Fun. H/M/L count three graders. Chapter 3 was not rerun in this pass's matched baseline, so no new matched before/after claim is made for it.

```text
Chapter                       Baseline Fun  Baseline Worth  Selected Fun  Selected Worth  Hooks  Fast Worth  Hooks
1 An inside of its own        2.3/4         4/4             2.3/5         3.7/5           1H/2M  4.7/5       2H/1M
2 The powerhouse of the cell  2.7/7         5.3/7           3.7/7         5.7/7           3H     6/7         1H/2M
3 One cell becomes two        not rerun     not rerun       1/3           3/3             2H/1M  2.7/3       1H/2M
4 Same DNA, different jobs    2.3/4         3.3/4           2/4           3/4             3H     3/4         3H
```

**E6's result:** the final first-chapter cohort has 2.3/5 Fun, up from candidate 3's 1/5, with one High/two Medium detailed hooks. Both starting constructions now have immediate goals; credited choices include continuing the boundary while the independent nucleus remains available. This recovers the baseline's 2.3 meaningful-action numerator over five rather than four actions; it is not an across-the-board improvement. Worthwhile remains 3.7/5 against the old 4/4. All three still described the channel/carrier sequence as somewhat repetitive, while valuing its different routes. The enclosed physical model corrects the user's objection even though its scores do not uniformly exceed the flawed patch-based model.

Chapter 2 improves on the matched baseline in both detailed counts and hook. Chapter 4's physical stages and messenger source are clearer, and all three hooks remain High, but its Fun/Worthwhile stay slightly below baseline. Do not restore vague ages or abstract signals to recover a score. The inside/outside protein contrast earns its place; the repeated messenger sequence remains a limitation. Chapter 3 retains a thin one-choice/three-action structure and an unexplained membrane-production process; it is not newly designated an emotional payoff. The first chapter is not claimed as a low-Fun payoff exception either.

The final factual reader found no material physiology/continuity defect under the actual format semantics and school-level reconstruction. It did not establish enjoyment or student comprehension. The most important missed issue in earlier source reviews was the premature compound description: only actual native player packets exposed that authoring error. The repaired conditional readings were then inspected directly and playtested. High score claims cannot override the user's judgment about what counts as a physical item.

Final native lint gives **zero errors and 11 reviewed mixed-fate warnings**. The enclosure is retained while fitted or producing ATP, then becomes the working cell; the working/copied cells can make membrane before their subsequent transformation. The other eight warnings retain the established compound-incorporation, immune-production and healing-state roles. Native analysis reaches the full ending. The 172-action draft remains below the approximate 200-action ambition; this correction adds one required action overall, not filler to hit that target.

<a id="ordinary-biology-revision"></a>

## Ordinary grades 9–10 biology — 13 November 2026

The user changed the audience to **grades 9–10, non-AP/non-Honors**, requested revision of the entire game, and required explicit tenth-grader role guidance for playtesters. A handful of advanced excursions may remain only when Fun and hook justify them. The active brief owns the exact unchanged guidance and the current maximum of four explicitly named advanced mechanisms; the initial allocation of three and its evidence-led correction are recorded below. The earlier AP/Honors-guided scores below remain historical evidence, not the baseline for this audience.

### Evidence and hypotheses before testing

- **H1 — understandable projects:** remove specialist operands and unnecessary molecular stages while retaining independent useful constructions. Predict fewer explanations that merely follow a named pairing, without sacrificing the meaningful-decision numerator.
- **H2 — actual changes:** make ingredient pictures distinguish a preparation from the result. Remove stages that already show the final capability, especially the young/developing-cell distinction and the extra fluid-to-tissue step. Predict better Worthwhile without attributing a denominator reduction alone to better Fun.
- **H3 — understandable comparisons:** use already built anatomy in independently available warm/cold and fed/between-meal situations. Predict useful project choice with real physiological contrast, not mere fitting-order permutations.
- **H4 — limited excursions:** provisionally retain lymph return, opposed blood-sugar signals and mechanical balance sensing. Require new-audience Fun/hook evidence for each; do not classify an advanced pathway as ordinary by hiding its names.
- **H5 — immune activation:** after the user explicitly expressed interest, check ordinary curricula before removing the topic. GCSE specifications explicitly include antigen-triggered responses, antibodies, memory lymphocytes and vaccination. Explore recognition, multiplication and distinct antibody/memory outcomes without requiring dendritic/helper-T/B-cell presentation machinery. This is not a claim that every US tenth grader has studied it.

New baseline: unchanged 200-combination manuscript SHA-256 `7f81494f9c14c654b5defe62bd231d1339212f930a31bc3202632186d4476fdc`, all 32 chapters in detailed mode, using the new exact audience guidance with actual Terra Medium players, independent graders and synthesis. Three players per chapter. Completed [new-audience baseline](playtests/2026.11.13_16.33.07/report.md): 96 detailed players, 96 independent grades and 32 chapter syntheses; 613 actions. Full provenance and native-replay audit passed: 838 fresh provider threads, zero transport retries, one rejected protocol reply, no failed records. The unchanged evaluation runtime hash was `b35ed84ec58e14658d1eaf523b901a94c44d4737321d4b7c89c0676f8981b11a`; guidance hash `2f369fe0c06b828b5ff33cf67527e5ef7f570902727c01771b976ccea2400656`. Whole-story fast and detailed candidate tests follow review and native lint; retain both denominators and any regression.

**First school candidate, fast:** [all 32 chapters](playtests/2026.11.13_16.54.43/report.md), source `7dd7b4ca9563107be612b06966bbb205a4139912c137d148d683679f44fc3ebb`, completed 96 players, 96 grades, 32 syntheses and 522 actions. Full audit passed: 746 fresh provider threads, no retries, rejections, refreshes or failures. This condition does **not** measure detailed Fun. Strong worthwhile passages included the membrane (5/5), division (3/3), bone (7/7), regional supply/lymph (6/6), warm/cold (6/7) and blood-sugar comparison (6/6). Hooks were mostly medium. Weak passages included liver (2.3/4), kidney (3.3/6), skin (4/7), and low/medium hooks in clock and ending. These are mixed results, not a whole-story improvement claim.

- **H6 — use what was built:** kidney filtering used a second, preassembled drainage illustration, leaving the actual organ fittings routine. Route the recovered fluid through the player-built kidneys, ureters and bladder. Same action count; predict more value from the constructions. Also correct the heart's conditional illustrations, which wrongly described a previously filled valve gap as empty.
- **H7 — replace serial connection tails with parallel route construction:** hearing and balance already have independent sensor projects, but the final nerve/body additions repeat the same completed scene. Prepare the body-side incoming/outgoing routes independently, then connect the sensors to them. Predict more useful project choice without requiring harder terminology or adding actions. This does not by itself guarantee a higher hook.
- **H8 — fresh endings:** ask clean-context authors for a body-clock physiology comparison and a renewal finale, giving only scope and rough purpose. The first attempted author spawns inherited context and were immediately stopped; replacement authors received no parent history and did not inspect the old chapters or scores. Test their candidates before adoption. The compact clock may be an earned physiology payoff after constructing senses, not a pretext for padding the stage with clock equipment.

The selected immune experiment adds recognition, activation and multiplication to the earlier three-action antibody/memory sketch. A clean author brainstormed three variants; an independent reviewer compared two anonymous, note-stripped variants and preferred the five-action response, with the threat and lymph-node support made visible. The selection is a hypothesis until its new-audience playtests finish. Activation includes help from neighbouring immune cells rather than asserting that arbitrary antigen binding is sufficient for every lymphocyte.

**Second candidate, whole-story detailed:** [32 chapter reports](playtests/2026.11.13_17.13.58/report.md), 96 players and grades, 32 syntheses, 554 actions, no failed records. It differs from the first fast candidate only in the five-action immune chapter. The actual opening is more comprehensible, but several weak patterns remain: heart 1.7/9 Fun and 4.7/9 Worthwhile; vision 1.3/8 and 7/8; skin 2.7/7 and 3.7/7. Neural tissue and breathing each have 1/4 Fun. High numerical results can also conceal friction: absorption's 5.3/7.7 Fun includes reasonable attempts blocked by an unnecessary order, followed by an artificial merge of two blood samples. Preserve the grades but do not preserve these problems to protect the numerator.

**Third candidate, focused tests:** [fast](playtests/2026.11.13_17.25.43/report.md), seven chapters; [detailed](playtests/2026.11.13_17.25.52/report.md), six chapters. H6's player-built kidney drainage improved detailed Worthwhile from 2.7/6 to 5/6; Fun moved from 1.7/6 to 2/6, with one high hook. H7's parallel routes improved hearing from 2.3/6 Fun and 4.7/6 Worthwhile to 6/7.3 and 7/7.3; balance from 2.3/6 and 4.7/6 to 5.3/7 and 7/7. Both hooks stayed medium. Extra actions are inspections, not extra required recipes, and must be inspected rather than described as more construction. H8 did **not** improve the endings' hook: both remain medium. Renewal's detailed 2/5 Fun and 4/5 Worthwhile are unchanged; the shorter clock has 1/3 Fun and 2.7/3 Worthwhile, versus the preceding 2/4.7 and 4/4.7. Treat compactness and different subject matter as editorial choices, not demonstrated score gains.

- **H9 — chapter boundaries can suppress independent projects:** merge lung tissues and breathing mechanics into one eight-action chapter, retaining airways, exchange surfaces and muscular chest as three open projects. No new action or molecular prerequisite. Compare with both old chapters together, not either one alone.
- **H10 — finish a prepared functional system:** move the nerve-cell supply work into one of its two independent branches, so convergence completes the tissue rather than preceding another obligatory attachment. Add the missing purpose of maintaining particle differences to the pump introduction. The integrated body picture should visibly connect cell work, nerve signals, blood and lymph to movement.
- **H11 — enact immune consequences:** extend the five-action immune response to seven, letting antibody-assisted engulfment and a later memory response develop as separate projects after activation/multiplication. Explicitly frame these as comparison views; a future view may be explored before the clearance view without claiming chronological completion.
- **H12 — replace repetitive organ fittings:** fresh clean-context authors receive only rough scope for heart and vision. Compare their outputs empirically; do not assume fresh authorship raises Fun. Skin receives a distinct tissue-supply observation and a visible contrast between resisting water loss and releasing sweat.
- **H13 — remove artificial difficulty even if the old score falls:** allow capillaries to join the intestinal lining before the outgoing vein, reveal the uptake goal only when the surface exists, and stop combining two already-absorbed blood samples as if that made a new physiological event. Predict fewer misguided attempts; distinguish this from any loss of independent useful projects.

Independent reviews found the immune recognition/activation account biologically sound at the selected level, and flagged a real missing marrow/iron prerequisite in the fresh ending, now taught in its opening/title. A delivery concern about pictures after `---` was checked against `src/playtest/picture-descriptions.ts`: native text play extracts braces from both popup and encyclopedia halves, so that concern was not adopted as a defect. The subsequent fresh functional review found a real immune chronology ambiguity, addressed with explicit comparison framing and conditional memory wording. Reviews establish neither human comprehension nor enjoyment. Cancelled cross-review requests to returning authors produced no retained assessment; fresh participants performed the actual review assignments.

**Closer record audit qualifies H7:** the third candidate's apparently large hearing/balance Fun increases include several biologically sensible nerve connections rejected by a newly imposed body-first order. This is counterevidence to the claimed structural improvement, not a reason to celebrate the larger numerator. Preserve those scores and the earlier provisional interpretation, then test a fifth candidate allowing direct sensor-to-nerve, complete-organ-to-route and body-first constructions. Required actions remain six per chapter. The purpose of these alternatives is to remove needless friction; they are not all counted as independent projects or extra combinations toward the target.

**Curriculum correction to the detour budget:** checking the original formatted Edexcel PDF revealed that 7.12B(b–c), skin-vessel widening/narrowing, is Higher Tier, whereas shivering in (a) is not. The initial maximum of three was our editorial interpretation of “a handful,” not the user's numeric cap. The current brief now permits four explicitly named excursions, counting this temperature relationship as D4 rather than hiding it under ordinary homeostasis. Its existing detailed comparison has 3.7/7 Fun, 6.3/7 Worthwhile and medium hook; final fast evidence will be recorded below. GCSE Higher Tier is a scope warning, not a claim of equivalence to American AP/Honors. No further unbudgeted mechanism is authorized by this correction.

**Verification detail:** the whole-story second-candidate audit initially failed because it trimmed the rendered synthesis block but not the expected synthesis's single trailing space. The scratch verifier now applies the same boundary whitespace trim to both strings. Raw provider outputs, retained arrays, grades, arithmetic and report text were not changed. The complete audit then passed: 783 fresh provider requests, no transport retries, protocol rejections or failures. A simultaneous launch collision for the fourth candidate's detailed directory occurred before any participant request; the launch was retried at a distinct timestamp.

Source work adds ordinary NGSS school expectations, Pennsylvania ordinary-biology assessment anchors, an explicitly non-Honors/AP ninth-grade course description, and GCSE immunity expectations. Private originals and Markdown extractions remain under ignored `sources/local/curricula/`; `curriculum.md` owns the term/mechanism audit. Advanced comparison sources remain available but no longer define the baseline.

### Review corrections before the first candidate freeze

Independent manuscript reviewers found misleading pre-completed cell pictures, an unnecessary fluid addition in tissue construction, a pump whose purpose arrived too late, and hearing/clotting mechanisms still too specialized despite generic names. The revision removes the redundant tissue step, makes pump direction visible before use, and simplifies the underlying hearing and clotting operations. It also supplies growth material alongside skin fuel and avoids a misleading retinal orientation. Two reviewers initially saw embedded author notes; their findings are independent manuscript critiques, **not** strictly rationale-blind player readings. The systems reviewer received a note-stripped view before reading. Native playtest participants receive only the normal public packets and the frozen audience guidance.

<a id="ordinary-selected"></a>

### Selected ordinary-biology revision

The sole manuscript now has **171 required combinations across 32 chapters**, 217 recipes including alternatives, and 336 items (161 primitive). Its source SHA-256 is `d4c5660422057561a13cb8455a73cbc0578f57add668606c27927d198afb69c3`. Native lint reports zero errors and 10 reviewed mixed-fate warnings; native analysis completes the required route. This supersedes the older 200-action, AP/Honors-guided selection below. The approximately 200-action/20–30-chapter aspiration remains open: reducing repetitive fittings and knowledge demands took priority over padding the count. The lung/breathing merge did not earn adoption.

This pass completed **17 campaigns, 519 chapter playthroughs, 519 fresh independent grades and 173 fresh chapter syntheses** using actual gpt-6.1-terra at medium effort throughout, as requested. All 519 completed. Full checks verified frozen source/runtime/guidance, native replay, original provider responses, independent packets, zero tool events, report arithmetic and retained arrays. There were 3,042 gameplay actions, 4,268 fresh provider threads, zero transport retries and one rejected protocol reply in the baseline. These are model chapter probes with supplied prior history, not 519 students or continuous whole-game playthroughs.

The same new audience guidance and rubric apply to every campaign. Fresh contexts do not erase a model's specialist knowledge, so the [actual-term and prerequisite audit](curriculum.md) is separate evidence. Whole-story baseline/detailed and candidate fast coverage were followed by targeted retests. The final selection combines applicable local chapter evidence from several versions; it is **not one uniform final-source cohort**. Earlier history and chapter boundaries can affect impressions. The table maps every retained chapter to its exact report condition rather than claiming every untested full-game experience is unchanged.

**One post-test presentation edit:** all three final fast energy players found that the pump image did not show the membrane's existing water/sugar routes. The selected caption now explicitly says it is an enlarged pump close-up with neighbouring routes outside the frame. No prerequisite, visible project, solution, result capability or action count changes. The D9/F9 evidence is retained with this narrow limitation; no additional Fun claim is attributed to the caption. Other post-test edits fix spacing in an invisible author note and add invisible three-goal acknowledgments for the independent hearing/balance projects; they change no player packet. The ten remaining warnings concern intentional lifecycle roles: membrane views are preserved for use and transformed when a second route is added; six compound structures remain while fitted and are incorporated into a larger whole; immune descendants can produce antibodies or join the body; healing skin is used in repair before its final transition.

#### What the later experiments changed

- **H9 rejected — merging is not automatically choice.** The eight-action lung/breathing candidate scored 3.3/8 Fun and 6.3/8 Worthwhile, with Medium Hook. The older separate passages together had 3/4.7 + 1/4 Fun and 4.3/4.7 + 3/4 Worthwhile. Denominators include investigation and cannot establish a controlled pacing gain. Restore separate chapters, then permit either local elastic-support/capillary order in the lung. The retest gives 3.3/5 Fun and 4.3/5 Worthwhile. Breathing remains a short, explicitly designated payoff to building heart and lung, not evidence that its low Fun is generally acceptable.
- **H10/H12 partly supported — finish useful systems.** Completing the nerve-cell supply within a parallel branch moves Fun from 1/4 to 1.7/4, but Worthwhile slips from 4/4 to 3.7/4. Retain with that tradeoff. A clean heart author considered chamber catalogues, a blood-drop tour and three parallel circulations. The selected lung/body/heart-muscle construction improves the first school draft from 1.7/9 Fun and 4.7/9 Worthwhile to 4/7 and 7/7. This is the clearest successful replacement of repeated fittings. The fresh optics/retina construction raises vision Fun from 1.3/8 to 2/6, while Worthwhile falls from 7/8 to 5/6: a shorter, clearer draft, not an across-the-board score win. Skin's changes leave Fun at 2.7/7 and improve Worthwhile from 3.7/7 to 4.7/7; Medium Hook persists.
- **H11 supported within limits — give activation consequences.** The five-action immune chapter had 1.7/5 Fun and 4/5 Worthwhile. Recognition/multiplication now feeds present antibody-assisted clearance and a possible later memory response: 3/7 and 6.3/7, Medium Hook. Longer play explains part of the larger counts. Retain the functional contrast and explicit comparison-view framing, without introducing helper-T/B-cell choreography. No claim that activation alone guarantees clearance.
- **H13 supported as a friction repair, not a Fun gain.** Allowing an intestinal capillary network before its outgoing vein removes a sensible rejected move. Cutting the merge of already-absorbed samples lowers recorded Fun from 5.3/7.7 to 2.3/6 and Worthwhile from 7.3/7.7 to 4.7/6. Airway routes similarly decline from 2.7/5 Fun to 1.7/5 after a direct equivalent joining route is permitted. Keep the honest compatibility and fewer redundant operations; report the loss rather than retaining arbitrary failures for a higher score. These passages remain candidates for stronger independent projects.
- **H14 supported — preparation goals invite the available work.** Describing the pump's purpose alone did not stop ATP-plus-unfitted-pump attempts. Showing separate preparation goals for ribosome and pump, alongside ATP, gives 3.3/6 Fun and 5/6 Worthwhile. Moving glucose entry into this same energy chapter honors the brief and brings the required count to seven. The final test gives 3.7/7 Fun, 5.3/7 Worthwhile and three High hooks; imported glucose followed by a fed-cell intermediate remains repetitive. These results are below the matched original baseline's 4/7 Fun and 6.3/7 Worthwhile, despite simpler prerequisites. Do not call simplification a demonstrated score increase.
- **H15 — natural routes also need equivalent goals.** Candidate 5 permitted sensible sensor/nerve/body orders, but goals still demanded obsolete body-first intermediate identities. All players reached functioning systems and then met redundant tasks. Reject the apparent balance score of 6.7/10: it rewarded investigation of this mismatch. Candidate 6 uses alternative goal achievements for equivalent completed routes. Hearing settles at 2.7/6 Fun, 4/6 Worthwhile; balance at 3.3/6 and 4.7/6, each with one High/two Medium hooks. Retain the coherent routes, not the inflated failure counts.
- **H16 — a completed membrane must remain usable.** Moving glucose entry into chapter 2 exposed sensible attempts to add a sugar carrier to a water-channel membrane in chapter 1. Candidate 8 accepted them but introduced an analysis dependency cycle by returning separate membrane identities to one another; discard that representation despite completed playtests. Candidate 9 makes a new combined membrane with both capabilities, usable for glucose entry or pump fitting. First-chapter Fun becomes 2/4, Worthwhile 4/4 and Hook Medium. The earlier 3.7/5 included rejected moves and is not a reason to restore them. Oxygen following its already-explained crossing remains a modest confirmation rather than a fresh surprise.

The fresh clock and renewal authors did not improve Hook beyond Medium. Their shorter, less specialized versions are retained as editorial choices with explicit limits. Fresh semantic readers of the repaired goal routes and opening handoff found no further material prerequisite defect; the later actual membrane playtests still caught an expectation those manuscript reviews missed. All later blind packets excluded author notes and prior criticism. Initial reviewer exposure limits remain recorded above.

#### Remaining weaknesses and bounded payoffs

Independent constructions work best when their purposes differ visibly: protein-making versus pumping, lung/body/heart-muscle circulation, digestive transformations, warm/cold responses, and turn/tilt sensing. Local fitting-order alternatives remove needless friction but do not themselves create those projects. Visible goals must invite the independent work and acknowledge all valid completed routes.

Low-Fun chapters are not automatically exceptions. **13 (breathing)** is retained as the physical payoff to 9–12's transport/exchange constructions; **20 (water balance)** applies 19's filter/recovery work; **31 (body clock)** offers a short comparison after building senses. Their respective detailed results are 1/4 Fun, 3/4 Worthwhile; 1/3, 3/3; and 1/3, 2.7/3. These are intended earned payoffs, with mostly Medium hooks, not permission for serial low-choice filler. Division (1/3 Fun) is a short foundational passage, not relabelled an emotional payoff; its one choice between DNA copying and membrane supply remains thin. Blood, liver, absorption, airway defense, skin and the final renewal also need stronger Fun or hook evidence before claiming the whole game meets the final quality ambition. The selection is a complete audience rewrite and tested draft, not a declaration that all earlier Fun concerns are solved.

The four explicitly budgeted excursions are lymph return (14), opposed blood-sugar control (23), mechanical balance (30) and skin-vessel heat delivery (22). Their exact terms, curriculum boundaries, both modes' scores and retention rationale live in [curriculum.md](curriculum.md#detour-budget-and-current-evidence). Immune activation is supported at this school level by the ordinary GCSE specifications, while lymph-node support and antibody-assisted engulfment remain explicitly locally taught extensions. Those labels do not certify prior knowledge.

<a id="fun-revision"></a>

## Fun, worthwhile and hook together — 13 November 2026

The user authorized an unrestricted number of score-guided revisions to repair Anatomy, and requested a standing authoring rule giving Fun its proper importance. Good worthwhile and hook results must coexist with good Fun evidence. A few high-worthwhile, low-Fun chapters may provide emotional or construction payoffs after meaningful play; this is an exception for earned payoffs only. Identify the preceding work and its culmination rather than relabeling ordinary weak chapters after testing. The adopted rule belongs in [story authoring](../../docs/story-authoring.md#make-combining-worthwhile); [evaluation](../../docs/fun-and-evaluation.md#report-a-profile) owns how to report the separate measures and their limits.

### Focused revision of chapters 1–5 — 13 November 2026

The user narrowed the next active work to grading, updating and iterating on the first five chapters under the clarified Fun criterion. Continue the larger study only as background; do not promote the untested later-system rewrites as part of this task. Fresh Terra requests are working again. All new player, independent-grader and synthesis requests retain actual gpt-6.1-terra at medium effort, identical audience guidance and the corrected standing rubric. The already verified unchanged-source baseline supplies the comparator; do not spend another cohort re-establishing its forced opening.

<a id="selected-opening"></a>

#### Selected opening and comparison

**Completed selection:** the sole [manuscript](anatomy.md) is now SHA-256 `7f81494f9c14c654b5defe62bd231d1339212f930a31bc3202632186d4476fdc`, with **200 required combinations across 32 chapters**, 241 recipes, zero lint errors and 12 reviewed mixed-fate warnings. Five focused paired revision rounds completed **120 chapter playthroughs, 120 independent grades and 40 chapter syntheses**, using actual gpt-6.1-terra at medium effort throughout. Two fresh authors replaced stubborn energy/division approaches. All rounds used the same audience guidance and evaluation runtime/rubric; their records and contrary results remain below. This is chapter-level evidence using planned-picture text, not a human enjoyment or learning result.

The comparison uses the [unchanged-source clarified baseline](playtests/2026.11.13_09.12.48/report.md), not the older incorrect criterion. The final column uses chapter 1 from [round 2](playtests/2026.11.13_12.34.08/report.md), chapters 2–3 from [round 5](playtests/2026.11.13_13.44.10/report.md), and chapters 4–5 from [round 4](playtests/2026.11.13_13.27.12/report.md). Inherited evidence and its applicability are explained in the round-5 scope below. Counts are mean credited actions / mean recorded gameplay actions, not ratings out of a fixed maximum.

| Chapter | Before Fun | After Fun | Before worthwhile | After worthwhile | After detailed hooks M/H |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1. Ways across | 0.7/4 | 2.7/4.7 | 4/4 | 4.7/4.7 | 1/2 |
| 2. The powerhouse of the cell | 0/4 | 4/7 | 4/4 | 6.7/7 | 1/2 |
| 3. More cells, complete genomes | 0/4 | 2.7/6 | 4/4 | 6/6 | 0/3 |
| 4. The same DNA, different work | 0.3/5 | 2.7/5 | 5/5 | 5/5 | 0/3 |
| 5. Strong bones, moving joints | 1.3/7 | 2.7/8 | 7/7 | 8/8 | 2/1 |

The baseline energy hook was already three High; the revision earns decisions while retaining two High/one Medium, not an improvement in every measure. Bone is still the weakest detailed Fun passage; its score is better than the matched baseline but lower than several intervening versions. Some of those earlier counts rewarded investigation of arbitrary restrictions now removed. Do not call the later declines statistical proof of either improvement or damage: these are small fresh cohorts. Inspect the recorded actions and their purposes alongside the counts.

Applicable fast results are **5.7/6, 6/7, 6/6, 5/5 and 7.7/8 worthwhile**, respectively. Their hooks are **1M/2H, 1M/2H, 1M/2H, 0M/3H and 2M/1H**. Fast mode does not measure Fun. Chapters 1, 4 and 5 inherit their identified rounds; chapters 2–3 have the new [round-5 fast results](playtests/2026.11.13_13.44.09/report.md).

The first five now require **30 combinations** rather than 24. One shared membrane tutorial opens into independent transport comparisons. Pump preparation, ribosome assembly and fuel processing offer different useful work; ATP connects the projects. Division has independent membrane, genome and spindle preparations before whole-cell convergence. Two descendant lineages compare common material under different instructions. Bone and the joint capsule develop alongside each other before meeting. These are real project choices under normal compatibility information, not a requirement to guess, risk failure or reach different endings. Optional local assembly orders are not counted as additional independent projects.

**Retained tradeoffs and useful patterns.** Passive-channel installation now shows flow immediately because the fluids and driving conditions already exist; the active pump still needs its ATP operation, which supplies a different physiological contribution. Whole-cell division replaces joining two views of the same cell. Removing a generic tissue-completion join and ribosome staging reduces work that merely repeats an already pictured state. Drawn mechanisms matter: shortening captions too far lost the DNA-copying and spindle explanations, and alternative routes must actually encounter the relevant picture. The two lineages' similar protein-making operations still feel repetitive, but their retained-versus-secreted products and unfinished muscle development earn value and interest. Equivalent ATP sources remain equivalent; no artificial consequence is added to make that compatibility choice look strategic.

There is **no blanket low-Fun exemption for these five chapters**. Their forced final steps are local payoffs after independent construction: ATP does useful work, copied chromosomes become two complete daughter cells, selected cell activity produces tissue material, and separately built bone/capsule structures become a moving joint. The remaining mild repetition and compressed organelle/signaling detail stay visible in the reports. Later chapters have not acquired new Fun scores from this pass.

Scope is limited to chapters 1–5 and the necessary chapter-6 handoff: the old duplicate muscle-precursor supply/recipe and duplicate global description are consolidated into the precursor population already built in chapter 4. Chapters **7–32 are byte-for-byte unchanged**. The broader 30-chapter proposal remains unadopted. Global recipes still permit legitimate early completion; native checks demonstrate both ATP uses before aerobic renewal, multiple division orders, and natural bone/joint assembly orders. The full native route reaches the ending. Use **Restart story** for browser progress made against the older opening.

**Round 1 hypotheses, recorded before results.** Use the prepared independent-construction opening with its revised visible goals, integrated into the canonical later chapters. Transport routes should offer distinct useful projects after one shared membrane tutorial. Fuel entry and pump installation should be independent preparations that meet at ATP-powered work. Division should expose cell growth, genome copying and spindle construction before convergence; its forced final joins may supply the payoff without every action needing a choice. Differentiation should make the same amino-acid supply produce different proteins under different messages, retaining its previous strong hook. Living bone and a lined joint capsule should be separately useful projects. Reject gains that depend on obscure instructions, arbitrary rejected combinations or extra low-value fittings. Do not require an additional experiment or failure risk.

The source is `00920c428a0e2d2589c4d38ca7e946cf789a2bc0d0a6c11ede91ab181d610f1c`. It changes chapters 1–5 and one necessary downstream handoff: chapter 6 consumes the muscle precursors already developed in chapter 4, removing its obsolete second induction. Leaving that global recipe would otherwise let players bypass the new differentiation path using a later primitive. The muscle precursor title matches the existing downstream identity. Chapter 6's other construction and goals, and chapters 7–32, remain unchanged. No later chapter inherits a new Fun score from these opening tests.

Mechanical checks give **204 required combinations, 32 chapters, 240 total recipes, zero lint errors and 13 reviewed mixed-fate warnings**. The first five chapters require 6, 7, 7, 6 and 8 constructions respectively. They replace the canonical first-five counts of 4, 4, 4, 5 and 7. Only material needed for the new relationships is added; alternative rules do not count toward required play. At this stage the canonical file remained frozen pending selection.

A fresh isolated Terra manuscript reader saw the first five chapters, age/format context and the independent-construction criterion, without old criticism, results or target scores. It found distinct useful projects and a coherent membrane-to-tissue hook, with no requested gameplay rewrite. Its sole apparatus objection is not adopted: the goal-count acknowledgment is intentionally invisible author metadata, and source/illustration notes belong in the manuscript. The supplied excerpt mistakenly retained some continuation lines of those notes; native player packets exclude them. This is a packet limitation, not evidence that players see production notes, nor a reason to remove grounded source material.

The first paired test uses three fresh players per chapter per mode, fresh independent grading and fresh synthesis. Source, runtime, guidance and criteria stay frozen. Candidate inputs, mechanical records and isolated review packets are temporary under ignored `runs/anatomy-opening-revision/`; the existing scratch harness owns the runtime sessions. The task will retain actual response arrays/reports and concise comparisons here, with failed hypotheses and score regressions left visible.

#### Focused round 1 results and round 2 hypotheses

Completed and independently verified [fast](playtests/2026.11.13_12.04.14/report.md) and [detailed](playtests/2026.11.13_12.04.15/report.md) campaigns: each has 15 completed chapter players, 15 fresh independent grades and five fresh syntheses. Fast has 107 actions, 122 accepted original player replies and 142 fresh provider threads; detailed has 111 actions, 126 replies and 146 threads. No provider retries, protocol rejections, failed sessions or refreshes. Exact responses, native replay, grader output, synthesis packets, arithmetic and assembled reports all verified. Criteria and guidance match the clarified baseline.

| Ch | Baseline detailed Fun | Round 1 detailed Fun | Round 1 detailed worthwhile | Detailed hooks M/H | Round 1 fast worthwhile | Fast hooks M/H |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 0.7/4 | 4.7/7 | 5.7/7 | 2/1 | 6.3/7.7 | 2/1 |
| 2 | 0/4 | 3.7/8 | 7.7/8 | 2/1 | 6.7/7 | 1/2 |
| 3 | 0/4 | 5/7.7 | 7.7/7.7 | 0/3 | 6.7/7 | 0/3 |
| 4 | 0.3/5 | 3.7/6 | 5.7/6 | 0/3 | 6/6 | 0/3 |
| 5 | 1.3/7 | 4.7/8.3 | 8.3/8.3 | 0/3 | 8/8 | 1/2 |

The baseline's first five had 24 required actions and high educational-value counts despite very little choice. Round 1 changes both amount and structure of play; the higher decision numerators alone do not isolate which change caused the response. Some new meaningful counts also reward failed attempts or investigation of unexplained prerequisites. Keep the original grades; remove arbitrary friction rather than retaining it to inflate Fun. The successful pattern is different useful constructions that meet, with the division-to-differentiation and bone-to-muscle connections supplying clear hooks. No opening chapter is claimed as an exemption for low Fun.

Observed weaknesses and prospective round-2 tests:

- **1: reduce passive-channel busywork.** All detailed players and two fast players found installing each channel and then separately testing it repetitive. The surrounding water, sodium and gradients already exist. Let an installed open channel immediately show its passive flow, removing two intermediate operations while keeping optional bare-lipid comparisons. Test whether rate-versus-direction and charge-versus-route remain valued, and whether hook/choice survives the shorter chapter; do not count a denominator reduction as a new insight.
- **2: remove arbitrary process rejection and clarify material roles.** Two detailed players tried the ATP-producing cytosolic state at the living-cell hotspot and failed; pyruvate arrows had pointed there. Rename that state to the concrete “Pyruvate and ATP” and allow the same fuel-plus-oxygen operation in the existing cell context as well as directly. Remove unexplained GTP from the player picture while retaining its biochemical role in notes. Give the opening a concise purpose: maintaining internal differences and making cell material require continuing work. Test natural compatibility, clarity and retained project choice. A lower decision count caused by losing failed attempts is not automatically a worse experience, but the remaining successful choices must still be evident.
- **3: clarify preparation and matching views.** Growth's membrane-vesicle intermediate was not apparent to several players. Show membrane preparation as the first stage of its goal, distinguish vesicle fusion from continuing intracellular growth, and use matching locators for the grown body and chromosome close-up. Show chromosome attachment before separation. Preserve the three real preparations; do not replace them with repeated raw-material slots or add a phase-name checklist. Test whether direct-growth and premature-division confusion recedes without losing the strongly valued separation/partition distinction.
- **4: cut the redundant generic tissue join.** Every player found some repetition in recombining fibroblasts with matrix already pictured beside those living cells. Remove that last operation and move its worthwhile extracellular-material insight into the secretion/assembly result. Keep the two lineages and same-amino-acids/different-messages contrast. Simplify nonessential lineage labels while retaining precise developmental grounding in notes. Test whether the shorter chapter preserves the tissue insight and high muscle-development hook; restore or rethink if it loses them.
- **5: allow a natural alternate construction order.** All three detailed players tried or investigated adding bone cells to the mineralised framework before its blood-supply detail. Support that order through an honest cells-in-matrix state before connecting vessels; keep the existing supply-first route. This local permutation repairs friction and is not claimed as another independent project. Give the completed-bone picture its combined structure instead of repeating the preceding nutrient-channel view. Test whether the rejection disappears while the bone/capsule choices and valued joint payoff remain.

A fresh isolated Terra factual reader saw the revised five chapters, their author notes explicitly identified as invisible, the current age/format constraints, and licensed OpenStax membrane, protein-synthesis, division and bone-structure readings. It accepted the core mechanisms and requested visible clarification of anatomical reconstruction, a self-renewing population rather than exactly two cells being spent across organs, and matching same-cell division views. These were adopted in the picture plans and brief prose; the bone lineage preserves the shared population. It did not establish enjoyment or student comprehension. The energy introduction was subsequently shortened around the same physiological purpose; no new mechanism was added.

The user clarified that **early completion is welcome**: chapter goals and cleanup guide attention, while global recipes may let a precocious player finish later work. The old muscle recipe is therefore not rejected for being available in chapter 4. A native audit confirms that retaining it adds both “Skeletal-muscle differentiation conditions” and “Skeletal-muscle differentiation signals” there, two near-synonymous supplies representing the same work at different granularity; the obsolete supply also appears in chapter 6 after its result already exists. The retained edit consolidates that duplication. No chapter gate is added. A separate native check demonstrates that proteins can be completed before the energy chapter reveals their later staged goal, using cytosolic ATP before aerobic renewal. The rule is recorded in the brief and owning authoring/UX guidance.

**Energy needs a fresh approach.** Its clearer enzyme site and three goals removed the recurring whole-cell target failure and increased detailed decision credit. Alternative ATP sources were useful but some players expected a consequential difference where either source served the same job. These are reasons to rethink the construction, not to manufacture an arbitrary distinction between identical ATP molecules. **Division's structural repair worked but its explanation became too thin.** Both modes lost some worthwhile credit and hooks became mixed. The whole-cell construction removed the earlier artificial join and its rejections; three fast players chose among the independent preparations, while all detailed players followed the same growth-first order. Neither identical choices nor divergent choices alone establishes Fun. Missing mechanisms now explain the educational loss: the copied-DNA picture omitted templating, the spindle merely named tubulin/motors, and its application jumped over attachment and separation. Keep six operations and whole-cell continuity, restore those mechanisms as drawable insets, remove an unexplained S-phase label, and depict ER production/Golgi delivery in the membrane view. **Bone exposes two further local ordering expectations.** One detailed player put cells with the unmineralised collagen view; another put the capsule around bones before highlighting cartilage. Both automatic redirects completed useful operations, and worthwhile remained full, but their original intentions were not arbitrary. Add those two anatomical-cutaway orders, keeping the same eight required operations and the same independently useful bone/capsule projects. No Fun gain is claimed merely for extra permutations. Pictures must identify these as constituent views of existing mature anatomy, not osteocytes developing before matrix or surgery on bare joint surfaces. Retest the altered chapter.

**Status at the earlier interruption, superseded by the focused selection above:** the revision was unfinished. The canonical `anatomy.md` was still SHA-256 `43a1726dfc1ce9b1a88f2c5f4109395c45b4eaebf5676ade6a1ca1657f2774e3` (195 required combinations, 32 chapters), with the rejected linear opening still present. The later focused pass above replaces that opening; the broader candidates described here remain historical, unadopted proposals. The second candidate has 203 required combinations across 30 chapters; its whole-story paired evaluation was interrupted by the CLI account's usage limit. A subsequent goal-only candidate remains untested and unadopted.

**Why vessels received zero Fun.** The [original-criterion chapter 10 report](playtests/2026.11.13_08.28.51/chapter10_report.md) gives all three players 0/6. Its graders discounted recognisable, predictable construction because it lacked uncertainty or substantial inference. The synthesis explicitly observed one player beginning with arteries and two with capillaries, but dismissed this as routine ordering rather than competing hypotheses. That was the wrong criterion for the user's intended choice between useful independent projects. The source also had only one displayed goal, “Connect arteries, capillaries and veins into a route through tissues.” It imposed no sequence but failed to invite the three projects separately. Poor invitation is a distinct design weakness; it cannot explain away a grading rule that rejected choices players actually made.

The [clarified-criterion rerun](playtests/2026.11.13_09.12.48/chapter10_report.md) uses the **unchanged manuscript**, fresh players and graders. Every grader awards **4/6 meaningful decisions**; worthwhile is **6/6**, hooks one high and two medium. This is a changed measurement with a new cohort, not a manuscript improvement. The original report and all contrary judgments remain intact. Choosing independent constructions is legitimate even when their purpose is obvious and both are necessary; later routine joins can remain uncredited.

The user additionally clarified that usually two goals, occasionally three, should invite exploration as Creation does. Adopted guidance now lives in [purposeful goals](../../docs/story-authoring.md#write-purposeful-goals-and-continuity) and [UX](../../docs/ux.md#goals-and-readings), with this story's intent in its single brief. Keep dependent stages within each project; do not conceal independently useful work behind one final objective or a single sequential chain. Goal visibility can affect Fun, so treat these revisions as new conditions requiring applicable tests.
