**Baseline:** freeze the current 32-chapter, 195-required-action manuscript at SHA-256 `43a1726dfc1ce9b1a88f2c5f4109395c45b4eaebf5676ade6a1ca1657f2774e3`. Campaign `2026.11.13_08.28.51` uses `--detailed` across every chapter, three fresh Terra Medium players per chapter, independent Terra Medium grading of each record and a separate fresh chapter synthesis. Use the unchanged grades 10–12 audience guidance, canonical rubrics and normal compatibility information. This is a new baseline for the actual manuscript; earlier detailed results cannot stand in for it. Concurrent concept drafting takes place outside the frozen manuscript.

**Prospective hypotheses:**

- Independent constructions with shared ingredients and meaningful convergence will let informed players choose what to investigate and discover further uses for familiar parts. More possible action orders alone is insufficient: look for actual inference, intention and consequences in the records.
- Grouping closely related subjects around an anatomical problem may make choices available sooner than small chapters of compulsory preparations. Test whether the resulting working set stays intelligible, especially in the opening.
- Physiology can offer substantive comparative choices through the different work of a membrane, route, signal or material. Distinct uses must be visible and biologically grounded; arbitrary extra recipes, needless inspection and withheld hints would not support this hypothesis.
- A short convergence chapter may deliver an earned payoff after varied construction. This remains a prospective arc judgment to test against its actual preceding play, not permission to retain a succession of low-Fun chapters.

**Method:** a fresh author develops three opening alternatives without seeing the rejected manuscript or its scores; a separate blind manuscript review examines later action patterns. Review concrete candidates independently before native lint and playtests. Use fast trials to reject weak worthwhile/hook candidates, then detailed trials for all three measures; the material structural changes require new detailed evidence. Record original counts and denominators, hook distributions, failed hypotheses and keep/change/reject decisions. Do not improve apparent results by changing rubrics, feeding criticism or target scores to participants, or selectively rewriting grades. Preserve better-supported content; use fresh authors when a design stops improving. No graphics, screenshots or engine changes.

### User clarification: independent construction is a meaningful choice

The user clarified the intended kind of Fun after the first baseline. These games normally converge, often at chapter end; a wrong choice need not threaten completion. `(A+B)+C` versus `(A+C)+B` usually offers a weak permutation on one object. `(A+B)+(C+D)` offers different useful things to build, and choosing which to pursue is itself legitimate Fun. The earlier demand for something beyond ordering was too broad: it incorrectly risked dismissing independent constructive projects along with trivial fitting permutations. Experiments and difficult inference can add value, but they are not required to justify this kind of agency.

Direct inspection confirms the example in Creation. Once Void exists, Earth + Void constructs Proto-Earth while Fire + Void constructs Sun; their results meet as Light. Earth + Waters constructs Asteroids, feeding Moon, which meets the world again. These are useful separate construction threads even though the chapter's conclusion is shared. This is a manuscript/graph observation, not a new Creation playtest or numerical score.

The owning authoring and evaluation documents now distinguish these cases explicitly, including the independent-grader and chapter-comparison criteria. **This is a user-directed criterion change, not a manuscript improvement.** Preserve all earlier original records and scores; do not compare higher new-criterion numbers directly with old-criterion numbers as a design gain. The old 95/603 aggregate below establishes what those graders credited, not an adequate measurement of the clarified independent-construction requirement. The first four chapters' forced one-option structure remains an actual manuscript defect under either criterion.

**New comparison launched:** the unchanged canonical source `43a1726d…` receives a fresh whole-story detailed baseline in `2026.11.13_09.12.48`. The first integrated candidate, SHA-256 `a9850e3e7e5e6f83d1d27176f297b7fbfab72411a3bdf762adffc4fa6ff1cc67`, receives separate fast and detailed cohorts for chapters 1–4, 6–8, 10, 22 and 23. All use the clarified criteria and the identical preceding audience guidance. No target scores, previous criticism or author routes enter participant packets. The candidate has 195 required actions across 32 chapters, zero lint errors and 16 reviewed mixed-fate warnings. Both candidate and canonical source stay frozen during their campaigns.

The integrated candidate retains genuine membrane comparisons, independent membrane/energy work, parallel specialization with a useful myoblast output, neural constructions and responses, and parallel regulatory functions. A factual/prerequisite review found no blocking contradiction; adopted small corrections show respiratory CO₂, specify concentration gradients at the pump, and match the number of input spikes to connected neural inputs. It disclosed incidental exposure to older curriculum-audit labels/counts, but no old manuscript, scores or reviews. Its gameplay predictions preceded the user's clarification and cannot override the newly specified independent-construction criterion. A separate fresh cardiopulmonary candidate remains unselected scratch. Do not replace later chapters simply because the earlier rubric gave their legitimate parallel work little credit.

The initial four chapters and vision remain weak even under the corrected criterion. Several apparently meaningful actions are attempts frustrated by arbitrary prerequisites (red cells before oxygen, bare cord before peripheral nerves, inner-ear sensors before their enclosing chambers); removing those failures may reduce counted decisions while improving the experience. Record that tradeoff rather than copying the obstruction.

The second candidate therefore retains the original vessel chapter, repairs the opening and neural passages, reconstructs bone/joint, heart/lungs and kidney/water work, replaces duplicate liver glucose processing with portal entry/exit construction, and tests a shared-retina vision/clock chapter. The breathing merge and vision/clock merge bring the outline to 30 chapters; they must earn their larger working sets in actual play. Subsequent factual review of energy, division, neural, portal and vision excerpts found no established blocker. Adopted minor clarifications show a faint grey-matter region rather than an apparent hollow cord, explicitly compare weak excitation with inhibition, and illustrate near/far lens focusing. The reader saw no earlier drafts, scores or criticisms; it does not establish enjoyment or student understanding.

Prospective payoff candidates for the next arc are **Connecting the systems**, earned by the earlier tissue/nerve/circulation/respiratory constructions, and **Still being made**, returning the completed body's ongoing maintenance to the opening's cellular mechanisms. **A body is a habitat** is a short conceptual culmination of the digestion/absorption route: what remains for microbes is still part of the body's living relationships. Its old 3/3 worthwhile result supports that role, but the next integrated reading must preserve the connection. No payoff designation is made for ordinary setup; the failed division-slot version is discarded. Water regulation remains a test of a useful brain/kidney relationship, not an automatic payoff exemption.

**Decisions and next hypotheses:**

- Retain the independent opening projects as the basis for another trial. Chapters 1–2 now offer choices beyond the earlier chain, but channel installation remains repetitive and the ATP chapter's three-ingredient hotspot creates hidden prerequisites. Replace those context-dependent joins with the intuitive binary combinations between already cellular preparations; preserve energy and material distinctions. Clarify first-use terms in drawable labels, not an extra essay.
- **Reject division's three growth slots.** Detailed Fun 0.7/5 and worthwhile 3/5, with the same weak growth fittings in fast, do not justify a payoff exception. Commission an author who has not seen that chapter or its results to construct separate growth, genome-copying and spindle projects. The preceding membrane/energy chapters would earn a brief culmination, but do not make repetitive low-value preparation acceptable.
- Keep the parallel differentiation hypothesis: 3.7/6 Fun, 6/6 worthwhile and three high hooks in detailed; 6/6 and three high in fast. The tissue recombination still felt artificial to some players, so avoid adding more versions of it.
- Muscle's physiology remains worthwhile (8/8 in both modes). Permit tissue construction before the contraction/reset demonstration instead of making that experiment a gate. Keep the same scale for biceps and triceps, and make the fibre-to-fascicle population and matrix relationship visible.
- Neural results separate decisions from payoffs: the circuit chapter's 6/8.7 detailed Fun contrasts with only 4.7/8 fast worthwhile. Clarify excitatory contacts and the circuit-to-grey-matter join; accept the physiologically sensible additional inhibition arrangement. System construction is promising (6.3/8.3 Fun; 8.3/8.3 worthwhile), but arbitrary brain/cord and touch/cord restrictions caused failures. Add honest intermediate routes rather than retaining friction for its credited decisions.
- **Reject the vessel-name experiment provisionally.** The unchanged graph under the same new criterion receives 4/6 Fun and 6/6 worthwhile in the completed baseline chapter; material-only names yield 3.7/6 and 4/6 in detailed. These small cohorts do not isolate causation, but offer no reason to replace the clearer retained version. This also directly supports the user's observation: independent artery/capillary/vein construction already works without further experiments.
- Keep the shorter thermoregulation and glucose-response candidates for continued testing: each gives 5/5 worthwhile in both modes, with detailed Fun 3.3/5 and 2.7/5. Different targets and different physiological conditions supply useful work. Remove the earlier liver chapter's duplicate storage/release sequence before adopting the glucose chapter; use that earlier chapter for the portal circulation instead.

**Next unselected candidates:** independently authored heart/lung and kidney/water constructions, a root-authored bone/capsule branch, and an author proposal joining vision and daily timing. A fresh tool-free factual reader used the six relevant excerpts and licensed textbook chapters. It found real drawable-continuity issues (closed pleural spaces, airway-region naming, renal perfusion and visible feedback), which are corrected before play. Its two alleged consumption deadlocks rested on an explicitly disclosed incorrect inventory assumption: the specification makes these population resources reusable where future goals require them. Full native lint and completion pass; do not add duplicate material tokens to repair a nonexistent engine defect. Source notes and count comments also need consolidation before promotion. No chapter is adopted solely because a fresh author wrote it.

### Second candidate: whole-story trial frozen

Source `f35239c757edee303879e44b92de443c374cee5f9db4f5854245ab9acfb8f93e`: **203 required combinations, 270 total recipes, 30 chapters**. It remains a candidate in ignored scratch until results are assessed. [Fast campaign](playtests/2026.11.13_09.59.20/report.md) and [detailed campaign](playtests/2026.11.13_09.59.26/report.md) each schedule three fresh players per chapter with separate fresh grades and chapter syntheses. Both cohorts use the same guidance and clarified rubric as the preceding comparison. They share a twelve-request pool; participants receive no prior criticism, candidate scores or expected routes. Whole-story coverage is justified by substantial changes to the opening, tissue/system work, chapter boundaries and later physiology, not a policy of repeating everything after small edits.

**Actual second-candidate changes:** independent division projects replace growth slots; the spindle/nuclear/cell cutaways stay in the same cell. Bone-cell supply, mineralised matrix and a joint capsule provide distinct constructions. Muscle assembly can proceed before its contraction/reset demonstration. Energy and neural recipes accept natural direct joins; brain/cord and peripheral routes can connect in either useful order. Blood can be assembled before oxygen exposure. The clearer original vessels are restored. Independent heart/pulmonary work leads into a merged lung/airway/chest chapter. Portal entry and hepatic return replace duplicate glucose-storage steps. The kidney builds filtering, recovery, collecting and drainage routes; water control connects a brain-to-blood pathway while drinking remains available. Surface cooling and glucose regulation retain their earlier tested parallel responses. Mucus clearance and alveolar digestion become separate branches. Shared retinal tissue links visual and daily-clock work in one chapter. Hearing separates transmission, cochlear mechanics and innervated sensory tissue; vestibular wiring can develop alongside its receptor organs. The final renewal remains a short culmination.

The division, heart/lung, kidney/water and hearing author had not read the corresponding rejected chapters or their scores. Vision/clock and airway work used a previously exposed collaborator as author, explicitly **not** an independent reviewer; the airway author disclosed earlier incidental reading and did not reopen the source or reports. Three fresh tool-free factual reviews saw only the supplied current excerpts and licensed/source references, without scores or earlier versions. Their genuine corrections include pleural enclosures, airway-region naming, visible kidney perfusion and water feedback, central grey-matter depiction, lens focus, otolith gel/hair-bundle attachment, alternating round-window motion and directional oxygen-binding labels. Their conclusions are scientific/clarity review, not measured play.

Native full lint reports **zero errors, 21 reviewed mixed-fate warnings and one no-recipe-opportunity warning**. Mixed fates reflect material populations, optional process observations and eventual incorporation into larger structures. The warning about the optional ascending-touch continuation is a pessimistic-analysis limitation: a native eight-action chapter route explicitly builds the peripheral cord, follows touch upward, adds the brain, continues the retained signal to the cortex and demonstrates a spinal response. It completes with that warned recipe actually executed. The continuous route completes all 30 chapters. These checks validate manuscript logic, not Fun or human understanding; no screenshots, graphics, engine changes or browser audit occurred.

A text-load check follows the actual first-available-action route: **205 actions**, including optional/comparison or preparation work beyond the 203 required-combination minimum. It displays 95 nonempty browser wonder texts, 2,130 words in total (about 22 per popup, maximum 47); planned-image passages are excluded using the native text filter. The previous manuscript's corresponding route had 94 such popups and 1,775 words. This keeps automatic prose brief while acknowledging an increase in explanation; it does not measure human reading time or the clarity of finished pictures. The commentary's approximate 95-popups/203-combinations description used the required count; the actual sampled-route denominator is 205.

A fresh blind whole-arc reader, shown player-facing manuscript content with author notes removed and no scores/history, found the cell-to-body progression worthwhile. It independently identified division, kidney and airway convergences as strengths, and **Still being made** as an earned ending through cell division, kidney/blood and living-bone callbacks. It cautioned about ordinary digestion/repair assembly and goals that announce an object without inviting a structure–function question. Those are hypotheses to compare against actual play, not instructions to discard the already supported independent constructions. It also distinguished useful independent branches from the extra local alternative orders in several chapters.

**Interpretation fixed before results:** the user's minimal `(A+B)+(C+D)` example can contain one choice followed by two completion actions. An honest 1/3 decision-density fraction can describe that useful converging construction; it is not equivalent to a compulsory three-step chain. Do not invent a universal numerical pass threshold, rescore routine actions, or add uncertainty to inflate the numerator. Keep the operational counts and compare actual live projects, their recognition by players, repetition, worthwhile payoff and desire to continue. This interpretation follows the user's example and changes neither current participant instructions nor the frozen grader rubric.

**What the baseline changes:** the weakness is wider than the opening. All three graders assign zero meaningful actions to vessels (10), gas exchange (12), liver (17), kidney (19) and vision (28), despite valued results in most of them. Shared ingredients and independent builds alone do not suffice: vessel construction is the clearest counterexample to accepting topology as Fun. The repeated named-wall, organ-slot and whole-body joins often execute a plan already supplied by the names or goal.

Concrete physiological inferences fare better: muscle calcium activation/reset, fibroblast expression followed by matrix production, fibrin formation and lymphatic return. Even the best local average, immune memory's 3.3/8, includes some motivated attempts frustrated by prerequisites. Preserve those grades but do not copy the friction. Plausible direct actions are rejected in blood, absorption, colon, skin, airway, immune, ear and clock passages; any repair must preserve the useful biological relationship while accepting sensible orders.

**First candidate experiments:** a blind comparison chose permeability-based opening A over membrane-gradient C and intracellular-cargo B; the reviewer specifically distinguished actual permeability experiments from arbitrary order among transport fittings. Shorten respiratory preparation, retain usable glycolytic ATP and keep glucose entry with respiration. A fresh neural author is developing shared-input circuit experiments followed by anatomical routes. A separate fresh author is developing heart/lung construction. Neither has seen old scores or the rejected chapters.

A small vessel-name experiment keeps the six-action graph but names the supplied walls by actual materials and geometry, requiring the player to connect those properties with pressure, exchange and return. This is a test of information guiding the choice, not a promise that obscurer names make better play. A regulation experiment offers the same controller/effector across contrasting conditions. Independent manuscript review judged its first version still too much a pair of announced plans and highlighted redundant body attachments; revise those before testing. All candidates remain in disposable scratch and are not promoted merely because they are newer. New fast and detailed cohorts will use the identical audience guidance and unchanged rubrics.

<a id="opening-linearity-review"></a>

## Opening linearity: user rejection and evaluation audit — 13 November 2026

The user played the first four chapters and found their repeated A + B → C, C + D → E construction pattern unfun. They pointed to Creation's beginning and questioned why the playtests had not penalized the absence of variation. This is direct player evidence that the current opening has not met the interaction goal, despite its explanatory payoffs. No manuscript or engine changes are made in this audit.

A read-only check used the existing `parseStory`, `startAdventure` and `choose` functions with `mode: 'story'` and `playStyle: 'fast'`, excluding look commands and following the first available combination. Anatomy exposes one action before each of its first 17 moves. Creation's first chapter exposes three initially and four after creating the Void; the inspected eight-action route has option counts 3, 4, 3, 3, 3, 3, 2, 1. Creation's counts describe that selected route, not an exhaustive route census or proof of fun. Anatomy's sole-option progression leaves no alternative successful combination route through those four chapters.

```text
Chapter                         Moves  Valid actions at each move  Each of 3 graders' fast diagnostic
1 Inside and outside            4      1, 1, 1, 1                  0/4
2 The powerhouse of the cell     4      1, 1, 1, 1                  0/4
3 One becomes two               4      1, 1, 1, 1                  0/4
4 The same DNA, different work   5      1, 1, 1, 1, 1               0/5
```

The applicable retained records are [chapter 1](playtests/2026.11.12_19.46.48/chapter1_report.md), [chapter 2](playtests/2026.11.12_20.00.09/chapter2_report.md), [chapter 3](playtests/2026.11.12_20.00.09/chapter3_report.md) and [chapter 4](playtests/2026.11.12_20.07.44/chapter4_report.md). Their relevant local passage content remains current after the title/path rename. All three players in each condition chose option 1 at every action. These are separate chapter cohorts, not three newly observed continuous 17-action playthroughs.

The evidence already identified the problem. Independent graders repeatedly called the sequence single-option, repetitive and weak in decision-making, with zero meaningful actions throughout. Worthwhile is the union of meaningful decisions and separately evidenced story/educational payoff, so a result can receive worthwhile credit even when choosing its combination requires no substantive thought. The chapter syntheses retained that zero alongside high worthwhile counts. Recent `--fast` work did not measure Fun; older detailed scores describe earlier versions and cannot validate this opening. The authoring failure was treating improvements in explanatory payoff and brevity as sufficient progress while leaving the repeated structural criticism unresolved. The records were not missing this signal.

Creation supplies a concrete comparison. Air, Earth and Fire can each interact with the initial waters for different results. Opening the Void supplies further constructions: Earth makes the world and Fire makes the Sun. The Sun/world and asteroid/world branches then meet in different relationships. Players can choose a legitimate focus and discover new uses for ingredients. This is more than making a prescribed chain harder to infer.

**Adopted design requirement:** preserve the cellular/physiological subject and clear guidance, while rebuilding the opening around independent subassemblies, ingredient reuse and meaningful convergence. A short forced sequence can be worthwhile; seventeen compulsory moves cannot be defended as varied construction. Do not hide compatibility or add arbitrary recipes to manufacture difficulty. Repeated player agreement alone is not a universal failure test: independent inference can converge on one solution. Here the one-option graph, actual comments and the user's experience agree that the opening lacks the desired agency.

**Retest decision:** the user clarified that detailed retests can be skipped when recent changes are not expected to materially affect Fun. The opening's dependency, ordering, chapter and information changes did carry that risk, so its earlier detailed evidence was insufficient and focused retesting was warranted. The failure was making that judgment incorrectly and leaving adverse diagnostics unresolved; a rule requiring detailed tests after every edit would overshoot the lesson. The [evaluation guide](../../docs/fun-and-evaluation.md#when-to-repeat-detailed-tests) owns the adopted material-impact rule.

**Next evaluation:** inspect the available productive actions and concrete branch/convergence patterns before commissioning more players. This opening redesign warrants fresh detailed-mode trials for Fun, alongside worthwhile and hook, with normal compatibility information. Compare what players could legitimately do and why they chose it; differing guesses or inspections alone do not demonstrate successful redesign. Keep these judgments separate from recipe correctness and educational value. This audit adds no new AI player scores and does not revise or discard any original report.

<a id="cell-prelude-revision"></a>

## Cell prelude: division and differentiation — 12 November 2026

The user approved a four-part opening: a cellular inside; glucose, mitochondria and ATP together; cell division; and differentiation as the bridge into tissues. The earlier word-only opening adjustment did not address these structural opportunities. The proposed “powerhouse of the cell” title uses recognition as an invitation; its value must still come from the work shown. The active brief now permits selected division/differentiation processes while keeping the whole game an explanatory anatomy/physiology model rather than a complete developmental timeline.

**Prospective hypotheses:** (1) separating selective-boundary construction from the whole fuel-to-ATP-to-work sequence will make both opening chapters more coherent; (2) daughter-cell populations will give later tissue quantities a meaningful origin; (3) a visible gene-expression/protein/function example will make differentiation more than a signal plus a renamed cell; (4) specific bone, muscle and neural branches can reuse the shared foundation without implying that all tissues descend from the same committed precursor; (5) the ending can apply renewal with fewer repeated preparatory moves once division has already been taught. Falsifiers include players valuing only the last result, describing intermediate states as label changes, missing the DNA-copy/expression distinction, or understanding no functional consequence of differentiation. These are hypotheses, not improvement claims.

A fresh author received the proposed scope and current requirements without the old manuscript or scores. A separate biology reviewer prepared primary-source grounding before seeing the draft. Selected tissue interfaces and the complete integration receive review before freezing the playtest version. The planned diagnostic uses three fresh Terra Medium players, independent graders and a fresh chapter synthesis per tested chapter in `--fast`, with the previous grades 10–12 guidance unchanged. Fun is not measured. Early chapter counterparts and prior scores provide context, not a controlled experiment on one isolated change. The stem-cell/tissue model and extra chapters change earlier history.

The first four chapters replace the earlier two, so this working version temporarily exceeds the 20–30-chapter aspiration; do not compress unrelated later passages solely to conceal the change. No graphics, screenshot tests, engine changes or publication.

### First reviewed candidate and fast observations

The independent arc and biology reviews exposed a missing payoff for the fibroblast-made matrix, a misleading RNA-to-protein phrase, and the need to identify the living stem-cell specimen visibly. Corrections connect that matrix to muscle fascicles, show amino acids becoming protein under an RNA instruction, and distinguish the selected lineage sequence from the subsequent mature-bone cutaway. A separate synaptic-receptor installation was folded into the existing neuron's machinery. Bounded follow-up reviews found no remaining blocking continuity or biology issue. They do not establish student comprehension.

The [initial fast campaign](playtests/2026.11.12_19.46.48/report.md) tested source `bd37c575a88bf9545b224a5081b676acef46e1dde0a6b5cd6c3f26ed24369f7b`: 32 chapters, 200 required actions and 227 rules. Three fresh Terra Medium players, independent graders and a fresh synthesis reviewer per chapter completed 24 chapter trials covering the first seven chapters and renewal. All 156 actions and original response arrays verified. Actual model selection, fresh request histories, matching frozen source/runtime/guidance, native replay and report arithmetic were checked. Fast mode does not measure Fun; its separate decision-density diagnostics were all zero. H/M/L below means high/medium/low hook.

```text
Chapter                                  Fun           Worthwhile  Hook
1  Inside and outside                    not measured  4/4         3M
2  The powerhouse of the cell             not measured  4/6         3M
3  One becomes two                       not measured  3/4         1L/1M/1H
4  The same DNA, different work           not measured  5.3/7       2M/1H
5  A framework that bends                 not measured  4.3/7       2M/1H
6  Muscle fibres, muscles and tendons     not measured  8.3/10      3M
7  Cells that communicate                not measured  8/9         2M/1H
32 Still being made                      not measured  3/5         3M
```

The first chapter's enclosure action is less engaging than its bilayer or selective passage, but every player still finds value in all four results. Keep it. In chapter 2, all three leave the separate transporter and mitochondrial installations uncredited; oxygen's role and ATP-powered pumping carry the strongest value. In chapter 3, growth is the sole uncredited action: the output enlarges the cell without showing what the supplied materials built. In chapter 4, the early lineage transitions are weaker than the concrete gene-expression, secretion and collagen-processing sequence. Unexplained lineage terminology and the signal-to-gene connection matter more than the mere absence of choices in a fast menu.

### Targeted retest hypotheses, recorded before testing

- **Chapter 2:** bundle the two uncredited installations into the living specimen's visible supporting anatomy. Four actions retain glucose breakdown, oxygen's distinct role, ATP renewal and ion pumping. A small inner-membrane inset will show how a hydrogen-ion gradient drives ATP formation, without extra operands, numerical yields or named enzyme chains. Hypothesis: the same valued sequence will work with fewer preparations and a better explained energy connection. Losing any of the four payoffs or making the mechanism harder to follow falsifies the intended improvement. A better fraction caused only by removing two actions is efficiency, not increased worthwhile content.
- **Chapter 3:** retain four actions, but show amino acids becoming proteins, phospholipids enlarging membranes and existing organelles multiplying over time. Hypothesis: the growth result will now explain the materials' contribution instead of merely becoming larger. An unchanged worthwhile count with the same complaint would reject that claim.
- **Chapter 4:** retain seven actions and the lineage restriction needed by later branches. Use plain names alongside optional lineage labels, and show signals at receptors, internal relays and altered gene activity before the cells acquire their specialized output. Hypothesis: these pictures make differentiation causally legible without extending the popups. Watch for unchanged setup criticism, a false single-signal destiny implication, or poorer hook despite more detail.

Retest chapters 2–4 with fresh participants and exactly the same grades 10–12 audience guidance. Earlier and later chapter results remain evidence for their tested version and history; do not pool selected scores into a newly measured whole-story result.

### First retest: an improved energy chapter, a failed lineage clarification

The [first retest](playtests/2026.11.12_20.00.09/report.md) used source `70767d21fcfcb53fedca3533b0b63ff045644730fbac605e179f9b71f89f9dbd`: 198 required actions and 225 rules. All nine players and their independent grades completed; 45 actions, original responses, fresh actual Terra Medium model requests, native replay and report assembly verified. The audience guidance was unchanged. Both reviewers checked the revised scientific and continuity relationships before testing.

```text
Chapter                         Before Fun    After Fun     Before W  After W  Before H   After H
2 The powerhouse of the cell    not measured  not measured  4/6       4/4      3M         1M/2H
3 One becomes two              not measured  not measured  3/4       3.3/4    1L/1M/1H   2M/1H
4 The same DNA, different work  not measured  not measured  5.3/7     5/7      2M/1H      1M/2H
```

**Keep chapter 2.** The worthwhile numerator stayed at four while two preparations disappeared. This is greater efficiency, not two new worthwhile actions. There is additional qualitative support: every player singled out the new electron-flow → hydrogen-ion movement → ATP panel as the strongest construction, and two reported a high hook. The oxygen-to-energy connection still feels briefly compressed to two players; the next result addresses one player's gap. Curriculum coverage was checked directly against AP 3.5.A.3(ii–iii), printed pp. 76–77; the inset teaches this mechanism rather than assuming it.

**Keep chapter 3's clearer material picture, with modest evidence.** One additional player credits growth, particularly the mitochondrial detail; the other two still leave it uncredited. All three call it least engaging. This is not a strong resolution of the growth-step weakness. DNA segregation and cytokinesis remain valued, while the ring's detailed mechanism is still abbreviated. These small independent samples do not establish a reliable population-level hook increase.

**Do not promote chapter 4's clarification as a success.** Worthwhile fell by 0.3/7 despite one more high hook. Both signalling preparations remain uncredited for all three players: the signals, lineage names and relays are still schematic before a concrete product appears. More explanatory drawing did not make the preparations earn their actions.

### Second retest hypothesis: differentiation through a working product

Fold the two lineage preparations and separate RNA-building-block supply into one operation: daughter stem-cell population + a changing fibroblast-development environment → developing fibroblasts with collagen messenger RNA. Preserve the selected mesoderm/fibroblast sequence over time, unchanged DNA, broader changed gene activity and a particular RNA message leaving the nucleus. The next amino-acid action still distinguishes an instruction from protein material. Subsequent secretion, collagen processing and matrix assembly stay intact. Five actions replace seven.

The shared mesoderm tile disappears; bone and muscle instead use distinct subsets of the original daughter population, with their intermediate lineages visible in their own pictures. Fibroblasts do not turn into bone cells, muscle or neurons. The independent biology and arc reviewers found this bounded compression defensible; they specifically required the lineage in the resulting cell picture and broader gene-activity changes, not just a collagen label.

**Prediction:** the first differentiation action will now have a valued functional consequence, retaining the copying-versus-expression distinction while shortening the preparation. A higher fraction alone cannot demonstrate this. Loss of the RNA/protein distinction, continued dismissal of the first action, or lower worthwhile numerator/hook is contrary evidence. Retest chapters 4–6 with fresh participants and unchanged guidance because the supply relationship changes in both downstream branches.

### Second retest: shorter differentiation, contrary muscle result

The [second retest](playtests/2026.11.12_20.07.44/report.md) used source `da640201722b96654db7822e499fcb3000ffcc0601cef4ee4ccbc0dcdee1941e`: 196 required actions and 223 rules. All nine players completed; 66 actions and the original responses, replay, independent grades, syntheses and combined report verified. Guidance and actual Terra Medium selection were unchanged.

```text
Chapter                               Comparator    Before Fun    After Fun     Before W  After W  Before H  After H
4 The same DNA, different work        first retest  not measured  not measured  5/7       5/5      1M/2H     1M/2H
5 A framework that bends             initial run   not measured  not measured  4.3/7     6.7/7    2M/1H     2M/1H
6 Muscle fibres, muscles and tendons  initial run   not measured  not measured  8.3/10    6/10     3M        2M/1H
```

**Adopt the shorter differentiation sequence.** Every player now values the combined signal → changed gene activity → RNA result. The worthwhile numerator remains five compared with the first retest; two uncredited preparations disappeared. Against the initial seven-action candidate's 5.3/7, the numerator is slightly lower because bundling can combine previously separate credits. This is evidence for an efficient, functional introduction to differentiation, not more independently valued content. RNA instructions and protein material remain distinguishable. Procollagen terminology and the gel's mechanical contribution still need care.

**Bone's measured increase is not wholly attributable to the edit.** New players value both the clarified lineage and the largely unchanged collagen/mineral, nutrient-supply and joint results. Different players and synthesis judgments can account for part of that change. Mesodermal/mesenchymal labels remain unfamiliar; cartilage remains thin for one player.

**Muscle is a contrary result, not an improvement.** All three records receive 6/10. The first three actions and repeated triceps construction are uncredited; contraction, relaxation, fascicle organization and opposing attachments retain value. Direct comparison of original player responses finds very similar reactions to fusion and filament installation in both conditions: clear retained nuclei, covered grip sites and readiness for a signal. Those two result pictures and recipes did not change, while the precursor source/description and preceding history did. The initial 8.3/10 versus new 6/10 therefore does not isolate a causal regression, but the loss must remain visible.

**Prospective fixed-condition replication:** run chapter 6 once more with the exact same `da640201…` manuscript, guidance and protocol, using fresh players, independent graders and a fresh synthesizer. Do not select the higher score or overwrite either result. Repeated low credit would strengthen the case that the new passage has a recurring weakness; a divergent result would expose instability in these small model assessments. No manuscript change is made for this diagnostic.

The [unchanged-condition replication](playtests/2026.11.12_20.14.41/report.md) again gives every player **6/10 worthwhile**, now with three medium hooks. All 30 actions, original responses, grading and assembly verified. Both syntheses leave the same four actions uncredited: differentiation, fusion, filament installation and repeated triceps construction. This supports treating the early preparations as a recurring weakness of the current passage; it does not explain why the first source received more generous credit for similar reactions. Both results remain, alongside the initial 8.3/10.

A fresh muscle author now receives only the textbook scope, audience and incoming/outgoing anatomical relationships, without the manuscript, study log, earlier drafts, criticism or scores. The task is to propose a compact lineage-to-muscle construction with equal treatment of biceps and triceps and a concrete pull/relaxation mechanism. The existing contraction, relaxation and opposing-attachment payoffs remain evidence to protect when selecting a candidate. A new draft still needs factual review and fresh testing; authorship alone is not acceptance.

### Muscle revision hypothesis: a different kind of cell, then a distinct organ

The fresh author's eight-action proposal emphasizes one long multinucleated cell, gene activity producing muscle/fusion proteins, fusion and maturation together, and the same tissue forming two anatomically distinct muscles. Its proposal also bundles contraction and relaxation into one calcium-control operation. The integrated candidate keeps the already valued contraction and relaxation as separate results, and keeps the earlier matrix's direct contribution to fascicle organization. Nine actions replace ten.

The first result now shows a concrete alternative to fibroblast secretion: the same inherited DNA supports actin, myosin and fusion proteins retained in muscle precursors. Fusion and maturation produce a striated fibre, with the membranes joining and nuclei retained shown in the result rather than fully previewed in the starting protein tile. The separate filament installation disappears. A brief wonder states that the whole long fibre is one cell. The triceps result replaces its duplicate biceps force-path inset with distinct anatomy: one head crosses shoulder and elbow while two cross only the elbow. Both muscles still use the same construction scale.

**Prediction before testing:** gene-product differences and one-cell/many-nuclei fusion will earn a more concrete early payoff; the triceps construction will reveal something not already shown by biceps. Preserve the calcium/sliding, ATP-supported relaxation, matrix organization and opposing-attachment payoffs. Compare the next fresh chapter-6 trial with BOTH 6/10 repeats and the original 8.3/10; dropping a preparation must not be called an extra worthwhile action. Failure to recover those earlier payoffs or continued dismissal of the revised results is contrary evidence. This candidate receives a fresh factual/continuity review before testing.

### Final muscle result and retained revision

The fresh factual/continuity reviewer received only the current manuscript and standing requirements, without prior drafts, proposals, scores or criticism. They checked the selected human myogenic lineage, membrane fusion and maturation, ATP/calcium distinctions, matrix hierarchy, actual biceps/triceps origins and insertions, and the outgoing nervous/circulatory connection. No essential correction was required. A later opportunity remains: chapter 14 could explicitly connect neuromuscular-junction activation back to the muscle's internal calcium release. This is a bounded explanatory omission, not an extra required assembly or a claim that the current interface is broken.

The [final focused trial](playtests/2026.11.12_20.41.58/report.md) tests retained source `03d80f99ba72500e330c508abe67af86e9371936c2dda2fd50e2c1fbf8e296bc`. Three fresh players each completed nine actions; native replay, original responses, independent grading, synthesis and assembly verified. **Adopt the nine-action muscle chapter.** The new first result's contrast with collagen-exporting fibroblasts, the fusion result's one-cell outline and the distinct triceps origins now earn credit. Sliding without filament shortening, ATP-supported relaxation, matrix organization and opposing attachments retain their value. This recovers the loss and improves the credited count relative to the initial sample while removing one action; it is not merely a higher fraction from a smaller denominator. Hook remains mixed medium/high, not uniformly strong. All three still find building the two muscles somewhat repetitive, and one calls their initial order arbitrary. Equal constituent treatment remains an explicit user requirement; hiding normal compatibility or supplying one whole muscle would not answer it.

### Final verification, findings and limits

The retained manuscript has **32 chapters, 195 required combinations and 222 rules**, with **zero lint errors and 10 reviewed mixed-fate warnings**. The full logical route completes all 32 chapters. Those warnings are the existing compound fitting/integration and immune-cell/bacterial uses, not new cell-lineage conflicts. Initial/peak objects for chapters 1–7 are **5, 5, 5, 6, 8, 9, 10**; the full-story maximum is 11. The curriculum audit's 32 titles and current counts match the manuscript. Local project links and whitespace were checked. No source edit follows the final tested hash above.

Across this pass, five retained fast campaigns contain **48 completed chapter trials, 324 actions, 48 independent grades and 16 chapter syntheses**, with 372 original player replies and 436 distinct provider threads. Every participant used actual `gpt-6.1-terra` at medium effort. Frozen source/runtime/guidance, fresh assignments, native replay, original payloads, per-action counts and exact combined-report assembly verified, with no tool events, retries or protocol failures. The requested arrays and reports comprise 69 files; disposable author/reviewer packets, orchestration, runtime snapshots and logs were removed after retaining these outcomes.

The strongest supported revision pattern is **give a construction its functional consequence before asking for another preparation**. Respiration retains four valued outcomes with fewer installations; differentiation reaches an RNA instruction instead of two precursor labels; muscle fusion now reveals an unusual kind of cell and its organized machinery. Merely adding explanatory detail did not rescue the two lineage steps. Growth remains the opening's least engaging action, with only a small 3/4 → 3.3/4 change. Later nerve-to-calcium continuity and some procollagen/matrix terminology remain opportunities for clearer teaching.

These are small, independent chapter trials using planned-picture text. They do not measure Fun, establish student learning or human enjoyment, or test continuous attachment through the full story. All fast decision-density diagnostics were zero. The latest local passage evidence is chapter 1 from the initial run; 2–3 from the first retest; 4–5 from the second retest; 6 from the final trial; 7 and 32 from the initial run. Earlier history differs across these conditions, so this selection must not be pooled into a newly measured final-game score. The other chapters retain their previous material and earlier evidence. The two additional bridge chapters temporarily exceed the 20–30-chapter aspiration while preserving later passages. No graphics, screenshot tests, engine changes, publication or unrelated commits were performed.

## Anatomy and physiology textbook revision — 12 November 2026

The user found an inconsistent construction scale in chapter 4: biceps was built from cells and tissue while triceps arrived whole. They also rejected the recurring cup exercise and asked to treat Undissection as an anatomy and physiology textbook, developing the nervous system, blood and heart for their own interest before connecting them to the arm. This is direct user evidence; the previous AI muscle grades do not answer that criticism.

The revision keeps one manuscript and 30 chapters. Both muscles now use the same fascicle-level supply and their distinct sheaths/tendons. A fresh author, given the subject and rough scope without the old nervous chapter or scores, proposed two chapters: neuronal cells/circuits and regional CNS/PNS anatomy. The later plasticity example moves into neural tissue, freeing its chapter slot. Blood, vessels, heart, gas exchange and ventilation follow; chapter 12 connects the nervous and cardiopulmonary systems to the already built arm and adds tissue-fluid return. Brain and spinal cord retain central locations. Ventilation no longer receives a second free-standing brainstem. The cup, weight and late bell encounter are removed.

**Design hypothesis:** constructing systems independently will make their later integration intelligible, and equal tissue-level construction will make the paired muscles feel consistent. Shared neural tissue represents region-appropriate populations; it is not one circuit multiplied into every organ. The intrinsic connections are microscopic sliding versus organ attachment, local neural integration versus long routes, information versus material transport, and blood supply versus lymph return. These are design reasons, not new measured score improvements.

**Independent review:** two fresh reviewers assessed the current manuscript without prior drafts, scores or each other's reports: one for anatomy/prerequisites/continuity, one for the arc and combination patterns. Both found the systems-first structure coherent. Their concrete findings changed the draft: neural tissue now exists before the separate plasticity demonstration; the four central-region pictures show distinct functions; chemical synapse pictures show transmitter crossing the gap and define the firing threshold; capillary support and endothelial cells are distinct constituents; large airways and small bronchioles show different walls and airflow resistance. Gas diffusion is distinguished from ventilation pressure, vestibular pictures show hair-bundle displacement and acceleration, and the clock's light-sensitive ganglion cells connect to the existing hypothalamus. The same reviewers checked those corrections as follow-through, not as new blind rounds. Minor final wording locates pericytes within basement membrane and glosses membrane voltage.

**Limits and follow-up:** no outstanding must-fix finding remained in those reviews. The four similar regional neural constructions remain a predicted repetition risk even with distinct pictures; this is not observed student boredom. Heart conducting-tissue fit order is an optional later improvement, not part of this revision. New neural vocabulary still needs evaluation with the intended audience. This turn adds no scored AI fast/detailed campaign: earlier grades cannot be assigned to these new chapters, and no improvement in Fun, hook, worthwhile or student learning is claimed.

**Verification:** `npm run lint -- projects/undissection/undissection.md` reports **zero errors and 10 reviewed mixed-fate warnings**. They describe compound owners retained during fitting then integrated into larger objects, and the established immune-cell/bacterial uses. `npm run analyze -- --json --details projects/undissection/undissection.md` completes **all 30 chapters in 187 combinations**, from **214 rules**. The first two chapters retain five initial/peak objects; chapter 4 now has nine, neural tissue nine, nervous-system anatomy seven, and integration seven. The whole-story maximum remains eleven. Final source SHA-256: `842a120590af46a7a9d8c8a0c3e28843117dfa30f07c6310e2810265a1863df7`. Chapter counts, current audit order and whitespace were checked. Disposable author/reviewer packets and analysis output were removed after retaining these findings. No graphics, browser/screenshot tests, engine changes or publication.

**Opening follow-up:** the user subsequently quoted the older “An arm's muscles contain living cells” introduction. That wording was absent from both the revised file and the local HTTP response; the browser's exact retained state was not inspected. The current introduction still named a muscle cell, so it now starts simply with the cell boundary and sugar entry. The specific muscle-cell cutaway remains in the illustration description for continuity with later anatomy. Local file and server response match byte for byte; lint remains zero errors and 10 warnings. This is prose only, with no recipe changes or new scored trial. Source SHA-256 after this edit: `1f5d3dde37418bf671c559c08070b4522c8889683418f885b15f1ed45b151a75`. The graphical loader uses a saved editor draft before fetching the file, and Restart story resets gameplay without fetching new manuscript text; either retained page content or a local draft could explain the older wording, but neither was established as the user's actual cause.

## Curriculum audit and grades 10–12 revision — 12 November 2026

The user enjoyed later chapters but found the opening difficult. They changed the intended audience to grades 10–12 and requested official honours/AP curriculum research, a complete story audit, revisions and one whole-story `--fast` campaign. [curriculum.md](curriculum.md) owns the sources and chapter audit; the design brief now makes its first-two-chapter constraint explicit. Published curriculum coverage is guidance rather than a rigid pass/fail bar. Later extensions can earn their place through visible teaching.

The research downloaded the current AP framework and corrections, two district Honors Biology curricula and an Ontario Grades 11–12 comparison. AP does cover pumps, electrochemical gradients, gene expression and trafficking; the old first chapter was chiefly overloaded integration, not simply college-only vocabulary. The detailed muscle crossbridge/calcium cycle is not required AP core. Ontario's senior curriculum explicitly supports organ-system physiology, preventing absence from the AP topic list from becoming a blanket reason to remove the later chapters.

### Prospective changes and falsifiers

- **Opening:** membrane formation, a selective boundary and facilitated glucose entry become four actions with five starting items and no process hotspots. The picture before insertion now shows very slow glucose crossing, so the transporter has a reason. Hypothesis: the purpose of the first construction can be followed from the visible changes. A menu of obvious pairs without a valued result would still be weak.
- **Second chapter:** four actions connect fuel/oxygen, ATP turnover and ATP-driven transport. ATP is introduced in the opening, and ADP/phosphate is labelled by its role as ATP ingredients. Five starting items. This stays inside the shared curricular core; success alone will not establish comprehension.
- **Muscle:** the specialized cycle moves to chapter 4, where the movable joint gives it a purpose. Supplied filament units avoid an extra assembly step. The whole chapter now has nine required actions and ten initial objects, an explicit pacing risk to examine. Comparisons must not credit extra actions merely for increasing the worthwhile numerator.
- **Gene expression:** repair now copies a collagen instruction before using amino acids to make support. Existing cell machinery processes and exports the protein. This preserves the RNA/protein relationship at a point where a visible gap explains why the cell needs a product; it adds one action to repair. Falsifier: the new instruction copy feels like another preparation or the picture compresses too much to follow.
- **Later support:** the water chapter depicts the existing osmotic driving condition and reduced ADH output as concentration approaches its usual range. Immune memory uses its established bacterial-feature language; the red-cell signal is named by its kidney/marrow job. Other later chapters are retained on the audit's evidence that their added relationships are taught in the game.

Two fresh informed reviewers received only the current manuscript, curriculum and standing requirements, without old drafts or scores. Their corrections made the muscle-cell identity visible from chapter 1, introduced ATP before requiring it, gave the membrane its missing selective-barrier picture, and separated resting myosin heads from the calcium-enabled attachment/release cycle. The opening reviewer judged the curricular scope and inferred working sets suitable; this is not pupil comprehension evidence. The mechanism reviewer found the water, collagen, immune and EPO representations suitable at the selected explanatory level.

The final candidate has **30 chapters, 182 required actions and 211 total rules**, with zero lint errors and 11 reviewed mixed-fate warnings. The first two chapters each start and peak at five objects along the analyzer route. Chapter 4 starts/peaks at ten; the existing digestion chapter remains the overall maximum of eleven. The actual diff was checked: chapters 1, 2, 4, 17, 22, 24 and 30 change player-facing material; chapter 3 changes only a carry-over author note. The proposed optional chapter-28 label was not applied because its actual wording already describes the local response; no change is claimed there. Earlier source SHA-256: `fd54da734f7137bc7556e5825ff00e4175ed8666a7177100095e1a3c38d89292`. Candidate: `99300b0bfd0ab903fed3dc07a463ec15d234b7b5e06d5dd6dcba057adcfa0adc`.

### Whole-story fast playtest

Completed [whole-story fast campaign](playtests/2026.11.12_08.12.50/report.md): **90 chapter playthroughs, 90 independent grades and 30 chapter syntheses**. There are 30 independent chapter conditions, three fresh Terra Medium players per chapter, a fresh Terra Medium grader per record and a fresh Terra Medium synthesizer per chapter. The native engine supplies actual checkpoint history and ordinary compatible-action menus. Fun is **not measured** in this mode. Audience guidance changes from grades 8–10 to grades 10–12, so comparisons with earlier records cannot isolate the manuscript revision from the audience change. Source, runtime, rubric and guidance remain frozen during play; no screenshots or engine changes.

The initial launcher rejected all 90 first responses because its infrastructure classified a CLI experimental-feature warning as tool use. Every CLI response was complete and each native session had zero accepted actions. The warning classification was corrected; all original responses were validated and recovered unchanged, rather than requesting replacement answers. An offline request capture confirmed `gpt-6.1-terra`, medium reasoning and no tools. Only adapter/CLI helper hashes changed, recorded against the original manifest; story, runtime, rubrics and audience guidance remained fixed. Genuine fatal errors and tool calls still fail validation.

Exact audience guidance and model/run metadata are retained in the individual chapter reports; a controlled follow-up should reuse that guidance unchanged. The table reports the final synthesis for each chapter of the same frozen manuscript. Worthwhile is mean worthwhile actions / mean total actions across three players, including normal fast-mode preparations. H/M/L means high/medium/low hook; a lone letter means all three agreed. No overall game score is inferred from these independent chapter conditions.

Some synthesis responses needed structural corrections to preserve the exact supplied grade JSON or timing metadata. Chapters 17 and 30 exhausted format retries because displayed elapsed times were rounded; an organizer block then restored the immutable packet's exact values. Removing that insertion reproduces each original third response exactly. All original grader JSON, chapter-reviewer judgments, structured action classifications and counts were preserved. No extra player or independent grader was requested to replace a poor result.

### What this campaign supports

- **A simpler entry is supported; a stronger hook is not established.** Chapter 1 has 2.3/4 worthwhile actions and 1 high/2 medium hook judgments; chapter 2 has 3/4 and three medium judgments. Players understood the purpose of the selective route and valued the ATP-driven pump. Transporter fitting and adding oxygen were weak as separate steps. Completing a shorter route does not prove student comprehension, and a better worthwhile fraction after removing actions would not by itself prove improvement. The audience change prevents a controlled comparison with older campaigns.
- **Moving the muscle cycle has useful support.** Chapter 4 has 8/9 worthwhile actions and three high hook judgments. All three players singled out the calcium-enabled sliding/myosin cycle; gathering other fibres into a bundle was routine. This supports the mechanism's place in an arm chapter, not a general claim that longer chapters improve engagement. Its nine actions and ten starting objects still need human pacing evidence.
- **Relocating gene expression has useful support.** Chapter 24 has 6/9 worthwhile actions and 1 high/2 medium hook judgments. All three players singled out the connection from collagen instructions through RNA/protein production to support in a wound. This supports giving familiar molecular biology a visible bodily consequence. It does not establish a controlled score improvement over the older repair chapter, and the surface fitting remains predictable after its sheet has already spanned the gap.
- **The water clarification is noticed, but the chapter remains weak overall.** Chapter 17 has 2.3/6 worthwhile actions and three medium hook judgments. All three chapter-17 players explicitly valued the extra channels and concentration difference, and all three identified reduced ADH signals as concentration approached the usual range. This is the intended visible explanation. They also found the separate hormone-route construction/attachment repetitive; clarification does not solve that structural weakness.
- **Later curriculum suitability does not establish strong play.** The vessel chapter gives distinct parts distinct observable jobs (6/6 worthwhile); the microbial-habitat chapter is short and valued throughout (3/3). In contrast, heart assembly has 2/8 worthwhile and liver 2.3/6. Several fittings repeat an explanation already delivered or provide a thin intermediate state. Gas exchange has a valued thin-barrier mechanism, but players flag the elastic-support and pressure-gradient explanations. These remain editorial findings, not reasons to exclude the subjects from secondary biology.

The retained revision meets the chosen opening-scope constraint and preserves the later physiological material. It is **not an across-the-board engagement improvement**. Most hooks are medium; skin has three low judgments, and thermal regulation has two low judgments. Repeated organ/route fittings, thin intermediate states and explanations deferred until a final assembled view remain weak patterns. The new audience guidance, changed earlier history and model variation prevent attributing differences from older scores to any single cause. A controlled comparison would require the same grades 10–12 guidance for both manuscript versions; this campaign does not supply one.

The strongest retained patterns are distinct observable contributions (muscle and vessel mechanisms, optical components), a small set of substantial relationships (the microbial habitat), and familiar biology serving a concrete new outcome (gene expression in repair). These are supported editorial directions, not evidence of pupil learning or human enjoyment. The first two chapters are a simpler entry with modest measured hook, not a proven solution to onboarding.

### Verification and retained files

Full verification passed for **30 chapters, 90/90 completed players, 90 independent grades, 30 syntheses and 550 scored actions**. Native `evaluate-cli --verify-only` replay and grading-packet checks matched each accepted raw response and final report to its retained array. The action counts, original independent judgments, worthwhile union and hook summaries validated; native `run-ai report` reassembly reproduced the combined report exactly. There are **121 retained artifacts**: 90 player arrays, 30 chapter reports and one combined report. No looks were recorded. CLI output and structured checks were used; no screenshots, graphics or engine changes were made.

After verification, one surplus final newline was removed. Tested source: `99300b0bfd0ab903fed3dc07a463ec15d234b7b5e06d5dd6dcba057adcfa0adc`. Retained source: `6abf04147adcf3ceb1e9f91d3031438ef9a35f32602b2d17e1fc37013e306a98`. The parsed gameplay is identical; the sole parser metadata difference is the last chapter's editor insertion position moving back by one character at EOF. Final lint has **zero errors and 11 reviewed mixed-fate warnings**, with 30 chapters and 211 rules; the analyzer route has 182 required actions. This cleanup does not create a new gameplay condition.

The [native report](playtests/2026.11.12_08.12.50/report.md) and original requested records remain in the project. The five requested curriculum PDFs and their verified manifest remain in ignored `sources/local/curricula/`. Disposable research extracts, raw reviews, source snapshots, CLI sessions and orchestration files under `runs/undissection-curriculum/` were removed after the concise findings were retained here. Earlier studies and their original audience/version identifiers are unchanged.

## Full-story development


Authorized 12 November 2026: approximately 200 required combinations in 20–30 chapters, with blind reviews, iterative fast playtesting and subsequent iterative detailed playtesting. The [design brief](../../stories/anatomy/design-brief.md) is the single source of intent and constraints. [Five-round findings](#detailed-round-5-observations-integration-audit-and-retained-ten-chapter-reference) are evidence to learn from, not a requirement to preserve weak chapters.

<a id="full-story-completed-pass-and-retained-draft"></a>

### Completed pass and retained draft

The [full draft](anatomy.md) has **30 chapters, 185 required combinations and 214 total rules**, including generated fittings and alternative orders. It begins below the cell scale and ends with the same body continuing to renew its tissues. The colon adds a distinct relationship with the body's microbial inhabitants. The retained gameplay is D4 with whitespace-only cleanup; final SHA-256 `fd54da734f7137bc7556e5825ff00e4175ed8666a7177100095e1a3c38d89292`.

Five fast campaigns and five detailed campaigns completed **363 chapter playthroughs, 363 independent grades and 121 chapter syntheses**, using Terra Medium throughout. Fast accounts for 183 players and does not measure Fun; detailed accounts for 180. Every baseline chapter was covered, followed by targeted revision/retest campaigns. These are independent chapter trials, not 363 whole-story plays or one continuous endurance test. Blind authorship/manuscript reviews informed hypotheses; the recorded play determined whether they worked.

Fast: [F0](playtests/2026.11.12_02.00.23/report.md) (90 players), [F1](playtests/2026.11.12_02.39.53/report.md) (36 players), [F2](playtests/2026.11.12_02.59.42/report.md) (27 players), [F3](playtests/2026.11.12_03.20.29/report.md) (18 players), [F4](playtests/2026.11.12_04.48.37/report.md) (12 players).

Detailed: [D0](playtests/2026.11.12_03.30.26/report.md) (90 players), [D1](playtests/2026.11.12_04.22.01/report.md) (27 players), [D2](playtests/2026.11.12_04.56.21/report.md) (36 players), [D3](playtests/2026.11.12_05.17.51/report.md) (18 players), [D4](playtests/2026.11.12_05.34.41/report.md) (9 players).

<a id="full-story-what-the-experiments-support"></a>

#### What the experiments support

- **Distinct consequences matter more than more labels.** ATP-supported release and relaxation, opposing muscle pulls, different vessel behavior, the heart's own blood supply, optical contributions and airway clearance provide concrete relationships. Several increased worth while Fun stayed flat or fell; the measures are not interchangeable.
- **Context can change what a fitting contributes.** Joining a local construction to the body can reveal its purpose, but can be repetitive when that purpose has already been fully shown. Both the shorter skin and parallel renewal ending lost value for this reason. The kidney-to-marrow connection links earlier organs to ongoing renewal.
- **Freedom of order is not sufficient engagement.** Keep real material, support and activation dependencies, and remove arbitrary placement requirements. Some earlier meaningful credit came from attempts rejected by those requirements. The corrected versions can score lower; those losses remain visible rather than being subtracted away.
- **Fresh authors are useful, not automatically better.** The new arm supplied a worthwhile load-response episode. Two fresh kidney treatments, the shorter skin, social greeting and parallel renewal ending did not earn replacement. An unplayed stair-climb draft repeated existing regulation and introduced false ordering; it was rejected editorially, with no invented test score.
- **Remaining weak patterns are specific.** Kidney construction still has zero measured Fun. Hearing, balance, daily timing and repeated cell/material preparations remain predictable. The long middle also risks repeated organ-to-body attachment. These are unresolved design weaknesses, not evidence that anatomy lacks enough material.

This is a substantial playable exploration, with enough distinct body relationships for the requested scale. It is not an evenly strong finished game. The illustration proxies do not establish student learning, recognition of eventual art, accessibility or human enjoyment. No artwork, screenshot checks, engine/UI changes or publication were performed.

### Experiment plan

1. Three fresh authors propose independent full-story arcs from the neutral scope and rough cellular-to-body premise, without earlier manuscripts or trial results. Fresh blind reviewers compare anonymized proposals for worthwhile relationships, hook, school-level coherence and repeated weak combination forms.
2. Develop the selected arc into a separate full manuscript. Give fresh chapter authors only their scope, rough purpose and interface constraints; independent manuscript/factual reviewers examine each frozen candidate. Verify the actual candidate diff against its recorded hypothesis before testing, then lint.
3. Establish a whole-story `--fast` baseline, then run at least two actual revision/retest cycles focused on observed weaknesses, with an unchanged comparison chapter where useful. Three fresh Terra Medium players, independent Terra Medium graders and a fresh Terra Medium synthesis for each chapter condition.
4. Freeze the supported fast candidate and establish whole-story `--detailed` coverage. Run at least two further revision/retest cycles driven by Fun, worthwhile, hook and action-level evidence. Replace stubborn passages with fresh authors rather than accumulating small edits indefinitely.
5. Retain supported changes, report failures and unresolved tradeoffs, verify final story lint and report arithmetic, and remove disposable packets/sessions. No graphics, screenshots, engine changes or publication.

The two-retest minimum at each mode is a starting workload, not a promise that every chapter improves or a reason to ignore a clear regression. Revision scope follows the evidence. Scores are separate; no composite weighting. Tables use W for Worthwhile and H/M/L for high/medium/low hook; for example, 1H/2M means one high and two medium judgments. Fun is meaningful decisions per recorded decision, not human enjoyment. AI text trials do not establish student comprehension, visual recognition or whole-game endurance.

### Neutral concept-author brief

Authors receive the game scope, target audience, existing-engine rules, text/illustration format, approximate length, and rough premise of constructing from below the cell scale toward a body that keeps functioning. They do not receive earlier Undissection manuscripts, rejected variants, score reports or preferred solutions. Concept assignments are original proposals, not blind evaluations of existing work. Reviewers will receive anonymized candidates without author identity or earlier outcomes.

### Blind concept comparison and working architecture

Three fresh Terra Medium authors independently proposed 24–25-chapter arcs without earlier drafts or results. Two fresh reviewers received anonymized proposals in different orders, with no preferred answer. Both ranked Reed first, Flint second and Cedar third. Reed supplied the ordinary-action through-line and distinct digestive routes; Cedar contributed explicit blood components and ventilation mechanics; Flint made the environmental limit clear: retaining water cannot replace drinking. Reviewers did not establish playable feasibility or enjoyment.

Adopted changes to that foundation: bring movement forward instead of spending ten chapters before the first step; introduce specialized epithelia within their actual organs rather than multiplying one generic sheet; distinguish digestion, absorption and routing; make each regulatory response oppose a specified change; replace repeated whole-body relabels with concrete relationships. The final eight-action allocation remains deliberately provisional and may shrink. Fresh authors receive only neutral scope, rough chapter purposes and input/output boundaries, not the failed predecessors.
