This is approximately the requested size; unnecessary actions will not be retained merely to approach 200. The body is not short of material.

### Integrated blind manuscript review and fast baseline

Three fresh Terra Medium reviewers independently examined chapters 1–11, 12–22 and 23–30 (the last also skimmed the whole arc). They received only the current manuscript, standing philosophy/authoring/specification, current brief and scientific references, with no earlier manuscripts, authorship or scores. All found no serious factual blocker. Shared concern: repeated detached assembly followed by whole-body attachment may become an inventory, and author notes cannot repair unclear player-facing causality.

Adopted before baseline: an opening promise of an ordinary reach; recognizable cell/fibre/arm continuity; explicit sodium channel/pump contrast; haemoglobin production inside developing cells; lymph drainage alongside tissue exchange; visible dendritic-cell travel and B-cell division; local synapse calcium gateway; wound capillary attachment; kidney-to-marrow signal; day/night melatonin contrast; visible swallowing/eating and gradual gut-to-blood exchange in the ending. Later platelet and red-precursor inputs now have distinct local names so they do not masquerade as earlier loose samples. Fixed two malformed chapter-fragment conventions and stale local-stub notes. No new art.

Held for measured testing: repeated blood/vessel fittings, separate digestion chemistry/organ assembly, repeated liver storage/release, detached kidney/ADH demonstrations and sensory-route attachment. The reviews suggest risks, not measured boredom; the first test establishes where changes are justified.

Fast baseline `udfull-f0` freezes SHA-256 `b79e0383d2309419ec5e195a93f500c51a7116d0595fab1c24a73c77f97acf89`. Organizer reviewed assembled source, actual initial diff and all 30 chapter hashes. Lint: 0 errors, 12 mixed-fate warnings reviewed as intentional compound-owner transitions and the antibody-producing population's secretion/incorporation roles. Three fresh players, independent graders and fresh syntheses per chapter; Terra Medium throughout, including synthesis as explicitly selected for this experiment. Exact audience guidance and hashes retained in reports. Planned illustration proxies remain untested as images. Whole-story coverage uses chapter checkpoints, not one person's 185-action endurance.

### Early baseline observations (recorded before completion)

The first fast chapter illustrates an interface distinction: 4.7/10 worthwhile, medium/high hook; both fixed-context placement actions were low-value, while RNA copying and the final functional transporter were valued. Those placements are explicit in fast mode but folded into detailed commands, so this does not by itself justify removing the protein mechanism. Chapter 2: 3/7 worthwhile, medium/high hook; ATP renewal and filament sliding were valued, while several preparations added little. These are initial observations, not the final selection of weak chapters or an adoption decision. Source remains frozen until full baseline completion.

#### F1 pre-registered hypothesis: conditional revelations

Observed in the actual chapter 8 record: the first heart result displayed both empty and fitted valve positions. Inspection found slot qualifiers embedded after an unqualified picture in a single wonder block. Under the existing specification, the condition must begin its own block (a repeated declaration of the same item); the embedded brackets were ordinary prose. The same authoring mistake affects chapters 3, 4, 5, 8, 12, 15, 17, 23 and 25. Lint correctly found no completion error: this is a presentation/meaning error in valid prose, not an engine defect. Earlier blind manuscript reviews did not catch its parsed effect.

Next test: preserve all recipe graphs, separate the intended conditional readings, and make base picture descriptions neutral about fittings and retain the retina already installed in the eye cups so a later overlay does not conflict with an always-applicable empty-state description. Retest all nine affected chapters, chapters 29–30 for the late presentation correction below, and unchanged chapter 2. Prediction: affected fitting results earn more worthwhile credit after their explanations arrive with the actual fitting; the unchanged comparison should not show the same consistent gain. Falsifier: no gain or merely moving the same valued moment without improving aggregate value; hook must also be inspected. This will not repair low-value structure by itself. Fast-only hotspot placement friction remains separately identified. Fresh joint and blood alternatives are prepared and independently reviewed but are deliberately held out of this syntax-focused trial.

#### Fresh replacement candidates prepared during the frozen baseline

A fresh joint author received only neutral game scope, a rough living-bone/movable-elbow purpose and the required output; no previous draft or scores. Three approaches led to a six-action same-bone framework, revised to avoid producing shaped bones from an unshaped tissue sample. A fresh reviewer found no anatomical blocker, flagged reading density and the need to keep future fibre carry mechanically verified. A fresh blood author likewise received only rough transport-medium scope and an output. Its first five-action inventory was independently criticized for asserting transport; a new carrier variant actually binds oxygen to haemoglobin in a stationary sample, bundles secondary cells and retains five actions. A different fresh reviewer found that model sound and requested school-level wording for oxygen release. A fresh regulation author received only the rough fed/between-meals question and existing-organ interfaces; its candidate reuses one pancreatic sensing-cell item under both conditions. Independent review prompted visible feedback closure and reduced popups from four to two.

These are candidate authorships, not blind score evaluations. Their independent reviewers saw only their assigned current fragment and neutral context. All candidates are held until the focused F1 correction test; none has yet earned adoption or a claimed score improvement.

The completed ending baseline revealed a second organizer integration error: the root's primitive-picture insertion matched three recipe lines as well as declarations. This attached a retinal-input picture to the clock result, a nerve-route picture to the pineal result, and an empty-cup picture to the filled cup. The ending's confusion is present in the actual record; it is not grader speculation. F1 also removes those three unintended readings in chapters 29–30. Recipe graphs remain unchanged. The baseline records remain valid evidence for the flawed source; they are not retrospectively relabeled as a test of the intended corrected presentation.

### F0 verified baseline and F1 launch

[F0 complete report](playtests/2026.11.12_02.00.23/report.md): all 90 chapter players completed, all 90 independent grades and 30 syntheses verified against raw accepted responses, replay, frozen source and rubric, model commands, action tables and assembled sections. 567 recorded decisions, 270 worthwhile; 14 menu-assisted meaningful classifications are diagnostic only and are **not Fun**. The strongest worthwhile counts were immune memory (7.3/9), renewal (5.7/6) and daily timing (3.7/4). Only the breach chapter had all-high hook; most chapters were medium, with low judgments in regulation, repair and ending. That is a mixed baseline, not a successful full-story experience claim.

### F2 proposed substantive hypotheses

Prepared against the corrected F1 manuscript, with actual adoption dependent on F1 outcomes. Two additional fresh blind reviewers received only the candidate manuscript, standing requirements and sources, focusing the changed sections; neither saw old drafts, criticism or scores. They found no serious factual blocker, supported the distinct reset/pull/valve/carrier/coronary relationships, and flagged repeated body snapshots and unresolved thin handoffs. Applied their specific corrections: biceps shown unattached until fitted, one-cell landmarks, visible hormone routes, explicit coronary continuity and parallel systemic beds, accurate incomplete-supply naming, and a clear relaxed-fibre result.

| Chapters | Hypothesis and concrete change | What would count against it |
|---|---|---|
| 2 | Distinguish ATP-supported release/reset from calcium exposing grip sites; make ATP-powered calcium recovery and relaxation an actual final construction. | A new action receives no worthwhile credit, or the reset seems like installing a pump to stop an ongoing signal. |
| 3 | A fresh author's same shaped bones persist through tissue and joint construction; material, supply, friction and side support are visibly distinct. | Another serial checklist with no gain over the corrected joint chapter. |
| 4–5 | A real muscle attachment creates the fitted arm compound; either opposing muscle can come first. The final vessel connection supplies a real route, eliminating generic hand/shoulder completion. Accurate arm naming carries into chapter 5; its graph is unchanged. | Removing a step only raises a fraction without improving the contribution of either muscle or supply route. |
| 6 | A fresh carrier encounter replaces the longer blood inventory: reversible oxygen binding is an actual action; secondary components are bundled. | Only a denominator gain, with no extra valued binding/release relationship or lower hook. |
| 7 | Distinct wall behavior and venous help from limb muscle replace generic pictures; remove one redundant branching preparation. | The repeated lining fittings still contribute little, or their functional contrasts remain imperceptible. |
| 8 | Individual valve fittings show distinct flow consequences; expose the coronary branch so the pump's own dependence on supply becomes a construction. | The added coronary action is just another fitting or the new information does not earn attention. |
| 19 | A fresh author reuses the same pancreatic sensing cells under different conditions; both tissue outcomes show feedback reducing the stimulus. Two popups replace four. | The preserved cell view is confusing, or the sequence still feels like advancing near-identical body labels with no increase in worth/hook. |

Proposed candidate: 183 required actions in 30 chapters, 187 total rules. Three fewer preparations and one shorter arm path are offset by two new physiological relationships; the exact change from 185 is −2 required actions. Unchanged high-worth chapter 21 is the comparison. Fast Fun remains unmeasured. These are prospective hypotheses and manuscript checks, not successful outcome claims.

### F1 observations and decision

[F1 report](playtests/2026.11.12_02.39.53/report.md). The correction helped some passages, not all: joint1.3→2/6, muscles2→3/6, heart2→3.7/7, digestion2.7→3.3/7, kidneys2.3→4/7, repair3→5/8. Nerves stayed 3/7 (hook gained one high), skin stayed 2.7/7. Vision fell 4.7→4.3/8; daily timing3.7→3/4; ending2→1.7/4. Unchanged chapter 2 exactly repeated 3/7 and its two-medium/one-high hook split. Digestion gained two high hook ratings; repair and ending still had one low. Correctness changes are retained even where scores fell: restoring contradictory cup states or premature fitted-state descriptions would be an invalid response to the scoring objective. The gains support properly timed local revelations, not a claim that formatting alone fixes weak construction.

Chapter 21 would preserve local wording but receive a changed earlier checkpoint history, making it a poorer control. F2 therefore tests chapters 1–8 and 19 (27 fresh players). No skin or ending replacement is smuggled into this candidate.

### F2 verified observations and decisions

- **1, unchanged comparison:** Substantial small-sample variation with identical local source and history.
- **2, muscle work:** ATP reset and calcium recovery provide distinct relationships; gain exceeds the added action.
- **3, fresh joint:** Same shaped bones and distinct material/mechanical contributions improved worth without extra actions.
- **4, opposing pulls:** Removing a generic completion step and showing actual attachments improved both numerator and hook.
- **5, renamed input only:** No measured improvement; retain accurate pre-circulation naming.
- **6, fresh blood carrier:** Modest numerator gain, fewer preparations and stronger hook; not a huge content gain.
- **7, vessel functions:** Contrasting recoil, exchange and assisted venous return made fittings more worthwhile.
- **8, heart depends on blood:** Coronary supply was valued by all three; individual valve consequences and final circulation also contributed.
- **19, fresh glucose feedback:** Failed worthwhile hypothesis. Only muscle uptake and liver release earned consistent value; setup and repeated sensing remained routine.

Retain the tested F2 candidate for detailed investigation. This is not evidence that every change succeeded: chapter 19's worth did not move, and the unchanged opening fell. The removal of the low hook judgment in chapter 19 is a limited signal, not proof of a durable gain. Detailed mode may distinguish informed choice from fast menus, but it must be measured rather than assumed. The most promising recurring pattern is a physical construction whose result exposes a different consequence, including the pump's own need for supply. Clearer serial state labels alone did not establish worth.

### F3 prospective late-chapter replacements

Persistent weak later passages receive fresh authors: skin17 (unchanged 2.7/7 after corrected presentation), synaptic change28 (2.3/6), and ending30 (1.7/4 with a low hook judgment). Each author received neutral game scope and a very rough chapter purpose/interface, not old drafts, grades or reviews, and proposed three approaches. Skin becomes a shorter continuous boundary, learning follows a recognizable repeated bell with a matched local synapse comparison, and the ending uses breath and articulation to greet another person and hear a reply. Independent fresh physiology and experience reviewers saw only these proposals; adopted corrections make local supply/sensing, receptor delivery and sound direction visible rather than hiding the explanation in author notes. These are candidate hypotheses, not score gains.

A further fresh author proposed three approaches to warm/cold physiology for chapter 18 (baseline2/4, medium). The selected seven-action candidate compares the same vessels under opposite conditions, distinguishes sweat appearance from evaporation, and reuses muscle activity for heat. This broadens a genuine homeostatic relationship rather than inserting an arbitrary completion fee. Chapter 19's input name must follow the changed body state; its graph remains unchanged. An independent reviewer checks the integrated thermal passage and neighboring continuity before testing.

Predictions: the shorter skin passage keeps its worthwhile boundary/sensing results with fewer inventory steps; learning's visible matched comparison earns more than a biochemical setup; greeting gives a recognizable reason to use earlier breathing/hearing; and thermal contrast adds worthwhile relationships rather than only a larger denominator. Falsifiers include unchanged or lower worthwhile numerators, worse hook, and players still describing middle actions as label advancement. Planned F3 test: 17, 18, 19, 28, 30 plus unchanged chapter 2 (whose preceding history also remains identical to F2). Detailed Fun is still unmeasured. The candidate has182 required actions/184 rules across30 chapters before any reviewer correction.

#### Fresh full-arc review during F3

A new reviewer read only the current manuscript, philosophy, brief and authoring guidance, without prior drafts or results. It identified three promising patterns: differentiated responses to changed conditions, materials crossing boundaries, and encounters changing later outcomes. It flagged serial component attachment, repeated close-up-to-body joins, and renamed intermediates as risks. The ordinary greeting gives a human purpose, but the reviewer could not establish 30-chapter endurance, and cautioned that mature-body continuity must be visible rather than only explained in notes.

These judgments do not override the trials. In particular, chapter 19 exemplifies the reviewer's favored contrasting-response pattern yet failed to gain worthwhile count in F2; chapters 7–8 include its criticized serial joins yet improved substantially when their consequences differed. The useful distinction to investigate in detailed mode is therefore the actual action's contribution and inference, not a blanket ban on fittings or blanket endorsement of feedback. The current muscle picture already shows glucose breakdown in cell fluid and ATP-supported recovery; the reviewer's unverified factual caution does not require changing that sound representation. No source edits were made during this frozen campaign in response to the review.

The older final body join had earned value for two players, whereas the already-body-linked replacement made that final join repetitive for all three. Barrier and living-layer supply remained worthwhile. This suggests changing the location and consequence of the final join, not restoring preparation for its own sake. The greeting was a plausible reviewer favorite that failed its actual trial; it is not retained merely because its human meaning sounds attractive.

The active candidate restores only chapters 17 and 30 from the corrected F2 source. Actual diff and chapter manifests reviewed. Detailed denominators include purposeful or routine inspection and unsuccessful well-formed attempts, so fast and detailed fractions are not interchangeable measures.

#### Candidate preparation during the frozen D0 baseline

Early detailed observations identify two false ordering premises. In chapter 5, all players expected skin and touch fibres to connect directly, and one also tried a motor attachment before the required skin-first step. In chapter 6, all expected oxygen-bearing red cells to join plasma directly, but the graph required white cells/platelets first. A scratch candidate connects skin and touch fibres first, then permits any of the three completed pathways to begin the arm compound; blood permits either cell group to join plasma first. Required action counts remain 7 and 5. A fresh reviewer, given only candidate chapters 4–6 and neutral requirements, found the orders and partial states coherent and caught two ingredient pictures already depicting their eventual junction. Those pictures are corrected before testing. The candidate passes lint; no adoption or score gain is claimed yet. Removing arbitrary failure can lower a meaningful-attempt count, so clarity and actual physiological inference must be distinguished from friction.

Chapter 3 is valuable despite0/6 Fun and 5/6 worthwhile in its first detailed trial. A small candidate change gives its formerly inert bone-cell fitting a visible contribution: embedded cells communicate through tiny passages, and a subsequent vessel supplies that network while red cells remain inside the vessel. It moves rather than adds a wonder and keeps the graph unchanged. Grounding is pinned OpenStax6.3, osteocytes/canaliculi; no mechanosensing molecular pathway is invented. This is a specific worth hypothesis, not an attempt to make naming harder for decision credit.

A second fresh finale author, blind to predecessors and scores, proposed watering a plant, catching a loose page, and following a kitchen sound. An independent review favored the plant's care/shared-environment premise but rejected a separate air-attachment step as mostly explanatory. The author reduced it from 5 to 4 actions; it remains an untested option. A separately prepared29-chapter/180-action alternative cuts the independent coda and moves the existing renewal passage to the end, connecting vision directly after repair and renewal after the clock. Its first scratch integration missed the reversed operand order of the vision input, which lint caught as a dependency cycle; corrected candidate lint is 0 errors. A fresh blinded comparison provisionally preferred the plant ending while identifying its serial setup risk. Neither preference is measured evidence. These alternatives are held pending the detailed results for renewal and the existing ending.

The unchanged chapter 2 kept its worth and high hook while Fun varied from 3.7 to 4.3. Parsed chapter definitions, prior history and global item definitions affecting this comparison were checked, not merely the chapter text hash. This is a small sample, not a significance test.

- **Bone-cell contribution:** worth rose from 5 to 6 without extra actions. Keep provisionally; one hook judgment fell to medium, so this is not an all-objectives win.
- **Arm ordering:** aggregate Fun and worth fell. All four D0 turns with incompatibility/redirect feedback had received meaningful and worthwhile credit; D1 had none. The clearer successful route does not itself provide enough engaging decisions. Retain truthful order freedom, but replace the chapter experimentally with a fresh feedback episode rather than declaring the old slots solved.
- **Blood:** small Fun increase, flat worth/hook. Keep the more natural order, without claiming new biological content.
- **Breathing:** Fun and worth fell while hook improved from one to two high judgments. D0 had four incompatibility/redirect turns, all credited meaningful and worthwhile; D1 had none. These are not four disposable failed actions: some automatically completed a valid alternative. Retain the valid assembly orders and existing ventilation relationships; do not silently adjust the official counts to manufacture a gain.
- **Lymph:** worth increased strongly, but the revision introduced a false prerequisite: all three players attempted to connect lymph ducts before attaching the local network to the supplied arm. Two such turns earned decision credit. Fix this regression, preserving the valued fluid-entry, valve and early blood-supply consequences, then retest.
- **Kidney:** Fun rose from zero to one, but worth fell from 3.3 to 2.7. Collecting passages and bladder fittings still felt routine, and one natural filter-to-vessel start was rejected. Do not defend the fresh author merely because the draft was new. A second fresh author receives only neutral scope and interfaces; filtration plus selective recovery remains the relationship to test.
- **Vision:** exactly unchanged scores despite the full-eye picture correction. Individual optical contributions and natural motor-attachment order remain a concrete next hypothesis.
- **Renewal ending:** Fun rose from 1.7 to 2.7 and worth from 6.7 to 7.7, with medium hook unchanged; mean decisions also increased from 6.7 to 8.7. Players valued chromosome separation versus cell splitting and the kidney-to-marrow connection. Keep the ending provisionally. Bone-cell pairing remains confusing and some decision credit came from testing that order; the extra one worthwhile action does not mean all two added actions earned value.

### Next candidate hypotheses after D1

A new arm author, blind to drafts and scores, proposed three approaches and selected a heavier-cup perturbation. Independent blind review favored making feedback visibly useful and questioned spending an action joining brain to spinal cord. The integrated candidate instead retains the earlier worthwhile neuromuscular-junction construction, supplies brain/cord together, permits either arm pathway first, and ends with a load that stretches the muscle and elicits adjustment. It has seven required actions; touch is deferred to the later skin chapter. The test is whether this actual use of the circuit adds worth and decisions, not whether the scene sounds relatable.

A second fresh kidney author proposed three approaches. Blind review rejected a one-nephron-to-two-kidneys scale fee and suggested bundling routine drainage preparation. The refined six-action candidate keeps the same paired-kidney context throughout, allows filter or recovery vessels to join the tubules first, and separates blood return from urine outflow. It must outperform the measured D1 loss or be set aside.

A fresh exertion author proposed a seven-action stair climb. It is not adopted or tested: its serial heart, vessel, breathing and sweat gates repeat relationships already present and imply false prerequisites. This is an editorial rejection, not a measured score. A different fresh author explored the colon as a human habitat. Integration revealed that the existing digestive tube already supplies its anatomy, so reinstalling nerves and vessels would be padding. The candidate becomes three actions relating mucus, microbial fermentation of the same meal's residue, and host uptake. The new biological relationship, rather than the requested approximate count, must justify this additional chapter.

Other targeted order changes let lymph ducts, local airway trapping, eye motor connections, cochlear membranes, vestibular reflex routes and the clock's indirect nerve route assemble naturally before whole-body placement. Dendritic uptake no longer requires a lymph-node hotspot: uptake occurs at the airway and the result depicts migration. The real requirement for an activated helper T cell remains. Optical fittings gain distinct ray/aperture diagrams without extra popups. These changes retain the original action counts; alternative recipes do not count toward the approximately 200 required actions.

Keep the concrete feedback episode; clarify the spindle's result immediately rather than relying on assumed specialist knowledge. This is not a universal gain.

Lymph loses much of D1's apparent gain and returns to D0's counts; removing the arbitrary duct order is not enough. All players still value propulsion/valves and the final combined supply/return, but intermediate routing is routine. A fresh author receives neutral scope and interfaces for the final bounded revision.

The colon's detailed result is 2/3 worthwhile and 1/3 meaningful, medium hook. Its lower detailed count is not a decline measured in the same mode as F4's3/3. Players value fermentation and the completed host exchange. One notices that adding mucus appears to trigger uptake, and another wants simpler terminology. Correct the partial views so uptake is already visible when the products meet the colon; use the label small fatty acids. Do not hide the uptake until the last unrelated fitting.

Restoring the kidney recovers worth relative to D1, but Fun returns to zero. Its 0.7 worth increase over D0 cannot be credited to a new local mechanism: only the incoming body changed. The restored version is better supported than the two rejected replacements, not a solved decision-design problem.

Airway defense keeps Fun at 1/5 and increases worth from 3/5 to 5/5, with one high hook judgment. Players now see local trapping and sweeping before the larger body placement, while escaped microbes give the macrophage role a concrete purpose. Vision's distinct optical diagrams increase worth from 3.7/8 to 6.3/8 and add one high hook judgment, but Fun falls. Both support showing each part's different physical contribution; neither proves student recognition of future artwork.

Immunity preserves worthwhile credit on every recorded action and increases meaningful count, while the number of recorded actions falls and all hook judgments drop from high to medium. Do not hide either the 9→7.7 worth numerator or the hook loss. Uptake at the airway is physiologically required to be possible without first placing the cell in the node. The real activated-helper dependency remains; molecular matching and branching cell fates still contribute.

Hearing, balance and clock lose Fun and worthwhile counts after accepting natural local assembly orders. Their remaining useful transformations do not make them strong decision passages. Retain the honest ordering corrections without presenting the scores as improvements or restoring arbitrary failure for credit. Hook also falls for hearing and balance; clock gains one high judgment. These remain pacing risks for a full-length game.

Repair loses both Fun and worth despite only two local picture additions. A separate evidence analyst identified lost credit in the unchanged capillary-growth inference and cell/nutrient preparations, plus fewer motivated attempts at direct support placement. The studies do not isolate the cause: different players, graders and prior history matter. Reverting the new pictures would not address the repeated unexplained capillary-first restriction. The next candidate makes new vessel connection, surface closure and support independent fittings, retaining existing vessel supply and depicting a new perfused loop when the sprout joins. The repeated cell/nutrient preparation remains a specific weakness to check.

A freeform sentence in D0's repair review says player 1 had 6 worthwhile actions; its table and verified counts show 7, which is the count used in the reported 6.7/8 mean. Preserve the original independent report; this erratum does not alter its judgments or the verified comparison.

### D3 hypotheses and reviewed launch

This is the final planned focused revision in this full-story pass: current chapters 5, 11, 15, 24, 30 plus unchanged chapter 2. It will be assessed against actual prior versions, retaining stronger passages rather than automatically promoting the latest proposal.

- Arm: give the spindle connection an immediate drawable stretch/signal-rate comparison and a goal explaining the incoming/outgoing roles. Seven actions remain. The test is whether its own contribution earns value without forcing the player to know the word spindle.
- Lymph: a new author proposed three variants from neutral scope. The selected five-action passage contrasts continuing filtration with insufficient return against continuing supply plus lymph return and roughly stable tissue fluid. Blind review required matching tissue views, a continuous return route and corrected research attribution. Root integration preserves either local-network/body order. The test is value from a genuine homeostatic consequence rather than another routing label.
- Colon: partial states already show water recovery and microbial-product uptake; the mucus fitting adds its barrier contribution. No action added. The test is coherent attribution while retaining the microbial-host relationship.
- Repair: the vascular branch, new surface and connective support can be fitted in any order after debris clearance. Existing nearby vessels support the cells; fitting the new branch joins another vessel to establish a perfused local route. Blind review specifically caught the invalid image of a blind-ended sprout already supplying through-flow. Eight actions remain.
- Ending: preserve copying/separation/division and EPO, but allow skin, blood or bone renewal first. A blind reviewer rejected free-floating bone-cell grouping; the revised operands are views of an existing vascularized bone surface and their combination depicts local removal followed by deposition. The whole body supplies the matrix and materials. Three independent renewal fittings make the final complete compound, with no completion fee. Eight actions remain; the test is worthwhile coordination rather than a hollow group or reliance on rejected pairings for decision credit.

The final blind integrated review found no other material issue in these five passages. Actual diff/manifests were inspected. The first D3 preflight was never played: diff review caught duplicated small in a colon picture. A fresh prefix, `udfull-d3a`, freezes the corrected actual candidate at SHA-256 `814c35fc400d6529809f8048bf399cdcaa1f5677482b5955f37ba555b9a34710`. - **Arm:** hook rises from one to three high judgments, but worth loses one and Fun is flat. - **Lymph:** the fresh fluid-buildup/return comparison earns value, but all three players attempt a valid tissue-entry-to-collecting-vessel connection that the manuscript omits. Retain the independent fittings and corrected perfusion because they remove a false prerequisite and a misleading vessel picture. Do not describe this as a Fun improvement. Repeated cell/material preparations remain weak.
- **Ending:** the parallel compound loses both Fun and worth. Preparing three local examples and then fitting them into the body makes the final actions feel repetitive; bone removal and deposition have also been collapsed into one step. Restore the better-supported sequential ending, which gives these changes distinct actions on existing bone. The corrective candidate separates its skin/blood and bone goals, and depicts the supplied bone cells before they are acting, so their ingredient pictures no longer imply they have already remodeled matrix. Its body chain remains an illustration order, not a biological prerequisite between tissues. This modified restoration needs its own test.

All candidates and accepted poor outcomes remain in the comparison; no player is dropped because of an unfavorable judgment. The actual source diff and chapter manifest were inspected before launch.

The corrected lymph chapter has no rejected connections in these three records. Its lower Fun and worth are real reported losses, not adjusted away: the original restricted version included credited attempts, and the now straightforward collecting-vessel and venous-junction fittings remain routine. Relative to D2's 0.3/5 Fun, 2.3/5 worth and medium hook, it retains a modest numerical gain and two high hook judgments. Players value the tissue-entry consequence and completed supply/return model. Retain the corrected fresh version without treating the D3 peak as its score.

The ending recovers two worthwhile actions on average over D3, while Fun stays at two and the denominator rises from eight to 8.3. One hook judgment rises to high. Compared with D1's 2.7/8.7 Fun and 7.7/8.7 worth, both numerators remain lower; do not claim a clean win over every predecessor. Keep the clarified sequential restoration because it again separates chromosome copying, chromosome movement, cell splitting, marrow signaling, bone removal and bone formation, and achieves the stronger ending without the parallel compound's repetitive example fittings.

Ordering is not completely resolved in the ending. One player inspected developing red cells before the skin attachment and found no current partner; this inspection earned meaningful/worthwhile credit. Another tried pairing the two bone-cell populations, and the engine automatically applied removal to the existing body; that action earned neither credit. A third inspected the removing cells and automatically applied them, receiving credit. Preserve these distinctions: inspection, rejected intent, automatic successful action and actual discovery are not the same thing. The body chain is a chosen illustration sequence, while bone removal and subsequent deposition refer to one real remodeling site. The final report identifies this residual order ambiguity rather than claiming that new goals eliminated it.

Final selection keeps D4's lymph and ending, D3's coherent colon pictures and independent repair fittings, and D2's restored arm. Other sections retain the explicitly recorded choices above. The final gameplay remains the D4 freeze. Final whitespace verification found spaces on five otherwise blank lines; these were removed. ### Final verification and retention

Final lint reports **zero errors and 12 reviewed warnings**. Ten warnings are intentional compound-owner preservation followed by transformation/incorporation; two concern the immune population's sampled bacteria and antibody-producing/memory-cell roles. They are not unresolved completion errors. Analysis reports **357 items (183 primitive), 214 rules, 185 required actions and 30 chapters**. Required actions, alternative routes and recorded trial decisions are separate counts.

Every campaign passed replay against its actual frozen source, exact accepted-response/final/look-state checks, independent model/rubric metadata checks, classification and mean-count arithmetic, and assembled-report verification.
