| What changes when water is scarce? | Water-loss signal + kidney response → water conservation; thirst + drinking route → replenishment | Responses change both internal handling and behaviour. | Same feedback pattern without a new consequence; may belong with the previous passage, 4–6. |
| Why is bone living tissue? | Old bone surface + removing cells → remodelled site; bone-building cells + matrix/mineral → replacement support | A solid-looking structure renews itself; strength is maintained, not simply installed. | Repeating skin repair at a slower tempo; 6–8. |
| How can a damaged bone regain continuity? | Aligned damaged ends + temporary repair tissue → bridge; replacement bone + loading history → reorganized support | Repair trades immediate continuity for later strength; different tissues have different possibilities. | A medical-treatment simulation or redundant repair states; 5–7, possibly merge with bone renewal. |
| How does defense distinguish a breach from an intact border? | Damaged boundary + local alarm → recruited defense; recognition + engulfing cells → removal | Barriers and active defense have different reach and costs. | Enemy-combat metaphor and immunity vocabulary; 6–8. |
| How can a past encounter change a later response? | Recognition + expansion of selected cells → targeted response; surviving memory cells + later encounter → altered response | The body's history changes its present capabilities. | Disease catalogue or universal-immunity claim; 6–8; needs a separate sourced brief. |
| How does light become a useful view? | Lens/cornea + retinal surface → focused image; receptor cells + signalling route → visual information; comparison + motor control → guided reach | Signals need interpretation and calibration, rather than tiny pictures sent to the brain. | Making consciousness a final combination; 7–9. |
| How does vibration become hearing? | Eardrum + ossicles → transferred vibration; inner-ear movement + sensory cells → nerve signals | The same broad task of sensing can be built from a very different physical input. | Repeated “sense organ + nerve” assembly; 6–8. |
| How does the body know which way it is moving? | Inner-ear motion sensing + position signals → orientation information; orientation + postural pulls → balance response | Multiple imperfect information sources cooperate to stabilize action. | A renamed version of the arm-feedback chapter; 6–8. |
| How can a response begin before a conscious decision? | Sensory route + local spinal circuit → rapid response; motor route + muscle → withdrawal | Information can be processed along different routes with different timing. | Unnecessary danger narrative or overstating independence from the brain; 4–6. |
| How do cells with shared DNA do different jobs? | Shared instructions + selective gene use → different protein sets; cell arrangements + surrounding material → different tissue functions | A common inheritance supports differences; neither DNA alone nor a parts list is a whole organism. | “Add specialization” rename fee; must revisit concrete cell types, 7–9. |
| How can tissue grow without losing its organization? | Cell division + local signals → controlled increase; new cells + existing scaffold/routes → growing tissue | More cells alone are not a larger functioning body. | A second generic cell chapter, 6–8. |
| How does repeated use change later ability? | Repeated demand + recovery/material supply → changed tissue capacity; changed capacity + familiar task → different response | Living construction continues after the apparent completion of the model. | Vague upgrade tokens, simplistic exercise advice, or duplicating bone/muscle growth; 6–8. |
| How do bodily rhythms coordinate changing demands? | Environmental light + internal timing → aligned rhythm; timing signals + tissue activity → coordinated changes | The body inhabits time as well as space; maintenance is not identical activity at every moment. | Abstract clock tokens and unsourced claims about sleep; 5–7. |
| What stays active while the person rests? | Maintained circulation + ventilation + tissue renewal → supported rest; sensory readiness + waking → resumed action | Visible inactivity conceals organized work, returning to the opening theme at a larger scale. | A recap charging again for mechanisms already built; 3–5, perhaps an ending rather than a chapter. |

These eighteen questions are a first selection, not an exhaustive inventory. Together with the current ten chapters they give 28 possible positions, with room to merge neighboring topics or deepen a particularly fruitful one. Their rough action ranges are planning placeholders. They omit much of digestion, liver chemistry, lymphatic return, endocrine signalling, peptides, brain function and development. There is no reason to treat the present 65-action sample as an upper bound on the larger story’s interest.

The longer arc should cross-link several concerns rather than tour organs once each:

| Overarching concern | Systems and smaller-scale mechanisms it can connect | A recurring question with different answers |
|---|---|---|
| Energy and materials | Digestion, absorption, liver storage and processing, cellular ATP renewal, muscle and brain demand | Where does the usable material come from, where does it go, and what changes between meals or during work? |
| Stable internal conditions | Lungs, kidney, skin circulation, sweating, thirst, hormonal signals | What is sensed, what responds, and how does that response alter the sensed condition? |
| Information and coordination | Receptors, sensory and motor nerves, spinal circuits, brain integration, endocrine signals, biological clocks | What is being communicated, to whom, by which route, and on what timescale? |
| Boundaries and return routes | Cell membranes, gut and lung interfaces, skin, blood vessels, lymphatic return | What should cross, what should stay separate, and how does material get back? |
| Construction and renewal | DNA use, ribosomes, peptides/proteins, secretion, matrix, growth, bone and skin repair | How do instructions, matter, energy and organization become useful living structure? |
| Defense and memory | Skin/mucus, local inflammatory responses, immune recognition, lymph nodes, immune memory | What happens when a boundary is breached, and how can an earlier encounter change a later response? |
| Activity across time | Movement, rest, sleep, circadian timing, growth, adaptation and aging | What changes when visible activity stops, when demands recur, or as the body develops? |

This is the more useful test of the eventual game: whether changing scale or revisiting a system answers a new question. The same liver, nerve route or cell machinery can acquire new significance when combined with a different relationship. Trimming a weak packaging step frees room for such relationships; it does not count against the breadth of the body.

Injury presently earns its place by exposing the difference between closing a hole and renewing tissue. Illness could later expose a specific failed relationship, but a catalogue of diseases would change the story's character. Dissection adds no demonstrated value to these constructions yet. Both remain open possibilities, not missing requirements.


### Concrete regulation candidates for later development

A separate proposal explored feedback in the same body. These are **unplayed follow-on candidates**, not extra chapters silently counted in the prototype. They illustrate that regulatory events can happen within a result: the player need not drag a separate “measurement,” “command” and “balance” token for every internal event.

- **Cooling has a cost:** sweat-secreting cells + surface-opening duct → gland; gland + existing body → connected sweating system; warm sunshine + that body → warming and increased sweating; dry moving air + that state → evaporation, cooling and an easing response. Results must show temperature sensing before the increased secretion and again as cooling reduces the response. Gland-cell secretion changes whole-body heat exchange through skin and surroundings. The named conditions and elapsed exposure matter; a breeze is not guaranteed cooling in every environment. Pinned sources: [OpenStax feedback](https://github.com/openstax/osbooks-anatomy-physiology/blob/b0058a743bdd2cc08196abaaa7d5469970c0d80c/modules/m45989/index.cnxml) and [heat balance](https://github.com/openstax/osbooks-anatomy-physiology/blob/b0058a743bdd2cc08196abaaa7d5469970c0d80c/modules/m46456/index.cnxml).
- **Keep water, replace water:** kidneys + drainage tract → kidneys with an outlet; that assembly + existing body → connected kidney work; continued activity in warmth + body → sweat loss and a water-conserving response; drinking water + that state → absorption, replenishment and easing of the additional conservation signal. The important distinction is retaining water versus replacing what has already left. Changes in transport by tiny kidney lining cells affect the whole body's losses; blood carries signals as well as materials. The result should describe blood-concentration feedback without claiming thirst can ease only after absorption—mouth/gut signals also matter. Sources: [NIDDK kidney function](https://www.niddk.nih.gov/health-information/kidney-disease/kidneys-how-they-work) and pinned [OpenStax water balance](https://github.com/openstax/osbooks-anatomy-physiology/blob/b0058a743bdd2cc08196abaaa7d5469970c0d80c/modules/m46416/index.cnxml).

Both proposals narrate linked physiological events; they are not continuously running simulations. Their shortness reflects this particular choice of scale, not a shortage of physiology. Deeper kidney or gland chapters could examine different relationships without requiring the rejected medical-student syllabus. A later combined passage could introduce the kidneys before one sustained warming episode, preserving the water loss rather than creating a second heat event solely to advance a chapter.

Further source anchors for the breadth map: [NCI/SEER on lymphatic return, immune and fat-transport roles](https://training.seer.cancer.gov/anatomy/lymphatic/), [NLM on urea and nitrogen processing](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/mesh/68014508), and [NIH on hormones](https://www.nigms.nih.gov/biobeat/2024/07/what-is-a-hormone). These support future liver–blood–kidney, tissue-fluid–lymph–blood and peptide-message–receptor connections. They are starting points for authorship, not a count of already tested combinations.


### Baseline and revision

The [65-action baseline](playtests/2026.11.11_20.27.59/report.md) covers all ten chapters with three fresh players per chapter: 30 completed chapter trials, 30 validated independent grades and ten fresh chapter syntheses. Players, graders and synthesizers all used actual `gpt-6.1-terra` with reasoning effort `medium`. The interface was `--fast`, with the normal compatible-partner menu and identical grade-8–10 guidance. **Fun was not measured.** Each later chapter began with its checkpoint’s complete public history; these are not thirty people voluntarily playing the whole story.

The cell passage received 6.7/8 worthwhile actions and high hook from all three graders, despite players calling the fittings repetitive. That argues for keeping the different cellular jobs. The temporary-seal passage received 3.7/4 and high hook. Digestion received 4.7/6 and low/medium hook; all three players named its organ-by-organ preamble as repetitive. The ending received 7/8 but low/medium hook: valued explanations and a satisfying finish did not necessarily produce a wish to continue. These are attributed AI judgments, not student learning measurements or proof of an ideal duration.

The revision keeps ten chapters and has **62 combinations**:

- Supply cell fluid and nuclei, reducing cell setup from eight actions to six while retaining selective membrane construction and all three functional organelle fittings.
- Keep digestion at six actions, but replace successive named-organ arrivals with chemical digestion and smooth-muscle mixing/movement. This gives the earlier muscle account a new arrangement and job before absorption and transport.
- Move the first glass lift to the end of the skin chapter, before the injury; skin now has eight actions. The same body then sustains the scrape.
- Combine export and extracellular collagen processing, distinguish local chemical signals from nerve commands, and let restored tissue conclude the story. Renewal now has six actions. Visible text explicitly connects growing protein chains to the internal processing system and surface coverage to reconnecting, maturing protective layers.
- Clarify an enzyme’s role in the force chapter and name the completed vessel network as a connected circuit. These minor wording refinements do not change their recipe structures.

A fresh reviewer examined the revised candidate without previous versions or verdicts. It found no remaining factual or identity blocker after recommending the visible protein-processing and barrier-maturation links. A focused follow-up checked the new digestion mechanisms against NIDDK and the licensed OpenStax reading; its route-before-flow clarification was adopted. These are informed reviews, not playtest participants.

The [revision campaign](playtests/2026.11.11_20.43.01/report.md) tests chapters 2, 7, 8, 9 and 10 with fifteen new players and separate graders. The other chapters retain baseline experience evidence; their earlier-history context differs, and the small wording changes in 3 and 5 were editorially checked rather than assigned another experience score. Run reports retain manuscript hashes and exact coverage. One baseline grading reply failed the JSON schema and was replaced by a fresh valid grading assignment; no player record or accepted assessment was discarded.


#### Revised outcomes

| Substantially revised chapter | Baseline worthwhile | Revised worthwhile | Revised hook |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| 2. Inside the muscle | 6.7/8 | 4.3/6 | High |
| 7. Food has not arrived yet | 4.7/6 | 5.7/6 | Medium/high: 2 medium, 1 high |
| 8. A living border | 5.3/7 | 6/8 | Medium |
| 9. Closed is not rebuilt | 3.7/4 | 4/4 | High |
| 10. The work that stays invisible | 7/8 | 5.3/6 | Medium |

The digestion change is the clearest promising local result: the same six actions now distinguish mechanisms and connect muscle organization to a different task. The revised ending lost the low hook judgment but remained conditional rather than eager. Moving the glass earlier did not by itself create high hook in the skin chapter. These small independent samples do not isolate causal effects.

**Shorter was not uniformly better.** The cell chapter retained high hook, but its credited action count and proportion both fell. That is not evidence that the two removed constructions were valueless. Its deeper issue is the relation between setup and what the player hopes to build. This matters especially for the user’s subsequent cells-first proposal.

### Cells-first opening experiment

The user proposed a different source of pleasure: assembling a body from parts smaller than those used by Frankenstein. That changes the question behind the opening. Instead of “what does this arm need?”, the player can ask “what can these small working parts become together?” The earlier concept rankings favored an ordinary-action anchor; they do not settle this revised construction premise.

*From the inside out* is a complete ten-chapter **63-combination** comparison manuscript. Its first three chapters build a muscle-cell model (eight actions), organize working muscles (five), then construct and fit an arm framework (six). The cell becomes an ordinary usable item before it is organized into larger tissue; its stored instructions, protein builders and energy machinery remain conceptually inside the larger assembly. The later circulation, breathing, digestion, skin and repair chapters follow the revised arc.

This variant restores cell-fluid and nuclear-compartment construction, beginning with their ingredients rather than their supplied assemblies. That both fits the new premise and takes the positive eight-action baseline seriously. It is consequently **not a pure order-only experiment** against the six-action cell revision. Its later organs still include supplied assemblies; it demonstrates a starting direction for building more of the body from smaller parts, not a completed microscopic construction of every organ.


The opening reviewer found the small-to-large construction substantive: force-producing cells become organized tissue, then attachment geometry makes their pulls useful. Its first reading was blind to other versions and judgments; assessment of the reinstated preparation recipes was an informed update within that assignment. Adopted refinements keep fuel processing inside the constructed fibers, make the selective mature-cell model explicit in player text, call the undissolved ingredient mineral salts, and describe tendon effects prospectively before bone attachment. The revised fuel sequence is sugar + fibers → sugar-processing fibers, then oxygen + those fibers → ATP-supported fibers; it supplies ordinary enzymes within the cells rather than packaging their work externally.

The [cells-first campaign](playtests/2026.11.11_20.54.09/report.md) tests only this variant’s first three chapters, with nine fresh Terra Medium players, independent graders and three fresh chapter synthesizers. Later chapters share the revised constructions but have not been replayed as a complete uninterrupted cells-first experience. The same audience guidance and `--fast` protocol apply.


#### Cells-first outcomes and current direction

| Opening chapter | Worthwhile | Hook judgments |
|---|---:|---|
| Inside the muscle | 6/8 | 3 high |
| A pull has a running cost | 5/5 | 3 high |
| A joint is not a motor | 3.7/6 | 1 medium, 2 high |

Players valued the way the completed cell machinery contributes to tension and then becomes useful at a larger scale. Applying fuel and oxygen to the already built fibers made the internal work continuous with their earlier construction. The arm's final opposing-pull and holding-still explanations remained stronger than its simpler bone/surface preparations. The reports still describe guided, often predictable making; the new order does not turn it into an inference puzzle.

**Current editorial preference: continue from the cells-first direction.** Its value is not just adding microscopic vocabulary. Smaller constructions persist inside the larger explanation, giving the player a reason to care what the next scale can do. The user's creation premise and the new opening records justify this direction; the samples do not prove it universally superior to the arm-first opening. The next long-game development can make more later tissues and organs from smaller units and connect their work through energy, material exchange, information, regulation, growth and defense. It need not turn every molecule or every named anatomical part into a required action.

#### Verification and retained evidence

Both expanded manuscripts pass lint with zero errors and three intentional mixed-fate warnings each: cell model, arm framework and heart chambers remain while fittings are installed, then transform through a substantive new contribution. No engine, UI, graphical, screenshot or browser verification work was done for this expansion.

Across the three expansion campaigns, all **54** retained response arrays match their original accepted session replies and final reports, including harness-owned look flags. All sessions completed and replay verification passed. The **342** action rows preserve their independent meaningfulness classifications; worthwhile either/or counts and displayed means were checked against the detailed tables. All **18** marked chapter syntheses are copied exactly into the three mechanically assembled reports. Each report identifies its manuscript hash, models, guidance, timing scope and coverage. These totals describe separate chapter assignments across versions, not a single 342-action game.

The requested response arrays and reports remain in `playtests/`. Disposable runtime snapshots, raw packets, adapters, prompts, logs and review scratch are removed after the checks. Licensed source texts remain ignored local background for the ongoing development project. The earlier four-chapter manuscript and its evidence remain unchanged.


<a id="expansion-three-further-fast-revision-rounds"></a>

### Three further fast revision rounds

The user requested three additional cycles of playtest → identify weak chapters or recurring combination styles → revise → freshly grade the result. These are new cycles after the preceding expansion work. Start with complete cells-first chapter coverage, then use targeted campaigns for each revision; the baseline alone does not count as a revision round. All roles retain the user's Terra Medium choice, and every run remains `--fast`. No graphics, screenshots, engine changes or publication are included.

The initial manuscript has ten chapters and 63 combinations. Each tested chapter receives three fresh players, an independent grader per record, and a fresh chapter synthesis. The same grade-8–10 guidance is frozen within each presentation condition. Decisions will use concrete reactions and weak moments as well as the summary counts. Ordinary compatibility guidance is part of the intended game; the mere existence of an obvious valid pair does not establish that its construction is worthless. A revision may add a richer relationship, reorganize or replace a passage, or trim an unproductive step. The body's supply of material is not in question.


#### Baseline and the user’s explanation constraint

The new [baseline](playtests/2026.11.11_21.09.50/report.md) covers all ten cells-first chapters. Its clearest weak passages were the arm framework (2.7/6 worthwhile actions; 2 medium and 1 high hook) and the longer assembly joins in circulation and skin. Cell work, air/blood separation and local repair retained specific value. One baseline grading reply failed the schema; the adapter obtained a fresh valid grader without discarding the player record or an accepted grade.

Before the first revision was tested, the user clarified that the story had **far too much explanatory text**. The intended experience relies primarily on medical-textbook-style art and item titles, with goals supplying connection logic, chapter openings motivating the work in two to four sentences, and brief wonders at important moments roughly every two to four combinations. This redirected round one from two local recipe changes to a complete pass over all ten chapters. It did not cancel the three requested rounds.

#### Round one — constructions and planned illustrations

The revision keeps 63 combinations. It replaces the framework’s simple grouping of two named bones with a living collagen framework reinforced by mineral, then gives cartilage, joint fluid and ligaments separate visible contributions. In circulation, one vessel-grouping action becomes a red-cell construction: hemoglobin joins a supplied flexible cell envelope, while arteries and veins arrive as a combined set. The different internal equipment of muscle cells and mature red cells connects cellular specialization to the larger transport system.

Player-facing wonder prose fell from **64 passages / 2,132 words** to **24 passages / 365 words**, using a consistent whitespace word count of unbraced item prose. These are authored passage counts; conditional fitting order can change exact intervals. The new 127 `{...}` passages describe planned anatomy, insets, labels, arrows and comparison panels. They are **illustration plans, not finished or visually verified artwork**. Scientific grounding and model qualifications remain in author notes.

A fresh biological/contribution reviewer checked the two changed recipe passages. A separate fresh reviewer audited all ten chapters’ picture plans without earlier drafts, verdicts or playtests. Adopted fixes separate ATP recycling from fuel processing, remove tendon cords before their installation, keep the lung interface incomplete until air-sac geometry is joined, establish the supplied hand apparatus before grip, and distinguish arterial flow direction from oxygen content. The short skin finale uses tactile grip feedback, supported by the cited human experiments; no extra gripping recipe was required.

The [round-one retest](playtests/2026.11.11_21.44.06/report.md) covers all ten revised chapters with thirty new players. Its guidance explicitly identifies planned picture descriptions and untested visual recognition. Consequently this is a changed presentation condition as well as a story revision, not an isolated causal test of deleting prose. The next revisions will use failures of intelligibility, concrete weak moments and evidence of inference before a wonder, rather than automatically restoring every omitted explanation. Adult-AI inference from diagram descriptions remains different from student learning from rendered pictures.


##### Round-one outcomes

| Chapter | Baseline worthwhile | Round one worthwhile | Round-one hook |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| 1. Cells | 7/8 | 5.7/8 | Medium/high |
| 2. Force | 5/5 | 4.3/5 | High |
| 3. Framework | 2.7/6 | 3.3/6 | Medium/high |
| 4. Signals | 3.7/6 | 2.3/6 | Medium |
| 5. Circuit | 5/8 | 3/8 | Medium/high |
| 6. Air | 6/6 | 2/6 | Medium |
| 7. Food | 5.3/6 | 2.3/6 | Medium/high |
| 8. Skin | 4.7/8 | 2.3/8 | Medium |
| 9. Seal | 3/4 | 3/4 | High |
| 10. Renewal | 4.7/6 | 3.3/6 | Medium |

Fun before and after: **not measured (`--fast`)**. The lower credited counts are retained, not treated as a reason to recover the previous volume of prose. Some earlier credit came from explanatory results that have deliberately been removed. The new evidence asks whether the constructions and picture plans can carry that work. Strong local connections survived: opposing muscle pulls, blood cells with different internal equipment, air/blood separation, grip feedback and protein export into tissue. Many joins still felt like routine assembly. There was one fresh grader retry after an invalid schema reply in chapter 9; no accepted assessment was replaced.

The clearest intelligibility failures were ATP's unexplained role in chapter 1, the undefined ADP acronym in chapter 2, and load arrows whose meaning was not evident in the cartilage result. Two goal descriptions also completed too early: an inside was announced before enclosing fluid, and covering the hand before attaching skin. These are authoring issues in goals and represented relationships, not engine failures.

#### Round two — make relationships visible

The second revision targets chapters **1, 2, 3, 6 and 8**, keeping 63 combinations. It moves the opening's generic completion wonder to a short ATP definition, gives ATP recycling a labelled used-carrier loop, shows individual muscle pulls reaching the connective wrapping, and compares equal forces over different cartilage contact areas. The two premature goal descriptions are corrected.

In breathing, joining two muscle groups is replaced by fitting pleural membranes around the exchange lungs, then connecting the supplied breathing chest. The resulting sequence distinguishes moist mechanical coupling from air entering the airway. In skin, the proposed drawings show fibers and cell junctions resisting a tug and nutrients diffusing from deeper vessels to the living surface cells. These are visible contributions rather than additional anatomy names or a return to explanatory paragraphs. The text descriptions remain plans for later art; their recognizability at the game's display size is not established.


A fresh informed reviewer received only the active brief, current manuscript and standing rules, with particular attention to chapters 1, 2, 3, 6 and 8. It found no blocking problem in the proposed drawings or state chains. Adopted refinements keep the fuel input a single sugar molecule rather than a starch-like chain, and assign the lung interface’s air-side and blood-side layers to separate operands. The [round-two campaign](playtests/2026.11.11_22.02.41/report.md) uses fifteen fresh players and independent graders, with five fresh chapter syntheses and unchanged illustration-proxy guidance.


##### Round-two outcomes

| Retested chapter | Before worthwhile | After worthwhile | Fun before / after | After hook |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| 1. Cells | 5.7/8 | 5.3/8 | Not measured / not measured | Medium/high |
| 2. Force | 4.3/5 | 4.7/5 | Not measured / not measured | High |
| 3. Framework | 3.3/6 | 2.7/6 | Not measured / not measured | Medium/high |
| 6. Air | 2/6 | 4/6 | Not measured / not measured | Medium |
| 8. Skin | 2.3/8 | 3.7/8 | Not measured / not measured | Medium |

Before: round one; after: [round two](playtests/2026.11.11_22.02.41/report.md), same picture-proxy guidance and `--fast`. Breathing's pleural connection and skin's underlying supply relationship gained concrete explanatory value in the new records. Two muscle players explicitly found the collective pull through connective wrapping surprising. The ATP cycle was all three force players' most worthwhile moment. The new early ATP definition removed the recurring unexplained-acronym complaint, but the opening retained ordinary setup and a question about how fibers actually pull. Clearer cartilage pictures did not raise the framework's credited count. These mixed findings prevent treating greater visual specificity as an automatic improvement.

The remaining breathing join was called repetitive after its interface had already been explained. Skin still ended with a much stronger everyday feedback payoff than its preparatory layers; its hook remained medium. No technical grading retries were needed in this campaign.

#### Round three — make the completed structures do more explanatory work

The third revision targets chapters **1, 4, 5, 6 and 7**, guided by the remaining round-two gaps and the weaker unrevised round-one chapters. It retains 63 combinations. The muscle-cell result now depicts unchanged-length filaments overlapping to shorten a repeating unit. The nerve passage replaces naming the central nervous system from two supplied organs with motor fibers plus myelinating support cells; this makes cooperation between different cells part of the outgoing route. The central system is supplied as an assembly, leaving its richer construction for the longer game.

The blood diagrams distinguish suspended cells from dissolved cargo and label the capillary exchange map. The air passage compares many small sacs with one large space and moves the exchange wonder to the completed lung arrangement, so that convergence adds surface area rather than repeating the earlier interface. The gut is given muscle layers before adding the meal: food now makes the tube's movement visible instead of merely filling a container. Chemical digestion and absorption retain separate actions and roles. These changes seek worthwhile relationships, not hidden compatibility or extra choices for their own sake.


A new informed reviewer saw the current manuscript, active brief and standing rules, excluding all earlier drafts and judgments. It found no blocker in the five targeted chapters. Its adopted refinements retain a support-cell nucleus outside the nerve wrapping, show the gut’s lengthwise layer contracting as well as circular squeezing, mark the capillary inset “Tissue exchange when supplied,” and specify the geometry of the lung shape comparison (one sphere versus eight at half the radius). This last inset is a geometric illustration of surface area, not a literal count or shape inventory of human alveoli.

The [round-three campaign](playtests/2026.11.11_22.12.30/report.md) tests those five chapters with fifteen fresh players, independent graders and five fresh chapter synthesizers. All use the same Terra Medium configuration, `--fast` mode and illustration-proxy guidance as rounds one and two. The reviewed and tested current version has **26 wonder passages / 389 words**, with no individual wonder longer than 20 words. Compared with the new baseline’s 64 passages / 2,132 words, this is about **82% less popup prose**. Its 127 picture plans contain 2,376 words; those are temporary verbal proxies for future drawings, so this reduction must not be described as a reduction in the text-only harness’s entire reading load.


##### Round-three outcomes

| Retested chapter | Before worthwhile | After worthwhile | Fun before / after | After hook |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| 1. Cells | 5.3/8 | 5.3/8 | Not measured / not measured | Medium/high |
| 4. Signals | 2.3/6 | 3.7/6 | Not measured / not measured | Medium/high |
| 5. Circuit | 3/8 | 4/8 | Not measured / not measured | Medium/high |
| 6. Air | 4/6 | 5/6 | Not measured / not measured | Medium/high |
| 7. Food | 2.3/6 | 6/6 | Not measured / not measured | Medium |

The before evidence is round two for chapters 1 and 6, and round one for chapters 4, 5 and 7. After: [round three](playtests/2026.11.11_22.12.30/report.md). These are different fresh chapter assignments, with changed checkpoint history as well as local revisions; they do not isolate a causal treatment effect. No technical grading retries were needed in round three.

The opening illustrates why the count alone is insufficient. Its worthwhile mean stayed at 5.3/8, but all three players now singled out the final sliding-filament construction as the strongest moment: the cells' structure explains shortening without the filaments themselves shrinking. That relationship appears only in the planned picture, not in an accompanying wonder. The added selectivity drawing also supported a functional reading of the membrane. Repeated nucleus preparation and fluid setup remain weaker.

Two digestion players chose adding the meal as their most worthwhile moment, because it made the previously fitted muscle layers do something visible. The third chose the convergence of gut and circulation. All six actions received story-value credit in the synthesis, but the graders still assigned **medium hook**; clearer worthwhile results did not establish eagerness for more chapters. Lining and vessel joins could still feel repetitive while clarifying distinct locations. Keep that contrary evidence alongside the high credited count.

The nerve construction gave a new reason to notice supporting cells, while the ordinary motor-nerve attachment remained predictable. Labelled tissue exchange became the circulation chapter's shared standout, but plasma plus red cells remained its weaker mixture. The lung shape comparison was valued by all three players; joining the completed interface to the lung spaces still repeated information for all three. The pleural and circulation relationships remain stronger than that join.

#### Current judgment after the three rounds

Retain the concise presentation and current cells-first manuscript. It has a concrete worthwhile proposition: the same bodily action depends on cellular work, tissue arrangement, exchange across boundaries and feedback, and those relationships can be revealed by construction. The strongest picture-dependent evidence comes from filament overlap, the collection of individual pulls, surface-area geometry, capillary exchange and the gut's movement. The final grip and later repair retain useful everyday and inside-to-outside connections.

**The show/do test is promising but incomplete.** The agent records can identify relationships carried by proposed pictures rather than wonder prose. They cannot show whether students recognize those pictures at game size, infer the mechanism unaided, retain it, or transfer it to a new case. The current trials also retain menu assistance and verbal picture descriptions. They do not establish the visual pleasure of making, student enjoyment or pacing over 200 combinations.

Do not restore the discarded explanatory volume merely to recover the verbose baseline's scores. Keep essential short definitions where a term otherwise has no meaning, and make further revisions earn their place through a specific visible relationship or construction. Remaining weaker passages include opening fluid/nuclear preparation, some framework fittings, plasma mixture, the repeated lung-interface join and late skin/repair assembly. Chapter 9 and 10's latest local evidence is round one; chapters 2, 3 and 8 retain round-two evidence. They have not been silently assigned scores for the final whole manuscript.

#### Verification and retention for these three rounds

The new baseline plus three completed revisions produced **90 completed chapter trials**, **90 independent grades**, **30 fresh chapter syntheses** and **579 action rows**, across four explicitly versioned campaigns. This is additional to the earlier expansion's 54 trials, not a single uninterrupted playthrough. All retained response arrays exactly match accepted session replies and final reports, including harness-owned look flags. All sessions replay-verified and completed; manuscript/runtime hashes and guidance were uniform within each condition. Every original meaningfulness classification is preserved, worthwhile either/or flags and displayed means match the detailed tables, and every marked synthesis is copied verbatim into its mechanically assembled report. The two schema-invalid grading attempts received fresh replacements before acceptance; no accepted judgment or player record was discarded.

Final manuscript SHA-256: `6760c9d6792cefe698d5fc1abcab9a2a27929142fb461e1272d14d342c0cf4e0`. It matches round three's frozen manuscript. Lint: **zero errors**, three reviewed intentional mixed-fate warnings for the cell, arm and heart compounds. Structural analysis: ten chapters; 63 combinations, all on the completion route; 119 item identities, 63 primitive; average chapter-start inventory 7.3, maximum 9. No engine, UI, artwork, browser, screenshot or publication work was performed.

The standing [evaluation protocol](../../docs/fun-and-evaluation.md) owns the reproducible player/grader/synthesis instructions. Each retained chapter report records exact guidance, actual model/effort, mode, source hash, timing scope and evidence limits; the [working notes](#prototype-11-november-2026--three-further-fast-revision-rounds) contain the aligned score tables. Disposable sessions, manuscript snapshots, raw prompts, logs, review scratch and one-off adapters were removed after verification. The requested player arrays and reports remain, along with the project's licensed local research library for ongoing development.

#### Subsequent detailed study

The user next requested three `--detailed` revision/retest rounds examining Fun, hook, worthwhile and strong/weak combination patterns. The [detailed study](#ten-chapter-detailed-trials) owns that follow-on work: a fresh ten-chapter baseline, three actual revisions, 57 chapter trials and 19 syntheses. It changes local assembly orders, introduces completed vessel/lung compounds, rejects a skin-compound experiment and tests a persistent resident cell producing repair material. The current draft has a 62-action completion route with 70 authored rules. The fast scores and manuscript counts above remain historical evidence for their specified versions, not scores for the later detailed draft.

## Ten-chapter detailed trials


This is a historical study of earlier ten-chapter manuscripts, which have been removed. Continue development in [anatomy.md](anatomy.md); the [full-story study](#full-story-development) covers its current 30-chapter form. Original filenames and scores below identify the versions tested.

The user requested three `--detailed` revision-and-retest rounds after the [fast study](#expansion-three-further-fast-revision-rounds), considering **Fun, hook and worthwhile together** and examining patterns of combination as well as weak chapters. The [single active brief](../../stories/anatomy/design-brief.md) governs audience, science, concise prose, planned illustrations and scope. The subject has ample material; this experiment asks which relationships and actions deserve the player's attention.

### Design and evidence limits

Start with a new all-ten-chapter detailed baseline of `from-cells.md`, then make three actual revisions, each followed by fresh tests of affected chapters. The baseline does not count as a revision. Each tested chapter receives three fresh Terra Medium players, an independent Terra Medium grader for each record and a fresh Terra Medium chapter synthesizer (`gpt-6.1-terra`, effort `medium`). Independent assignments use only their defined public context. The [evaluation protocol](../../docs/fun-and-evaluation.md) owns the rubrics and retention rules.

Freeze the following additional guidance throughout this detailed phase:

> This text-only story is for grades 8–10 students with basic school biology. It explores relationships between cellular work, tissues and body systems. Specialist anatomy and biochemical pathways may not be assumed. Picture descriptions stand for planned medical-textbook-style illustrations; finished images do not exist and visual recognition is untested. Assess clarity and your experience from the supplied item titles, picture descriptions, goals and brief prose. Assess what the constructions and results contribute and your honest interest in continuing. Normal inspection and compatibility information are part of ordinary play. AI performance is not evidence of student comprehension.

Detailed mode exposes inspect/combine commands and information seeking. Its denominator includes inspections and unsuccessful well-formed attempts, but excludes protocol corrections, looks, automatic follow-ups and final reports. **Fun is meaningful decisions / total decisions**, the project's operational measure, not measured human enjoyment. Worthwhile can also credit an evidenced valued construction or story result. Hook remains a categorical assessment of reported interest. Do not average the three criteria into a single score or compare detailed Fun directly with fast-mode diagnostics.

The current artwork exists only as `{...}` descriptions of planned diagrams. Chapter checkpoints provide actual preceding public history, not experience of performing those chapters. Neither these adult-AI records nor a high score establishes student learning, visual recognition, cumulative attachment or pacing across 200 combinations. No graphics, screenshots, browser tests, engine changes or publication are part of this work.

### Pattern map recorded before the detailed baseline results

A fresh mapper received the current manuscript, active brief and standing rules, excluding earlier drafts, reports and judgments. Its neutral distinction separates **mechanical form** from **explanatory opportunity**. This prevents treating every new name or every transformation as the same experience.

| Mechanical form | Definition | Examples and cautions |
|---|---|---|
| Merge | Both explicit inputs are consumed to form a result. | Water + salts, membrane fats + transport proteins, motor fibers + support cells. The same form can mix materials, construct an interface or depict cooperation. |
| Transform | One explicit input persists through a new identity while the other is consumed. | Add oxygen to fibers, wrap fibers, connect lungs to circulation, apply a scrape to the body. A transform can install something, supply work, connect systems or change a condition. |
| Fit | Install a part in an existing compound, preserving the compound. | Nuclei/ribosomes/mitochondria; opposing muscles; heart muscle/valves. Count each actual installed slot once, not every hypothetical order permutation. |

Secondary opportunities: material/structure; interface/route; capacity/process; regulation/information; disturbance/repair. Tags can overlap and describe what a construction *could* reveal, not evidence it did. For interpretation also examine **preparing → fitting**, **adding capability → applying it**, **parallel branches → joining them**, repeated similar additions, and a completed smaller unit used at a larger scale. These sequences are not interchangeable with the three mechanical forms.

The baseline map is auditable by result:

| Chapter | Merge results | Transform results | Fittings |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Cells | Selective cell membrane; Cell fluid; Muscle-cell model; Muscle nuclei | Muscle fibers | Nuclei; ribosomes; mitochondria |
| 2. Force | Bending muscles; Straightening muscles | Sugar-processing fibers; ATP-supported fibers; Muscle bundles | — |
| 3. Framework | Arm bones | Cushioned bone ends; Lubricated elbow; Arm framework | Bending muscles; straightening muscles |
| 4. Signals | Motor nerves; Visual information route | Seeing control system; Nerve-linked arm; Guided arm; Arm with position feedback | — |
| 5. Circuit | Red blood cells; Oxygen-carrying blood; Connected vessel circuit | Beating heart; Blood-filled circuit; Circulation | Heart muscle; heart valves |
| 6. Air | Open lung spaces; Lung exchange surface | Exchange lungs; Lungs in pleural sacs; Breathing lungs; Oxygen-delivering circulation | — |
| 7. Food | — | Muscular digestive tract; Mixing and moving gut; Chemically digesting gut; Absorbing gut; Gut-to-blood supply; Body supply network | — |
| 8. Skin | Supporting skin layer; Connected surface layer | Surface barrier; Layered skin; Sensitive skin; Supplied arm; Living body model; Body holding a glass | — |
| 9. Seal | — | Small scrape; Platelet plug; Temporary seal; Ready to rebuild, all retaining the body | — |
| 10. Renewal | Directed protein builders; Spreading surface cells | New collagen chains; New supporting fibers; Rebuilding body; Renewed barrier | — |

Record-level contrasts chosen before outcomes: selective membrane versus cell-fluid mixture; heart muscle versus valves versus opposing arm pulls; supplying oxygen versus connective wrapping; position feedback versus skin sensation; temporary clot versus restored surface. Compare actual reason, expectation, available information, result and reaction. Similar mechanics may differ in content, order or diagram clarity; these samples cannot isolate a universal causal effect of a pattern.

For observed records, distinguish direct combinations, inspection-triggered combinations, information-only inspections and unsuccessful attempts. Attribute a resolved operation to its actual recipe; do not assign a failed hypothesis the pattern of a later successful result. Preserve each grader's classification. A strong final result does not automatically credit its prerequisites, and low Fun does not automatically make a result worthless.

### Detailed baseline

The [new detailed baseline](playtests/2026.11.11_22.32.57/report.md) starts from the final fast manuscript, SHA-256 `6760c9d6792cefe698d5fc1abcab9a2a27929142fb461e1272d14d342c0cf4e0`: ten chapters, 63 combinations. It is a new interaction condition. The earlier fast verdicts are context for the development history, not detailed-mode scores or a substitute for these records.


#### Baseline findings

All thirty chapter assignments completed, with 198 actual decisions (189 successful construction operations and nine unsuccessful decisions). Independent graders credited 43 meaningful decisions. Chapter reviewers credited 160 worthwhile decisions. These pooled counts describe the records, not a separate whole-story or human score.

| Chapter | Fun | Worthwhile | Hook |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| 1. Cells | 3/8 | 5.7/8 | High |
| 2. Force | 1/5 | 4.3/5 | High |
| 3. Framework | 0.7/6 | 5.3/6 | Medium/high |
| 4. Signals | 1.7/6.3 | 6.3/6.3 | Medium/high |
| 5. Circuit | 1.7/8.7 | 5.3/8.7 | Medium/high |
| 6. Air | 3.3/7 | 6/7 | High |
| 7. Food | 0/6 | 6/6 | Medium/high |
| 8. Skin | 1.3/9 | 8/9 | Medium |
| 9. Seal | 1/4 | 3/4 | High |
| 10. Renewal | 0.7/6 | 3.3/6 | Medium/high |

The baseline separates predictable-but-valued work from ordering friction. All three digestion records have zero credited meaningful decisions but six worthwhile results. Fittings include only 2 meaningful decisions among 21 observed operations, yet 13 receive worthwhile credit. These observations do not make every fitting weak or every transform strong: the fitted part's distinct contribution and the result matter.

Ten unsuccessful *requests* were automatically redirected into successful constructions; these are included in the 189 operations, not in the nine unsuccessful decisions. In the lung chapter, all three players tried adding the lining directly to the lung spaces and encountered a required ordering with no clear anatomical reason. All three signal players tried connecting motor nerves to the control system first; the first-operand rule made the feedback differ. All three skin players tried attaching skin before the body supply network. Two circulation players tried attaching the heart before filling the vessels. Those are reasonable assembly hypotheses, not evidence that players need an arbitrary order puzzle.

#### Revision one — permit sensible assembly orders

The first revision targets chapters **1, 4, 6 and 8**, with both weak-passage evidence and a cross-chapter pattern hypothesis: when two components contribute independently, their required preparation order should not imply a false physiological dependency. The falsifier is continued confusion, gratuitous extra setup, or loss of the represented contributions despite the additional routes.

- A lipid boundary can enclose cell fluid before receiving transport proteins, or the selective membrane can be prepared first. Both routes produce the same model; neither creates a functioning living cell from isolated materials.
- Motor nerves can connect to the control system or the arm first, then join the other end.
- The lung lining can join the air sacs or open air spaces before the capillary mesh, or the interface can be prepared first. Every completed lung still includes all the same contributions.
- Skin can join the arm before or after the continuing body supply network. The living-body goal still requires both.

The revision has **73 authored combinations but a 63-action completion route**. The ten added rules are alternative orders, not ten extra required pieces of anatomy or inflated progress toward a 200-action game. Three intentional compound-transition warnings remain; lint has zero errors. This changes story recipes only, using the existing engine.

A fresh informed reviewer saw only the candidate, active brief and standing guidance, excluding earlier versions and outcomes. It found no factual blocker in the alternative routes. Adopted picture refinements keep nerve endings visibly unattached, leave the sac's magnified lining patch unfinished, mark standalone exchange arrows “When supplied,” connect the lung bed visibly to pulmonary vessels, and retain the supply network in the arm's picture. The brief wonder about a selective boundary moves to the shared completed cell model so that either route receives it.


The [round-one retest](playtests/2026.11.11_22.47.57/report.md) covers chapters 1, 4, 6 and 8 with twelve new players and independent graders. All twelve completed, in 84 decisions; the tested chapters had 91 decisions across their baseline assignments. These samples use the same detailed guidance.

| Chapter | Before Fun | After Fun | Before worthwhile | After worthwhile | After hook |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 1. Cells | 3/8 | 3/8 | 5.7/8 | 6.7/8 | High |
| 4. Signals | 1.7/6.3 | 1.3/6 | 6.3/6.3 | 4/6 | Medium/high |
| 6. Air | 3.3/7 | 1.7/6 | 6/7 | 5/6 | Medium/high |
| 8. Skin | 1.3/9 | 0.7/8 | 8/9 | 7/8 | Medium |

The results are mixed. The nerve-to-control connection and skin-before-supply routes no longer fail. There are no unsuccessful decisions in the retest, but seven requests still redirect automatically into another construction. Every lung player tries adding the capillary mesh before the airway and encounters another unexplained order restriction. A skin player tries joining surface and support before adding the protective outer layer, exposing the same pattern at a different stage. The additional paths therefore fix particular cases without resolving the general design problem.

The lower Fun counts are not silently treated as failures: some previously credited hypotheses were attempts to overcome ordering friction. Conversely, a smoother route does not automatically establish a more compelling passage—the signal chapter's worthwhile count also fell. Slots and sequential connections remain predictable; the final sliding-filament and grip results still carry clear value. Retain the observations and test a different construction form rather than adding every possible permutation by hand.

#### Revision two — complete a compound in independent orders

The second revision targets chapters **5, 6 and 8**. It tests whether independent fittings represent partially completed anatomy more honestly and simply than chains of increasingly qualified result names. The falsifier is a larger checklist that removes order friction while adding no intelligible contribution or losing the valued process explanations.

- The vessel circuit accepts the beating heart and blood in either order. Flow appears only once both are fitted; the completed compound becomes the circulation input to the lung connection.
- The air-sac model accepts its airway, air-facing lining, blood interface, pleural connection and moving chest independently. Conditional drawings reveal exchange once both interfaces are present, and ventilation once airway and chest coupling are present. A completed compound combines with the circulation in one substantive system connection.
- A connected skin sheet accepts deeper support, outer protection and sensory endings independently. Each fitting has its own anatomical inset; a fully qualified patch supplies the common living-body result.

These changes preserve the distinct material and functional inputs rather than substituting an empty completion token. Keeping a complete compound usable as a whole also tests the user's requested transition from parts to a single larger ingredient. The revised air chapter no longer needs the round-one permutation chains. The ordinary known-slot guidance remains visible; this is a test of coherent making and results, not a proposal to hide compatibility information for a higher Fun score.


A fresh reviewer checked only the compound candidate, active brief and standing rules. Adopted fixes require the thin lining before the ventilation inset can appear, and keep airway, chest/pleural coupling and one-way heart flow visible in the final system diagram. Lint passes with zero errors and six intentional compound-transition warnings. The candidate has 69 authored combinations and a 63-action completion route.

The [round-two retest](playtests/2026.11.11_23.01.28/report.md) has nine fresh completed players and independent grades. All 66 decisions directly perform successful constructions: there are no rejected requests or compatibility redirects. That resolves the tested ordering friction, but does not by itself establish a stronger game.

| Chapter | Before Fun | After Fun | Before worthwhile | After worthwhile | After hook |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 5. Circuit | 1.7/8.7 | 1.7/8 | 5.3/8.7 | 4/8 | High |
| 6. Air | 1.7/6 | 0/6 | 5/6 | 5/6 | Medium/high |
| 8. Skin | 0.7/8 | 0.7/8 | 7/8 | 2.7/8 | Medium |

Before: detailed baseline for circuit, round one for air and skin. After: round two. The chapter histories also differ, so these tiny fresh samples cannot isolate a causal effect of slot presentation.

**Keep the vessel and lung compounds provisionally; reject the skin conversion.** The vessel route retains its specific cellular/exchange interest and all three graders report high hook, without the rejected heart-before-blood connection. The lung compound removes order friction while retaining 5/6 worthwhile actions and the same hook distribution; all decisions are now routine under the rubric. These are clear guided constructions, not an improvement in measured Fun. The skin conversion instead loses much credited value without improving Fun or hook. Its three role-labelled fittings read as a checklist, with the outer-layer discovery and glass feedback carrying most of the value. Do not keep every compound conversion merely because it is mechanically tidy.

#### Revision three — progressive structures and a persistent producer

The final round tests chapters **8 and 10**. Skin returns to the progressive representations from round one, with an additional legitimate order: living surface and supporting layers can join before the outer covering. It keeps the accepted skin-before-supply alternative. This is a focused restoration and correction, not a claim to support every physically imaginable assembly order.

Repair tests a different combination pattern: **instructions + material, in a working producer → product**. A fixed resident skin repair cell supplies the context for copied collagen instructions and amino acids to make collagen chains. Processing and export occur within the same cell; new supporting fibers then contribute to the existing body's repair. This removes the separate “Directed protein builders” intermediate and keeps the cell visible as a producer instead of consuming its machinery into its product. The context is a magnified resident cell, not a treatment station, transplantation or new game engine mechanic.

The hypothesis is that applying an earlier cell-building principle in this new context can earn a substantive inference and a more coherent production payoff. Its falsifier is a routine station click, unexplained context requirement, or faster completion without retained value. A higher ratio caused solely by removing a routine denominator action would not prove a more enjoyable passage. The skin hypothesis is recovery of the progressive layer relationships without the observed outer-before-support rejection.

A fresh chapter-ten reviewer saw only the candidate and active requirements. It found no scientific or causal blocker. Adopted drawing refinements retain the same recognizable cell, intact Golgi and RNA template, locate the processing machinery within that cell, label the collagen chain and carry local repair-signal dots into the surface-cell response. These distinguish reusable instructions and producer machinery from incorporated material without adding explanatory paragraphs.

A separate fresh skin reviewer found no blocker. Its adopted refinement marks the nutrient arrows in the living layers “Diffusion when supplied,” so assembling the layers does not imply an already active circulation. Lint passes with zero errors and five intentional compound-transition warnings. The candidate has 70 authored rules and a 62-action completion route: eight rules provide alternative orders, while the repair revision removes one required preparation action.

The [round-three retest](playtests/2026.11.11_23.10.58/report.md) completes all six fresh chapter assignments in 39 decisions.

| Chapter | Before Fun | After Fun | Before worthwhile | After worthwhile | After hook |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| 8. Skin | 0.7/8 | 0/8 | 2.7/8 | 7/8 | Medium |
| 10. Renewal | 0.7/6 | 2.3/5 | 3.3/6 | 4.7/5 | Medium/high |

Before: round two for skin, detailed baseline for renewal. Skin recovers round one's 7/8 worthwhile count, but neither Fun nor hook improves. The restored layer pictures and glass feedback are valued; attaching completed skin to the arm remains routine and uncredited for all three. One player takes the new support-before-outer-cover route successfully. That route misses the dead-outer-cell wonder attached to the other intermediate, and the player finds the covering less informative. This is a remaining route-specific presentation weakness, not a reason to restore the rejected ordering constraint.

Renewal's change is more than a smaller denominator: meaningful decisions rise from two across the three baseline players to seven across the three revised players, and worthwhile decisions from ten to fourteen. All three revised players infer that the resident cell uses copied instructions and amino acids to produce collagen. However, all three then try collagen chains directly with processing membranes. Compatibility feedback supplies the cell hotspot and performs the operation; the graders credit the **attempted processing inference**, not intentional selection of that corrected partner. Two players also try edge cells on the body before combining them with repair signals. All five rejected requests redirect successfully, so there are no unsuccessful decisions but there is interface friction. The final hook distribution remains two medium and one high, unchanged from baseline. Keep the producer experiment and its visible cell continuity, with the required context still needing clearer communication.

### Patterns supported—and limits exposed—by the records

A fresh final analyst compared the four sets of public decision records and attributed reports, without this study's conclusions or instructions to prefer a variant. It preserved the existing grades. The following comparisons join that independent reading with the chapter syntheses; they are working design findings, not universal rankings of recipe forms.

| Pattern | Concrete evidence and contrary cases | Consequence for this draft and expansion |
|---|---|---|
| **Prepare a part, then install it** | In baseline chapter 1, all three players receive meaningful credit for DNA + nuclear envelopes at step 4, then none for installing those nuclei at step 5. Ribosome fitting at step 6 is meaningful for two players, however. In chapter 2, the second tendon repeats a known construction, while the eventual opposing pulls still explain movement. | Inspect sequences for a second action that merely executes an already settled plan. Keep a separate fitting when its geometry or function adds something; do not assume every slot is redundant. |
| **Fill independent roles in a compound** | Round two removes all rejected requests in the tested circuit, lung and skin chapters. Lung worthwhile stays 5/6 while Fun becomes 0/6; skin worthwhile falls 7/8 → 2.7/8. Restoring progressive skin gives 7/8 again, with Fun 0/8. | Keep vessel and lung compounds provisionally for coherent assembly order and whole-unit reuse. Reject the skin checklist. Compound mechanics alone do not guarantee either interest or a worthwhile result. |
| **Make a capability produce a visible consequence** | Baseline chapter 7 has Fun 0/6 but worthwhile 6/6: adding food makes the assembled gut's movement visible, and absorption differs from transport. In round-three skin, all three value the slipping-and-steadying glass result; none receives meaningful credit for choosing it. Final skin attachment itself is uncredited for all three. | Applications can justify predictable construction without retroactively justifying every prerequisite. Seek distinct consequences of work already done, rather than another name for the completed assembly. |
| **Connect different scales through a persistent producer** | Baseline renewal's instructions-to-builders preparation is uncredited for all three. Revised instructions + material in a resident cell is meaningful and worthwhile for all three. Export connects internal machinery to external tissue support in both versions. The revised export step also exposes the hotspot misunderstanding described above. | Retain this experiment. A smaller whole can do work in the next scale, as well as become an ingredient. Clarify the operating context before interpreting higher Fun as a better interaction; the corrected operation was not what players chose. |
| **Distinguish successive bodily conditions** | Baseline chapter 9's platelet plug limits loss while leaving missing skin visible; the repair chapter's final surface closure still shows deeper remodeling. These results are valued even when the next pairing is routine. The more ordinary fiber-to-body join remains weaker. | Injury can reveal why previously built structures matter. Each state must show a different remaining need, rather than simply relabelling the body as progressively more repaired. |
| **Allow plausible orders without inventing false dependencies** | All three baseline signal players try nerves-to-control before arm; all three lung players try lining on air spaces. Revisions accept these plans. Round one then exposes another lung order restriction, which the compound resolves. Some rejected hypotheses had received meaningful credit, so removing friction can lower Fun. | Judge the biological and assembly logic independently of the score. Do not preserve arbitrary rejection to manufacture meaningful decisions. Supporting one alternative does not imply supporting every permutation. |

Different orders can also move explanatory value between operations. Adding blood last to the revised vessel circuit supplies both the transport-medium inset and the whole-flow result at once; worthwhile counts credit that decision once. In skin, a branch can bypass the intermediate carrying a wonder. Review **when a relationship becomes visible on each route**, not just whether the final compound contains the right parts.

The broad mechanical labels are too coarse to decide which combinations work. A transform may merely add the next labelled component, demonstrate an interface, test an ordinary action or depict repair. A fitting can be an obvious checklist entry yet show an indispensable distinction. The useful comparison is the player's prior plan, the new relationship made visible, and the consequence they actually value.

### Judgment after the first three rounds — superseded

Retain the revised ten-chapter manuscript as an exploratory construction game. The resident-cell production passage and restored progressive skin are the strongest final changes; the lung and vessel compounds are retained for clearer assembly and reuse, **not** as demonstrated Fun improvements. The final draft still has weak preparation/attachment sequences, a medium-hook skin passage and ending, and a repair-context ambiguity. Higher worthwhile counts do not establish stronger desire to continue.

There is ample biological material for 200 combinations. These trials suggest an editorial test for expanding it: a new action should expose a distinct contribution, connect previously separate constructions, or make a capability produce an observable consequence. Repeating labelled assembly for every organ would not earn its length merely by being accurate. Cellular production, transport across different boundaries, feedback, disturbance and rebuilding provide different kinds of relationship to develop; these records do not yet establish their pacing across a long game.

Keep concise wonders, purposeful goals and the picture plans. The final manuscript has **26 wonder passages / 384 words**, none longer than 20 words, and **133 planned-picture passages / 2,496 words**. Those descriptions are verbal proxies, not finished illustrations or a measured reduction in the harness's reading load. No conclusion here establishes student comprehension or human enjoyment.

### Verification and retention

The new detailed baseline and three actual revision/retest rounds produced **57 completed chapter trials, 57 independent grades, 19 fresh chapter syntheses and 387 decision rows**. Coverage is versioned: baseline all ten chapters; round one 1/4/6/8; round two 5/6/8; round three 8/10. Other chapters retain their earlier local evidence, with subsequent checkpoint history changes; there is no invented all-chapter score for the final version.

All retained response arrays exactly match accepted session replies and final reports, including harness-owned look flags. All sessions completed and replay-verified. Source/runtime hashes and guidance were uniform within each campaign. Original independent classifications and reasons are preserved; worthwhile either/or flags, action counts and arithmetic means were checked against all 387 rows. Every marked chapter synthesis was copied verbatim into its mechanically assembled report. No replacement grading attempts were required.

Final manuscript SHA-256: `d76b8feed7d915377de79aefc068eea38f4d1934274b739812aa2c90a4d0df11`, matching the round-three frozen manuscript. Lint has **zero errors** and five reviewed intentional mixed-fate warnings: cells, arm, heart, vessel circuit and lung spaces retain their identity while receiving parts, then contribute to a larger construction. Analysis finds ten chapters; 70 authored recipes, 62 required actions; 115 item identities, 63 primitive; average chapter-start inventory 7.3, maximum 9. There were no engine, UI, graphics, screenshot, browser or publication changes.

The standing [evaluation protocol](../../docs/fun-and-evaluation.md) owns the reproducible instructions. Retained chapter reports record exact guidance, actual model/effort, source hash, mode and evidence limits; [working notes](#prototype-11-november-2026--three-detailed-revision-rounds) contain aligned score tables. Requested response arrays and attributed reports remain in the four linked campaign directories. Disposable sessions, snapshots, adapters, prompts, logs and review scratch are removed after verification. The licensed local source library remains available for the ongoing project.

<a id="detailed-five-further-detailed-rounds"></a>

### Five further detailed rounds

The user challenged the adoption decisions above: Fun mostly declined, and worthwhile mostly declined too. Chapter 8's final increase recovered from the failed compound conversion; it did not exceed its original baseline. Chapter 1 had a small worthwhile gain with unchanged Fun. Chapter 10 was the only clear increase in meaningful-decision counts, with some credit arising from the failed export attempt. The earlier claim that the final draft should be retained on the strength of its design rationale was not supported by the overall results.

The next five rounds cover chapters **1, 4, 5, 6, 8 and 10**, with eighteen fresh players, eighteen independent graders and six chapter reviewers per round. Actual Terra Medium selection, detailed mode, canonical rubrics and the exact additional guidance above remain fixed. Starting source: `d76b8feed7d915377de79aefc068eea38f4d1934274b739812aa2c90a4d0df11`. The latest scores for that starting version are chapter 1 and 4 from the first retest, chapter 5 and 6 from the second, and chapter 8 and 10 from the third. Earlier baseline scores remain explicit reference points; changing chapter history prevents treating these as paired controlled trials.

#### Adoption rule and evidence discipline

For every chapter, compare the candidate with its retained predecessor **and** the original detailed baseline. Show Fun and worthwhile counts and denominators separately. A smaller denominator alone is not evidence of additional worthwhile decisions. Assess whether credited decisions concern a real relationship, or an unclear interface/arbitrary order; preserve the independent grades either way. Record hook as a guard against making an exercise that earns counts but loses interest.

Keep a candidate provisionally when its records support the proposed mechanism and it improves at least one primary measure without reducing the other. If counts and proportions point in different directions, record the tradeoff rather than declaring a win. A large or repeated regression is rejected; a small ambiguous change may receive a specific discriminating retest but is not promoted as an improvement. These three-player samples are development evidence, not statistical proof. Retain reversible chapter snapshots in scratch during the five rounds so rejected experiments do not become the next default merely by being newest.

#### Round 1 hypotheses — functional contributions rather than repeated fittings

Two diagnostic assignments separately inspected actual earlier records for chapters 1/4/5 and 6/8/10. They saw the current manuscript and attributed evidence, not future judgments. Each then authored candidate sections as a design collaborator; they were not reused as independent reviewers. A fresh informed reviewer receives only the integrated candidate and standing requirements before testing.

| Chapter | Observation and hypothesis | First test and falsifier |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Nucleus preparation was credited but installation usually merely executed that plan; organelle fittings were repeatedly called predictable. The final filament overlap was valued. An RNA–ribosome handoff may provide a more useful relationship than making and installing the same nucleus. | Supply nuclei, combine copied RNA with ribosomes, then fit the machinery; keep membrane alternatives, ATP and shortening. Eight required actions remain. Reject if the new preparation/install pair simply relocates the repetition or loses earlier value. |
| 4 | Myelinating support cells and eyes-closed position feedback were valued; the intervening route joins were predictable. Applying control to a visible target may distinguish information about a destination from information about the arm. | Supply the seeing system, build outgoing nerves, use a visible target in the reach, then add position feedback. Five required actions replace six. Reject a ratio-only gain, an artificial demand to reach before adding sensors, or loss of worthwhile signals. |
| 5 | Heart slots and the pump-to-circuit join were routine; red-cell differences and tissue exchange were valued. Showing what a squeeze fails to accomplish before adding valves may make direction a problem to solve. | Replace two heart slots with chamber → squeeze → one-way-flow states, then add the intrinsic rhythm. Keep blood/exchange and free-order circuit fittings. Eight required actions remain. Reject if it is only renamed assembly or if the mechanical comparison implies impossible physiology. |
| 6 | Five labelled slots yielded zero meaningful decisions in the latest test, though exchange and ventilation retained value. Separately constructing an airway, exchange interface and chest coupling may require connecting different functions. | Build those three branches independently, fit the exchange surface, connect chest motion, then circulation: six required actions. Reject repeated preparation/attachment or renewed unexplained order failures. |
| 8 | The skin-to-arm join was uncredited for all three; the slipping-and-steadying glass picture was valued but automatically completed. Acting on the slip might make feedback a relationship the player uses. | Preserve the progressive skin; lifting the glass now precedes a changing-contact → steadied-grip combination. Nine actions replace eight. Reject if the extra event tile is a magic completion key, merely repeats the goal or dilutes the existing payoff. |
| 10 | All three players chose collagen chains + processing membranes; feedback supplied the required cell hotspot. That error contributed meaningful credit. | Allow that direct processing pair while retaining the initial resident-cell producer and identical cell-continuity pictures. Five actions remain. Test whether insight and value survive without the unclear operating context; do not interpret a lower friction-derived score as success on both objectives. |

##### Round 1 observations and decisions

[Report](playtests/2026.11.11_23.37.50/report.md): all eighteen players completed; 123 decisions, 23 independently meaningful and 75 worthwhile. Eight requests were redirected. The first three chapter syntheses required technical recovery after the formatter rejected responses; their players and independent grades were preserved. Recovery supplies new chapter reviews, not replacement playthroughs. The organizer normalizes category-label formatting, removes duplicate mode headers and derives displayed arithmetic from validated action flags without changing judgments. The retained reports and all rows were verified.

| Chapter | Starting Fun → candidate | Starting worthwhile → candidate | Decision and observed mechanism |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 3/8 → 2.3/8 | 6.7/8 → 5/8 | **Reject.** RNA preparation then installation relocated the same repetition; two players explicitly describe this. Keep the earlier nucleus construction as the next reference. |
| 4 | 1.3/6 → 0/5 | 4/6 → 2.7/5 | **Reject.** The visible target supplied another obvious pairing. All three give routine accounts; the eyes-closed contrast remains the valued result. |
| 5 | 1.7/8 → 3/8 | 4/8 → 6/8 | **Keep provisionally; confirm.** All three use the two-way-flow picture to infer the need for valves and name that construction most worthwhile. This exceeds the original baseline as well as the starting version on both measures. |
| 6 | 0/6 → 1/6 | 5/6 → 5.7/6 | **Change; not an unqualified win.** All three meaningful decisions are motivated pleural-connection attempts that encounter the arbitrary chest-first rule. Both lining and pleural ordering restrictions recur. Test the same relationships with those attempts accepted. |
| 8 | 0/8 → 0/9 | 7/8 → 2/9 | **Reject.** The contact event is an unusual tile whose stated goal gives away its use. The extra action adds no meaningful decisions; return to the progressive skin/grip passage for a different hypothesis. |
| 10 | 2.3/5 → 1.3/5 | 4.7/5 → 3.7/5 | **Reject as an improvement.** Removing the hotspot removes some inference credit and does not preserve the worthwhile count. Do not simply reinstate confusing interaction to recover that credit; test a different substantive relationship next. |

Hook: high for cells; medium for signals, skin and repair; two high/one medium for circulation and breathing. Worthwhile attribution also varies among fresh reviewers. In particular, most of skin's earlier actions are unchanged, so the large aggregate fall does not isolate the effect of the extra slip tile. The unanimous lack of new meaningful decisions and the player's event-tile criticism independently fail its hypothesis.

#### Round 2 hypotheses — distinguish a missing function and test the promising comparison

Round one supports one concrete pattern: the valve acts on a visible consequence of the player's preceding construction. It does not support adding a target or splitting a result into an event tile. Two fresh design collaborators inspected those actual records and proposed the following tests. A separate fresh informed reviewer saw only the candidate sections and brief, found no blocker, and proposed two adopted refinements: position sensors **contribute** information, and surface-closed skin still shows sparse early collagen so overlapping repair is visible.

| Chapter | Test derived from the preceding observations | Required actions and falsifier |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Restore the earlier nuclei and direct ribosome fittings. Supply cell fluid; show a boundary that excludes charged particles before transport proteins provide selective routes. Both preparation orders remain legal. | Seven instead of eight. Require evidence about the boundary's missing exchange function; reject merely improved density from dropping the fluid preparation or a loss of the valued cell relationships. |
| 4 | Remove the failed target episode. Begin with the familiar question of sensing the arm with eyes closed; show an outgoing-only diagram before adding the sensory return. | Four instead of six in the retained reference. Compare absolute credited counts as well as fractions; reject a shorter sequence of the same obvious matches. |
