| 5 | Repeat the unchanged squeeze → valve → rhythm chapter with fresh players while other chapters change. | Eight. This is confirmation of round one's promising local change, not another claimed revision or isolated causal replication. Reject a claim of stable improvement if the new records do not support it. |
| 6 | Accept lining directly on the airway/sac model and pleura directly on the lung before chest coupling; preserve the three functional relationships and pressure explanation. | Six. Test whether the physiological inference survives without the two observed order rejections; retain any loss in the scores rather than redefining it as success. |
| 8 | Restore the eight-action skin/grip passage. The exposed living sheet now visibly loses water; adding the lipid-rich outer layer reduces that loss, with fewer rather than zero outward arrows. | Eight. Test whether the visible problem makes barrier construction meaningful, while preserving dead/living layers, supply and grip. Reject another obvious matching operation or a displaced payoff. |
| 10 | Keep the direct export interaction as an experimental base, then allow surface closure and deeper support in either order. Intermediate pictures show different remaining gaps; both processes are needed for the final combined state. | Five. Test whether choosing between two real, overlapping repairs produces insight/value beyond the failed direct-export-only trial. Adoption must also compare with the pre-experiment 2.3/5 Fun and 4.7/5 worthwhile, not just recover the regression. |

##### Round 2 observations and decisions

[Report](playtests/2026.11.11_23.57.34/report.md): eighteen completed players, 116 decisions, 25 meaningful and 90 worthwhile. One player had a transport interruption after its first accepted decision. The same frozen assignment resumed at the same packet; original turns and the recorded error were preserved, cumulative timing retained, and replay verified before and after. No playthrough or accepted judgment was replaced. Its missing independent grade and synthesis were then completed; all other completed syntheses were preserved.

| Chapter | Round 1 Fun → round 2 | Round 1 worthwhile → round 2 | Decision against the retained reference |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2.3/8 → 2/7 | 5/8 → 6.3/7 | **Tradeoff, not adopt as a win.** Worthwhile density recovers, but meaningful counts and density remain below the starting 3/8; worthwhile count remains below 6.7/8. The organelle fittings remain repetitive. |
| 4 | 0/5 → 0/4 | 2.7/5 → 3.7/4 | **Reject as a Fun improvement.** The question gives the result value, but every player still executes predictable connections. Removing two actions from the retained six-action version has not supplied an interesting decision. |
| 5 | 3/8 → 2.7/8 | 6/8 → 5.7/8 | **Keep.** The unchanged chapter again exceeds the starting 1.7/8 and 4/8, and the original baseline; all three now have high hook. Small score movement is not another implementation gain. |
| 6 | 1/6 → 0.7/6.7 | 5.7/6 → 3.7/6.7 | **Reject this variant.** Correcting two orders exposes another: two players connect circulation before ventilation, reasonably. Those failed attempts receive the only meaningful credit. The new lining-first branch also bypasses the valued gas-exchange reading. |
| 8 | 0/9 → 1.3/8 | 2/9 → 6/8 | **Partial support; continue experimentally.** All three infer the lipid-rich outer layer from the observed water loss. This is new meaningful evidence, but worthwhile remains below the pre-experiment 7/8; both later joins are uncredited for all three. Recovery from round one's failure is not the target. |
| 10 | 1.3/5 → 1.7/5 | 3.7/5 → 4.7/5 | **No demonstrated repair-order effect.** All three still work on deeper support first, so the alternative-order hypothesis is untested in these records. Worthwhile returns to the starting count; Fun remains below 2.3/5. Hook is now two high/one medium. |

The action-level evidence sharpens the transfer hypothesis. The valve and outer-layer decisions respond to **a specific limitation visible in the preceding construction**. A general interesting question, named target or preparation-state label has not produced the same response. In lung assembly, the credited hypotheses continue to encounter artificial restrictions; this is not evidence to preserve those restrictions.

These remain explicit comprehension hypotheses, not positive scores.

| 5 | Keep the confirmed heart chapter unchanged. | Another fresh check, with changed preceding history explicitly acknowledged. |
| 6 | Permit circulation before or after ventilation. Flowing blood with unrenewed sac air shows a diminishing oxygen difference; ventilated lungs without circulation cannot carry oxygen away. Every completed exchange branch receives the separation diagram and short wonder. Six required actions. | Test whether identifying the remaining function replaces order-error credit with substantive choice and retains value on different routes. Reject mere accumulation of intermediate labels or another missing route. |
| 8 | Keep the water-loss contrast. The supply join now shows finite local stores before connection and continuing delivery to muscle plus diffusion from dermal vessels afterward. Early layer diagrams present capacity/anatomy rather than already active delivery. Eight actions. | Test whether this gives a previously routine join a distinct cross-scale consequence without taking away earlier skin value. Both Fun and worthwhile must be compared with the starting passage. |
| 10 | Keep direct export and parallel repair, but identify the actual **Golgi and vesicles**, with a teaching inset locating cargo transport between rough ER and the cell boundary. Fun remains below the starting 3/8; worthwhile count is above 6.7/8 but density is almost unchanged. |

Hook: high for cells and signals; two high/one medium for circulation and lungs; medium for skin and repair. Pictures of everyday grip, separate air/blood spaces and repair after closure repeatedly earn value. A useful diagram can earn worthwhile credit without creating an interesting decision; neither score should stand in for the other.

Round three suggests separating the successful relationship from the surrounding repetitive connections. These are prospective tests, not claims that each candidate is better.

Two fresh design collaborators prepared the cell/control and skin/repair candidates. A fresh informed reviewer, without trial reports, found no blocker; an adopted label identifies the muscle-length poses as an example. All natural orders of the three control connections are accepted. The repair goals now begin with surface closure, followed by deeper strength; the former production-first goal was removed so this test actually presents the proposed functional contrast. Both repair orders remain legal.


Comparator audit while round four runs: the starting cell chapter's three players all attempted membrane fats + water; each motivated but rejected attempt received meaningful credit. Thus its 3/8 Fun reference also includes ordering friction, not only the round-three candidate. Retain those original judgments and denominators; compare successful biological inferences separately in interpretation rather than silently rescoring either condition.

A separate fresh informed reviewer read the candidate without prior results and found no blocker. The installed-nucleus RNA-copy route explicitly supports either production/installation order. Its optional skin-perfusion clarification was adopted: the intermediate picture labels the supply connection as not yet shown and depicts finite stores beside both muscle and living skin cells. This is an explanatory omission, not a proposal that skin can live without circulation.

##### Round 5 observations, integration audit and retained ten-chapter reference

[Report](playtests/2026.11.12_00.46.05/report.md): eighteen completed players, 123 decisions, 30 meaningful and 89 worthwhile. All structured responses, replay results, independent judgments, worthwhile flags, arithmetic and assembled report sections verified.

| Chapter | Round 4 Fun → round 5 | Round 4 worthwhile → round 5 | Result against starting reference |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2.3/8 → 2/8 | 4/8 → 6.7/8 | Two players name RNA copying/production most worthwhile; the new content has value. But worthwhile only matches starting and Fun falls from 3/8. Copying decisions partly concern locating installed nuclei in the model, not discovering transcription. **Do not adopt as a two-measure improvement.** |
| 4 | 1.3/6 → 1.7/6 | 3.7/6 → 3.3/6 | All three infer the muscle-length/sensory relationship, but the intervening motor connections still add little. Fun exceeds starting 1.3/6 while worthwhile falls from 4/6. **Reject this tradeoff.** |
| 5 | 2.3/8 → 2/8 | 5.3/8 → 6/8 | **Retain the pump/valve construction.** All five tests exceed starting 1.7/8 Fun and 4/8 worthwhile. Observed Fun ranges 2–3/8 and worthwhile 5–6/8; latest hook is high. This repeated result is stronger evidence than one favorable sample. |
| 6 | 1/6 → 1.3/6 | 5/6 → 5.3/6 | **Retain the accepted-order exchange/ventilation construction provisionally.** Three clean tests have Fun 1–1.3/6 and worthwhile 5–5.3/6 versus starting 0/6 and 5/6. The worthwhile gain is small and not stable across all three samples. It does not recover the original baseline's absolute counts. |
| 8 | 1.3/8 → 1.7/8 | 6.7/8 → 4/8 | Sensory connection earns meaningful credit from all three; the supply join again earns no value. **Reject as aggregate improvement.** The intended restoration of the supply-focused picture was not actually integrated; see audit below. |
| 10 | 1/5 → 1.3/5 | 4.3/5 → 4.3/5 | All three now infer the cell-production operation, and value collagen's journey to tissue. The modest Fun rise does not beat starting 2.3/5; worthwhile remains below 4.7/5. **Do not adopt this revision as a win.** |

**Integration error:** post-run inspection found that chapter 8's round-five candidate retained the round-four final supply picture and wonder. The intended return to the round-three material-focused result was absent from the frozen manuscript; only the intermediate perfusion clarification changed. Therefore this is **not a completed test of the stated supply-focus hypothesis**. The actual playthroughs and scores remain valid evidence for their frozen source. They show that near-identical chapters can receive substantially different worthwhile attribution, not that restoring the older picture failed. Future candidate preflight must inspect the actual chapter diff against the recorded hypothesis, in addition to lint and source hashing. No result or grade has been replaced to conceal this error.

The retained ten-chapter reference keeps the circulation chapter tested in all five rounds and the final lung chapter. Chapters 1, 4, 8 and 10 return to their pre-experiment versions: the tested alternatives did not improve both objectives without a loss. This is a deliberate hybrid of previously tested sections, not a new whole-story trial. Its earlier-history context differs from the local chapter assignments. The restored cell membrane order and repair-cell export context retain their previously recorded interaction weaknesses; their higher raw Fun partly includes motivated unsuccessful attempts. They are unresolved authoring problems, not desirable sources of difficulty.

Across the five rounds: **90 completed chapter playthroughs, 90 fresh independent grades, 30 fresh chapter syntheses, 612 decisions, 133 meaningful and 435 worthwhile**. These pooled totals describe workload, not a combined score or a final-version estimate. Exact response arrays and attributed reports remain; scratch versions and sessions are disposable after verification. Terra Medium, detailed mode, illustration proxies, audience guidance and grading criteria stayed fixed.

##### What the five rounds actually teach

- **Most repeatable success: resolve a visible functional limitation.** Squeezing chambers move fluid both ways; valves make flow directional. All five circulation samples exceed the starting primary scores. This is a relationship to reuse, not an instruction to repeat the same three-step shape everywhere.
- **Useful physiological inputs can create a decision.** Muscle-length change and a sensory receptor, and a skin signal awaiting its return path, repeatedly earn meaningful credit. Their surrounding preparation-and-attachment chains still depress chapter value; local success does not justify adopting the entire passage.
- **A predictable action can have a strong result.** Filament overlap, separate air/blood spaces, grip correction and closed-but-weak repair repeatedly attract attention. None establishes strong Fun by itself.
- **Rejected hypotheses:** adding a visible target; turning slipping into another event tile; preparing an RNA/ribosome assembly before fitting it; replacing a function label with “Golgi”; compressing connections without replacing their value; and assuming a shorter introduction alone repairs the sequence. Several recovered a prior regression without surpassing the retained reference.
- **Clarity and scores must both be reported.** Accepting natural assembly orders removes friction, but some earlier meaningful credit came from the rejected attempts themselves. Do not preserve confusion to inflate Fun, or claim that clarity automatically improved it.
- **Sampling and attribution remain material limits.** The unchanged circulation chapter varies, and the near-unchanged skin chapter varies much more. Three AI players do not establish human enjoyment or student comprehension. Source hashes protect the evaluated version; they do not prove the intended edit was present or a hypothesized cause was isolated.

#### Next scope — full story, authorized 12 November 2026

The user judged that the subject has enough substance and requested a roughly 200-combination full story, with blind reviews, iterative fast testing for worthwhile/hook and subsequent iterative detailed testing for Fun/worthwhile/hook. They explicitly encouraged replacing stubbornly weak passages with fresh authors who receive only the scope and a very rough chapter purpose. The [full-story study](#full-story-development) owns that next experiment; this ledger remains the complete five-round record. The [single design brief](../../stories/anatomy/design-brief.md) continues to govern both references and the expansion.

Retained ten-chapter reference SHA-256: `6027ae16b373134bd7dba63327246f193c6a170454329f0b4467e81824157e5a`. Final lint: zero errors, four reviewed compound-transition warnings; 82 rules including generated fittings and alternative routes, 62 required actions. Chapters 2, 3, 7 and 9 were unchanged throughout the five rounds.
